Section 04: IAM & AWS CLI (9)

4·1U04-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q1

What is a proper definition of an IAM Role?

IAM Role-এর সঠিক definition কোনটি?

Correct: C. Why it is right

IAM Role holo ekta IAM identity, user er moto permission thake, kintu eta physical manush use kore na - AWS service (jemon EC2 instance, Lambda function) use kore tomar hoye AWS e action nite (PDF p. 37).

Common role: EC2 Instance Role, Lambda Function Role, CloudFormation role.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Eta user ar group er relation (ek user multiple group e thakte pare); role er definition na.
  • B. Password policy holo account setting (min length, expiry, reuse prevent); role er sathe somporko nai.
  • D. Eta user e attach kora permission/policy er bornona; role holo alada identity ja AWS service use kore.

Keyword: used by an AWS service + set of permissions → IAM Role

Instructor feedback (English)

A: IAM Users can belong to multiple User Groups, but it's not the definition of an IAM Role.

B: IAM Roles are not a password policy.

C: Some AWS services need to perform actions on your behalf. To do so, you assign permissions to AWS services with IAM Roles.

D: IAM Roles can not be assigned to IAM Users.

4·2U04-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q2

Which of the following is an IAM Security Tool?

নিচের কোনটি একটি IAM Security Tool?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Course e duita IAM Security Tool (PDF p. 38):

  • IAM Credentials Report (account-level): sob user er list ar tader credential (password, MFA, access key) er status.
  • IAM Access Advisor (user-level): user ke kon service er permission deya ache ar shesh kobe use koreche.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. IAM Root Account Manager bole kono tool nai.
  • C. IAM Services Report bole kichu nai; asol ta holo Credentials Report.
  • D. IAM Security Advisor nai; asol naam IAM Access Advisor - naam mil diye trap.

Keyword: IAM Security Tool → Credentials Report (ar Access Advisor)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: IAM Credentials report lists all your AWS Account's IAM Users and the status of their various credentials.

B: There's no such AWS service.

C: There's no such AWS service.

D: There's no such AWS service.

4·3U04-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q3

Which answer is INCORRECT regarding IAM Users?

IAM Users সম্পর্কে কোন উত্তরটি ভুল (INCORRECT)?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Question ta INCORRECT statement chay. IAM user nijer credential diye AWS e dhoke: console e username + password, CLI/SDK te nijer access keys (PDF p. 33). Root account er credential kokhono use/share kora jabe na (PDF p. 39).

Baki tin ta statement sotti, tai D-i answer.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Sotti statement: ek user multiple group e thakte pare (jemon Charles: Developers + Audit Team, PDF p. 25).
  • B. Sotti statement: user er group e thakar badhyobadhokota nai (Fred kono group e nai, PDF p. 25).
  • C. Sotti statement: policy sorasori user e attach kora jay, ba inline policy deya jay (PDF p. 27).

Keyword: INCORRECT → IAM user root credentials use kore na, nijer credential use kore

Instructor feedback (English)

D: IAM Users access AWS services using their own credentials (username & password or Access Keys).

4·4U04-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q4

Which of the following is an IAM best practice?

নিচের কোনটি একটি IAM best practice?

Correct: B. Why it is right

IAM best practice (PDF p. 39): root account use korbe na, shudhu account setup er jonno. Daily kaj er jonno nijer IAM user (admin) use koro.

Sathe: one physical user = one IAM user, credential share na, MFA on.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Ulta: one physical user = one AWS user. Ek manusher jonno onek user banale audit ar management kothin hoy.
  • C. Credential kokhono share korbe na; colleague er jonno alada IAM user banao.
  • D. Ulta: MFA use ar enforce kora best practice; password churi holeo account safe thake.

Keyword: IAM best practice → root user use na

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is not an IAM best practice, you should create one IAM User per physical person.

B: Use the root account only to create your first IAM User and a few account/service management tasks. For everyday tasks, use an IAM User.

C: This is not an IAM best practice, never share your AWS account credentials (password or Access Keys) with others.

D: This is not an IAM best practice, you should enable Multi-Factor Authentication (MFA) for all AWS accounts and IAM Users to ensure maximum security.

4·5U04-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q5

What are IAM Policies?

IAM Policies কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

IAM Policy holo JSON document ja permission define kore - kon action, kon resource e Allow/Deny (PDF p. 26, 28).

Policy attach kora jay users, groups ar roles e. Group e dile group er sob user inherit kore (PDF p. 27).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. IAM policy account-to-account interaction define kore na; eta kono identity ke ki permission ache ta bole.
  • C. Eta password policy (min length, character type, expiry) - IAM policy na, alada account setting.
  • D. IAM policy customer-AWS somporko bole na; eta permission er JSON document.

Keyword: JSON documents + set of permissions → IAM Policies

Instructor feedback (English)

C: This is a password policy, not an IAM Policy.

4·6U04-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q6

Which principle should you apply regarding IAM Permissions?

IAM Permissions-এর ক্ষেত্রে আপনার কোন principle apply করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS e least privilege principle: user er jotota dorkar tar beshi permission dibe na (PDF p. 26).

Access Advisor diye dekhte paro kon permission use hoy na, tarpor komiye dao.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Ulta: most privilege dile bhul ba hack hole damage onek boro hoy.
  • B. Chaile-i permission deya thik na; shudhu kaj er jonno ja lagbe tai dao.
  • D. IAM policy diye root account er permission komano jay na; ar eta kono permission principle o na - principle holo least privilege.

Keyword: principle for IAM Permissions → least privilege

Instructor feedback (English)

A: It's a bad practice to grant maximum privileges to all users.

B: You shouldn't grant permissions to your employees unless it's relevant to their work.

C: That's right! Don't give more permissions than the user needs.

D: You can not restrict root account permissions.

4·7U04-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q7

What should you do to increase your root account security?

আপনার root account-এর security বাড়াতে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: D. Why it is right

MFA = password (ja tumi jano) + security device (ja tomar kache ache) (PDF p. 30). Password churi/hack holeo device chara login hobe na, tai root account safe thake.

Course bole: root account ar IAM users duto-i MFA diye protect koro.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Root account e by default sob permission thake, eta remove kora jay na.
  • B. Shudhu CLI use korle root security bare na; CLI o access key diye kaj kore, ar root access key rakha-i uchit na.
  • C. Ulta: root dia daily kaj bad practice; IAM user banao ar root shudhu setup e use koro.

Keyword: increase root account security → Enable MFA

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You can not remove permissions from the root account, it has all permissions by default.

B: This won't help with your root account security.

C: This is a bad practice. You should access your AWS account using IAM Users.

D: When you enable MFA, this adds another layer of security. Even if your password is stolen, lost, or hacked your account is not compromised.

4·8U04-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q8

IAM User Groups can contain IAM Users and other User Groups.

IAM User Groups-এর মধ্যে IAM Users এবং অন্য User Groups থাকতে পারে।

Correct: B. Why it is right

False. Groups only contain users, not other groups (PDF p. 25). IAM e nested group nai.

Tobe ek user ekadhik group e thakte pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Group er bhitor group rakha jay na; group e shudhu IAM users thake.

Keyword: Users and other User Groups → group e shudhu users → False

Instructor feedback (English)

A: IAM User Groups can not be part of other User Groups.

B: IAM User Groups can contain only IAM Users.

4·9U04-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 04 · Q9

An IAM policy consists of one or more statements. A statement in an IAM Policy consists of the following, EXCEPT:

একটি IAM policy এক বা একাধিক statement নিয়ে গঠিত। একটি IAM Policy-র statement নিচেরগুলো নিয়ে গঠিত, তবে এটি বাদে (EXCEPT):

Correct: C. Why it is right

IAM policy structure (PDF p. 28): policy level e Version, Id (optional), Statement. Protita Statement e: Sid, Effect, Principal, Action, Resource, Condition.

Tai Version statement er part na, puro policy document er part ("2012-10-17").

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Effect (Allow/Deny) statement er part.
  • B. Principal (kon account/user/role er upor apply) statement er part.
  • D. Action (kon API call allow/deny) statement er part.
  • E. Resource (kon resource e action apply) statement er part.

Keyword: statement er part EXCEPT → Version (eta policy level e)

Instructor feedback (English)

C: A statement in an IAM Policy consists of Sid, Effect, Principal, Action, Resource, and Condition. Version is part of the IAM Policy itself, not the statement.

Section 05: EC2 Fundamentals (12)

Note: Section 05 note. Answer first, then press Check.

5·1U05-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q1

Which EC2 Purchasing Option can provide you the biggest discount, but it is not suitable for critical jobs or databases?

কোন EC2 Purchasing Option আপনাকে সবচেয়ে বড় discount দিতে পারে, কিন্তু critical job বা database-এর জন্য উপযুক্ত নয়?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Spot Instances holo AWS er sobcheye cheap option: On-Demand er tulonay up to 90% discount (PDF p. 68).

Kintu AWS jekono shomoy instance ta ferot nite pare (spot price tomar max price er upore gele), tai course bole: not suitable for critical jobs or databases. Batch job, data analysis, image processing er moto failure-resilient kaj er jonno Spot best.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Convertible RI long workload er jonno, instance type/family change kora jay; discount up to 66%, Spot er 90% er cheye kom.
  • B. Dedicated Host hocche sobcheye expensive option (puro physical server book); compliance/BYOL er jonno, discount er jonno na.

Keyword: biggest discount + not suitable for critical jobs or databases → Spot Instances

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Convertible Reserved Instances are good for long workloads with flexible instances. But, it does not provide you the biggest discount.

B: Dedicated Hosts are good for companies with strong compliance needs or for software that have complicated licensing models. This is the most expensive EC2 Purchasing Option available.

C: Spot Instances are good for short workloads and this is the cheapest EC2 Purchasing Option. But, they are less reliable because you can lose your EC2 instance.

5·2U05-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q2

What should you use to control traffic in and out of EC2 instances?

EC2 instance-এ traffic ঢোকা ও বের হওয়া control করতে আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Security Group holo EC2 instance er charpashe firewall. Eta control kore kon traffic instance e dhukte parbe (inbound) ar kon traffic ber hote parbe (outbound) (PDF p. 52-53).

SG te port, IP range (IPv4/IPv6) ba onno SG reference kore rule likha hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. NACL subnet level e kaj kore, instance level e na; eta pore VPC section e ashbe. EC2 traffic control er main answer SG.
  • C. IAM Policy AWS API/service access control kore (ke ki action korte parbe), network traffic na.

Keyword: control traffic in and out of EC2 instances → Security Groups

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is a distractor. We will see this service later in the course. NACL operates at the subnet level.

B: Security Groups operate at the EC2 instance level and can control traffic.

C: IAM Policies enable you to manage access to AWS services and resources securely.

5·3U05-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q3

How long can you reserve an EC2 Reserved Instance?

আপনি একটি EC2 Reserved Instance কত সময়ের জন্য reserve করতে পারেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Reserved Instance er reservation period shudhu 1 year (+discount) othoba 3 years (+++discount) (PDF p. 66).

Majhamajhi kono duration (jemon 2 years ba 18 months) kena jay na. Savings Plans o 1 ba 3 year commitment.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. 2 ba 4 year er kono RI term nai; shudhu 1 ar 3 year.
  • C. 6 month er RI term nai. Minimum term 1 year.
  • D. Jekono duration choose kora jay na; fixed term shudhu 1 year ba 3 year.

Keyword: how long reserve → 1 or 3 years

Instructor feedback (English)

A: EC2 Reserved Instances can be reserved for 1 or 3 years only.

D: You must reserve the instance only for 1 or 3 years, not any time in between.

5·4U05-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q4

You would like to deploy a High-Performance Computing (HPC) application on EC2 instances. Which EC2 instance type should you choose?

আপনি EC2 instance-এ একটি High-Performance Computing (HPC) application deploy করতে চান। আপনার কোন EC2 instance type বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

HPC (High Performance Computing) holo compute-intensive kaj, mane powerful CPU lage.

Course slide e Compute Optimized er use case list e direct ache: batch processing, media transcoding, HPC, scientific modeling & ML, gaming server (PDF p. 48).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Storage Optimized local storage e high sequential read/write er jonno (OLTP, data warehouse), CPU-heavy HPC er jonno na.
  • C. Memory Optimized boro data set RAM e process korar jonno (in-memory DB, cache), HPC er CPU need er jonno na.
  • D. General Purpose CPU, memory, network er balance; web server/code repo er moto kaj er jonno, high-performance compute er jonno na.

Keyword: High-Performance Computing (HPC) → Compute Optimized

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Storage Optimized EC2 instances are great for workloads requiring high, sequential read/write access to large data sets on local storage.

B: Compute Optimized EC2 instances are great for compute-intensive workloads requiring high-performance processors (e.g., batch processing, media transcoding, high-performance computing, scientific modeling & machine learning, and dedicated gaming servers).

C: Memory Optimized EC2 instances are great for workloads requiring large data sets in memory.

D: General Purpose EC2 instances are great for a diversity of workloads that require a balance between computing, memory, and network.

5·5U05-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q5

Which EC2 Purchasing Option should you use for an application you plan to run on a server continuously for 1 year?

যে application আপনি একটি server-এ 1 বছর ধরে একটানা চালানোর পরিকল্পনা করছেন, তার জন্য কোন EC2 Purchasing Option ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Server continuously 1 year cholbe = steady, long workload. Eta exactly Reserved Instance er use case: 1 ba 3 year commit korle On-Demand er cheye onek discount (up to 72%) (PDF p. 66).

Course: RI steady-state usage er jonno recommended.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Spot cheap kintu jekono shomoy interrupt hote pare; continuously cholte hobe emon app er jonno reliable na.
  • C. On-Demand short, unpredictable workload er jonno; 1 year continuously chalale full price dite hobe, RI er cheye beshi khoroch.

Keyword: run continuously for 1 year → Reserved Instances

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Reserved Instances are good for long workloads. You can reserve EC2 instances for 1 or 3 years.

B: Spot Instances are good for short workloads but are less reliable.

C: On-demand Instances are good for short workloads with predictable pricing.

5·6U05-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q6

You are preparing to launch an application that will be hosted on a set of EC2 instances. This application needs some software installation and some OS packages need to be updated during the first launch. What is the best way to achieve this when you launch the EC2 instances?

আপনি একটি application launch করার প্রস্তুতি নিচ্ছেন, যা এক সেট EC2 instance-এ hosted হবে। প্রথম launch-এর সময় এই application-এর কিছু software installation দরকার এবং কিছু OS package update করতে হবে। EC2 instance launch করার সময় এটি করার সবচেয়ে ভালো উপায় কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EC2 User Data diye instance bootstrap kora hoy: launch er shomoy script dile seta first start e ekbar run hoy, root user hishebe (PDF p. 44).

Software install, OS update, file download, sob automate kora jay. Onek instance hole proti ta te manually kaj korte hoy na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Kaj hobe, kintu manual SSH kore proti instance e install kora slow ar error-prone; automation er best way na.
  • B. AWS Support tomar instance e script run kore na; eta tomar nijer dayitto (User Data diye).

Keyword: software installation + OS update during the first launch → bash script in EC2 User Data

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This can be done, but a lot of work to do that takes a lot of time especially if your application will be hosted on a large number of EC2 instances.

B: There's no such thing. You can't contact AWS Support to install software and packages on your EC2 instances.

C: EC2 User Data is used to bootstrap your EC2 instances using a bash script. This script can contain commands such as installing software/packages, download files from the Internet, or anything you want.

5·7U05-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q7

Which EC2 Instance Type should you choose for a critical application that uses an in-memory database?

একটি in-memory database ব্যবহার করে এমন একটি critical application-এর জন্য আপনার কোন EC2 Instance Type বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

In-memory database mane data RAM e thake, tai onek memory lage.

Memory Optimized instance boro data set memory te fast process korar jonno; course use case: in-memory databases, distributed cache, real-time big data processing (PDF p. 49).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Compute Optimized CPU-heavy kaj er jonno (HPC, batch, transcoding), RAM-heavy in-memory DB er jonno na.
  • B. Storage Optimized local disk e high sequential I/O er jonno; in-memory DB er main need RAM, disk na.
  • D. General Purpose balanced resource dey; critical in-memory DB er boro RAM need er jonno best fit na.

Keyword: in-memory database → Memory Optimized

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Compute Optimized EC2 instances are great for compute-intensive workloads requiring high-performance processors (e.g., batch processing, media transcoding, high-performance computing, scientific modeling & machine learning, and dedicated gaming servers).

B: Storage Optimized EC2 instances are great for workloads requiring high, sequential read/write access to large data sets on local storage.

C: Memory Optimized EC2 instances are great for workloads requiring large data sets in memory.

D: General Purpose EC2 instances are great for a diversity of workloads that require a balance between computing, memory, and network.

5·8U05-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q8

You have an e-commerce application with an OLTP database hosted on-premises. This application has popularity which results in its database has thousands of requests per second. You want to migrate the database to an EC2 instance. Which EC2 Instance Type should you choose to handle this high-frequency OLTP database?

আপনার একটি e-commerce application আছে, যার OLTP database on-premises-এ hosted। এই application-এর জনপ্রিয়তার কারণে এর database-এ প্রতি second-এ হাজার হাজার request আসে। আপনি database-টি একটি EC2 instance-এ migrate করতে চান। এই high-frequency OLTP database handle করতে আপনার কোন EC2 Instance Type বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Course slide e Storage Optimized er use case list e prothomei ache: High frequency online transaction processing (OLTP) systems (PDF p. 50).

Thousands of requests/second mane local storage e onek fast read/write (high IOPS) lage, ja Storage Optimized dey.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Compute Optimized CPU-heavy kaj er jonno; high-frequency OLTP er bottleneck disk I/O, CPU na.
  • C. Memory Optimized o database er jonno use hoy, kintu course slide e high frequency OLTP specifically Storage Optimized e listed; exam e ei keyword e Storage Optimized.
  • D. General Purpose balanced; thousands of transactions/second er high I/O er jonno optimized na.

Keyword: high-frequency OLTP database → Storage Optimized

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Compute Optimized EC2 instances are great for compute-intensive workloads requiring high-performance processors (e.g., batch processing, media transcoding, high-performance computing, scientific modeling & machine learning, and dedicated gaming servers).

B: Storage Optimized EC2 instances are great for workloads requiring high, sequential read/write access to large data sets on local storage.

C: Memory Optimized EC2 instances are great for workloads requiring large data sets in memory.

D: General Purpose EC2 instances are great for a diversity of workloads that require a balance between computing, memory, and network.

5·9U05-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q9

Security Groups can be attached to only one EC2 instance.

Security Groups শুধু একটি EC2 instance-এ attach করা যায়।

Correct: A. Why it is right

Statement ta False. Ekta Security Group multiple instances e attach kora jay, ar ekta instance e multiple SG o thakte pare (PDF p. 55).

SG ekta region/VPC combination e locked thake.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Bhul: SG ar instance er moddhe one-to-one relation nai; ek SG onek instance e lagano jay.

Keyword: attached to only one EC2 instance → False (multiple instances possible)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Security Groups can be attached to multiple EC2 instances within the same AWS Region/VPC.

5·10U05-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q10

You're planning to migrate on-premises applications to AWS. Your company has strict compliance requirements that require your applications to run on dedicated servers. You also need to use your own server-bound software license to reduce costs. Which EC2 Purchasing Option is suitable for you?

আপনি on-premises application-গুলো AWS-এ migrate করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন। আপনার company-র কঠোর compliance requirement আছে, যার জন্য আপনার application-গুলোকে dedicated server-এ চালাতে হবে। cost কমাতে আপনাকে আপনার নিজের server-bound software license-ও ব্যবহার করতে হবে। কোন EC2 Purchasing Option আপনার জন্য উপযুক্ত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Duto shorto: strict compliance (dedicated server lagbe) + own server-bound software license (BYOL).

Dedicated Host e puro physical server tomar; compliance requirement meet kore ar per-socket/per-core license use kora jay (PDF p. 69).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Convertible RI shudhu billing discount + instance type change er flexibility; dedicated server ba BYOL license support dey na.
  • C. Spot shared hardware e chole ar jekono shomoy interrupt hoy; compliance ba server-bound license kono tai mele na.

Keyword: compliance + dedicated servers + own server-bound license → Dedicated Hosts

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Convertible Reserved Instances are good for long workloads with flexible instances. But, it does not provide you the biggest discount.

B: Dedicated Hosts are good for companies with strong compliance needs or for software that have complicated licensing models. This is the most expensive EC2 Purchasing Option available.

C: Spot Instances are good for short workloads and this is the cheapest EC2 Purchasing Option. But, they are less reliable because you can lose your EC2 instance.

5·11U05-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q11

You would like to deploy a database technology on an EC2 instance and the vendor license bills you based on the physical cores and underlying network socket visibility. Which EC2 Purchasing Option allows you to get visibility into them?

আপনি একটি EC2 instance-এ একটি database technology deploy করতে চান, এবং vendor license আপনাকে physical core এবং underlying network socket visibility-র ভিত্তিতে bill করে। কোন EC2 Purchasing Option আপনাকে এগুলোর visibility পেতে দেয়?

Correct: C. Why it is right

License bill hoy physical cores ar sockets er upor. Tai underlying hardware dekhte (visibility) parte hobe.

Shudhu Dedicated Host physical server er lower-level hardware er visibility dey, tai per-socket/per-core/per-VM license er jonno eta lage (PDF p. 69).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Spot shared hardware e chole, physical core/socket er visibility dey na.
  • B. On-Demand ekta billing option; shared hardware, physical socket/core dekha jay na.
  • D. Reserved Instance shudhu discount; hardware visibility dey na.

Keyword: license billed on physical cores / sockets + visibility → Dedicated Hosts

5·12U05-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 05 · Q12

Spot Fleet is a set of Spot Instances and optionally ...............

Spot Fleet হলো Spot Instance-এর একটি set এবং ঐচ্ছিকভাবে ...............

Correct: B. Why it is right

Course definition: Spot Fleet = set of Spot Instances + (optional) On-Demand Instances (PDF p. 77).

Fleet target capacity meet korar cheshta kore price constraint mene, multiple launch pool theke.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Spot Fleet e Reserved Instance add kora hoy na; fleet e shudhu Spot ar optional On-Demand.
  • C. Dedicated Host Spot Fleet er part na; eta alada purchasing option.
  • D. Dedicated Instance Spot Fleet definition e nai; fleet = Spot + optional On-Demand.

Keyword: Spot Fleet = Spot Instances and optionally ... → On-Demand Instances

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Spot Fleet is a set of Spot Instances and optionally On-demand Instances. It allows you to automatically request Spot Instances with the lowest price.

Section 06: EC2 - Solutions Architect Associate Level (5)

Note: Section 06 note. Answer first, then press Check.

6·1U06-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 06 · Q1

You have launched an EC2 instance that will host a NodeJS application. After installing all the required software and configured your application, you noted down the EC2 instance public IPv4 so you can access it. Then, you stopped and then started your EC2 instance to complete the application configuration. After restart, you can't access the EC2 instance, and you found that the EC2 instance public IPv4 has been changed. What should you do to assign a fixed public IPv4 to your EC2 instance?

আপনি একটি EC2 instance launch করেছেন, যা একটি NodeJS application host করবে। সব প্রয়োজনীয় software install করে এবং application configure করার পরে, access করার জন্য আপনি EC2 instance-এর public IPv4 লিখে রেখেছেন। তারপর application configuration শেষ করতে আপনি EC2 instance stop করে আবার start করলেন। restart-এর পরে আপনি EC2 instance-এ access করতে পারছেন না, এবং দেখলেন EC2 instance-এর public IPv4 বদলে গেছে। আপনার EC2 instance-এ একটি fixed public IPv4 assign করতে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

EC2 instance stop → start korle tar public IPv4 bodlate pare (AWS er pool theke notun IP ashe). Fixed public IPv4 lagle Elastic IP allocate kore instance e associate koro (PDF p. 82).

Elastic IP holo ekta public IPv4 ja tumi own koro jotokkhon release (delete) na koro; ek somoy ek instance e attach hoy. Course hands-on e dekhano: EIP attach thakle stop/start er poreo IP same thake.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. OS er bhitore public IP set kora jay na: instance er OS shudhu private IP dekhe (hostname e private IP), public IP AWS network level e map hoy. Static config dile network-i bhenge jete pare.
  • C. AWS Support er emon kono 'fixed public IP' service nai; console theke nijei Elastic IP allocate kora jay.
  • D. Bhul: Elastic IP diye fixed public IPv4 deya jay. Private IP to stop/start e emnitei same thake.

Keyword: public IPv4 changed after stop/start + fixed public IPv4 → Elastic IP

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Elastic IP is a public IPv4 that you own as long as you want and you can attach it to one EC2 instance at a time.

C: This can't be done, there's no such thing in AWS Support.

D: You can assign both public and private IPv4 to your EC2 instances.

6·2U06-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 06 · Q2

You have an application performing big data analysis hosted on a fleet of EC2 instances. You want to ensure your EC2 instances have the highest networking performance while communicating with each other. Which EC2 Placement Group should you choose?

EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet-এ আপনার একটি big data analysis করা application hosted আছে। আপনি নিশ্চিত করতে চান যে আপনার EC2 instance-গুলো একে অপরের সাথে communicate করার সময় সবচেয়ে বেশি networking performance পায়। আপনার কোন EC2 Placement Group বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Cluster placement group instance gulo ke ek AZ er bhitore low-latency hardware e pashapashi rakhe. Enhanced Networking on thakle instance gulor moddhe 10 Gbps bandwidth, low latency, high throughput (PDF p. 86).

Course er use case: Big Data job ja druto shesh korte hobe, ba app jar instance gulor moddhe extremely low latency + high network throughput lage. Ekhane exactly tai chawa hocche.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Spread protita instance alada physical hardware e (multi-AZ) rakhe failure risk komate; instance gulo dure dure thake, tai network performance best na.
  • C. Partition instance gulo ke alada rack er partition e bhag kore (Hadoop, Cassandra er moto) failure alada rakhar jonno; highest network performance er jonno na.

Keyword: highest networking performance between instances (big data) → Cluster placement group

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Spread Placement Group won't be suitable here as your EC2 instances are placed on different physical hardware. Thus, increases latency between your EC2 instances.

B: Cluster Placement Groups place your EC2 instances next to each other which gives you high-performance computing and networking.

C: Partition Placement Group won't be suitable here as your EC2 instances are grouped into partitions and distributed across different physical hardware. Thus, increases latency between your EC2 instances.

6·3U06-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 06 · Q3

You have a critical application hosted on a fleet of EC2 instances in which you want to achieve maximum availability when there's an AZ failure. Which EC2 Placement Group should you choose?

EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet-এ আপনার একটি critical application hosted আছে, যেখানে AZ failure হলে আপনি সর্বোচ্চ availability পেতে চান। আপনার কোন EC2 Placement Group বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Spread placement group e protita instance alada physical hardware e thake, ar group ta multiple AZ jure span korte pare. Tai ekta hardware ba ekta AZ fail korleo baki instance gulo bache (PDF p. 87).

Course er use case: app jar maximum high availability lage, ar critical app jekhane protita instance er failure onno gulo theke isolated. Limit: 7 instance per AZ per group.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Cluster sob instance ek AZ te rakhe; AZ fail korle sob instance ekshathe fail kore, ja maximum availability er ulto.
  • B. Partition boro distributed workload (HDFS, Cassandra) er jonno rack failure alada rakhe, kintu ek partition er onek instance ekshathe pore jete pare. Critical app er protita instance alada rakhte Spread better.

Keyword: critical application + maximum availability (AZ failure) → Spread placement group

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Cluster Placement Groups won't be suitable here your EC2 instances are placed next to each other which gives you high-performance computing and networking.

B: Partition Placement Group won't be suitable here as your EC2 instances are grouped into partitions and distributed across different physical hardware. The question doesn't ask about connectivity between EC2 instances.

C: Spread Placement Group places your EC2 instances on different physical hardware across different AZs.

6·4U06-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 06 · Q4

Elastic Network Interface (ENI) can be attached to EC2 instances in another AZ.

Elastic Network Interface (ENI) অন্য AZ-এর EC2 instance-এ attach করা যায়।

Correct: B. Why it is right

False. ENI ekta specific Availability Zone e bandha (PDF p. 90). ENI banate subnet choose korte hoy, ar subnet ek AZ er; tai shudhu oi AZ er EC2 instance e attach ba move kora jay.

Failover er jonno ENI ek instance theke arek instance e move kora jay, kintu dui instance-i same AZ e thakte hobe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul: ENI AZ-bound, onno AZ er instance e attach hoy na. Onno AZ e lagle oi AZ er subnet e notun ENI banate hobe.

Keyword: ENI + another AZ → False (ENI is bound to one AZ)

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Elastic Network Interfaces (ENIs) are bounded to a specific AZ. You can not attach an ENI to an EC2 instance in a different AZ.

6·5U06-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 06 · Q5

The following are true regarding EC2 Hibernate, EXCEPT:

EC2 Hibernate সম্পর্কে নিচেরগুলো সত্য, তবে এটি বাদে (EXCEPT):

Correct: A. Why it is right

EXCEPT proshno: bhul statement ta khujte hobe. Hibernate e RAM er state root EBS volume er ekta file e likha hoy, tai root volume must be EBS, encrypted, not instance store, ar RAM dhorar moto boro (PDF p. 92-93).

Tai 'root volume must be Instance Store' bhul → eta-i answer. Baki tin ta (On-Demand/Reserved support, RAM < 150 GB, root EBS) slide onujayi thik.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Thik statement: Hibernate On-Demand, Reserved ar Spot instance e available (PDF p. 93). EXCEPT proshne thik statement answer na.
  • C. Thik statement: instance RAM 150 GB er kom hote hobe (slide er limit; instructor bole limit bodlate pare, exam e dharona-i main).
  • D. Thik statement: root volume EBS hote hobe (ar encrypted), karon RAM er dump oi EBS e likha hoy.

Keyword: EXCEPT + Hibernate root volume → Instance Store bhul (must be encrypted EBS)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: To enable EC2 Hibernate, the EC2 Instance Root Volume type must be an EBS volume and must be encrypted to ensure the protection of sensitive content.

Section 07: EC2 Instance Storage (9)

Note: Section 07 note. Answer first, then press Check.

7·1U07-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q1

You have just terminated an EC2 instance in us-east-1a, and its attached EBS volume is now available. Your teammate tries to attach it to an EC2 instance in us-east-1b but he can't. What is a possible cause for this?

আপনি সবেমাত্র us-east-1a-তে একটি EC2 instance terminate করেছেন, এবং এর attached EBS volume এখন available। আপনার সহকর্মী এটি us-east-1b-র একটি EC2 instance-এ attach করার চেষ্টা করছেন, কিন্তু পারছেন না। এর একটি সম্ভাব্য কারণ কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EBS volume ekta specific AZ e bandha (PDF p. 95-96). us-east-1a er volume shudhu us-east-1a er instance e attach hoy; us-east-1b er instance e hoy na.

Onno AZ e nite hole: age snapshot nao, tarpor oi snapshot theke us-east-1b te notun volume create koro (PDF p. 99).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. IAM permission thakleo AZ alada hole attach hoy na. Question er asol clue holo 1a vs 1b, mane alada AZ.
  • B. EBS Region e na, aro choto scope AZ e bandha. Region lock hole same Region er 1b te attach hoye jeto.

Keyword: us-east-1a er volume → us-east-1b er instance e attach hoy na → EBS AZ locked

Instructor feedback (English)

C: EBS Volumes are created for a specific AZ. It is possible to migrate them between different AZs using EBS Snapshots.

7·2U07-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q2

You have launched an EC2 instance with two EBS volumes, Root volume type and the other EBS volume type to store the data. A month later you are planning to terminate the EC2 instance. What's the default behavior that will happen to each EBS volume?

আপনি দুটি EBS volume-সহ একটি EC2 instance launch করেছেন: Root volume type এবং data store করার জন্য অন্য EBS volume type। এক মাস পরে আপনি EC2 instance terminate করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন। প্রতিটি EBS volume-এর ক্ষেত্রে default-ভাবে কী ঘটবে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Default Delete on Termination: root EBS volume e enabled, tai instance terminate e root delete hoy. Baki attached EBS volume e disabled, tai sheta theke jay (PDF p. 98).

Root volume rakhte chaile root er Delete on Termination off koro (console ba CLI).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Shudhu root er attribute default e on. Extra data volume er default off, tai sheta delete hoy na.
  • C. Ulta bola: default e root delete hoy, extra volume thake.
  • D. Root er Delete on Termination default e enabled, tai root delete hoy.

Keyword: default behavior on terminate → root delete, extra EBS volume thake

Instructor feedback (English)

B: By default, the Root volume type will be deleted as its "Delete On Termination" attribute checked by default. Any other EBS volume types will not be deleted as its "Delete On Termination" attribute disabled by default.

7·3U07-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q3

You can use an AMI in N.Virginia Region us-east-1 to launch an EC2 instance in any AWS Region.

আপনি N.Virginia Region us-east-1-এর একটি AMI ব্যবহার করে যেকোনো AWS Region-এ একটি EC2 instance launch করতে পারেন।

Correct: B. Why it is right

False. AMI ekta specific Region er jonno build hoy (PDF p. 101). us-east-1 er AMI diye shudhu us-east-1 e launch kora jay.

Onno Region e launch korte age AMI ta oi Region e copy koro, tarpor copied AMI theke launch koro.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. AMI Regional; onno Region e shorashori use hoy na, age copy lagbe.

Keyword: us-east-1 er AMI → any AWS Region? → False (AMI Regional, copy lage)

Instructor feedback (English)

B: AMIs are built for a specific AWS Region, they're unique for each AWS Region. You can't launch an EC2 instance using an AMI in another AWS Region, but you can copy the AMI to the target AWS Region and then use it to create your EC2 instances.

7·4U07-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q4

Which of the following EBS volume types can be used as boot volumes when you create EC2 instances?

EC2 instance তৈরি করার সময় নিচের কোন EBS volume type-গুলো boot volume হিসেবে ব্যবহার করা যায়?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Boot volume hote pare shudhu SSD type: gp2/gp3 ar io1/io2 (PDF p. 105). HDD type st1 ar sc1 boot volume hote pare na (PDF p. 108).

Original explanation e Magnetic (standard) o ache: eta purono previous-generation type, boot hoy, kintu option e nai.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. st1 ar sc1 HDD, boot volume hote pare na. Ei list e shudhu gp2/gp3 thik.
  • C. io1/io2 thik, kintu st1/sc1 HDD boot volume hote pare na.

Keyword: boot volumes → shudhu SSD: gp2, gp3, io1, io2 (st1/sc1 na)

Instructor feedback (English)

B: When creating EC2 instances, you can only use the following EBS volume types as boot volumes: gp2, gp3, io1, io2, and Magnetic (Standard).

7·5U07-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q5

What is EBS Multi-Attach?

EBS Multi-Attach কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EBS Multi-Attach: ek-i EBS volume same AZ er multiple EC2 instance e ekshathe attach (PDF p. 110). Shudhu io1/io2 family te; max 16 instance; protita instance er full read/write permission.

Use case: clustered Linux app (ex: Teradata); cluster-aware file system lage.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Multi-AZ hoy na: Multi-Attach soho EBS ek AZ e bandha. Onek AZ jure share lagle EFS.
  • B. Ek instance e onek volume attach kora normal EBS behaviour, eta Multi-Attach na.
  • D. EBS onno AZ er instance e attach-i hoy na, ar eta Multi-Attach er definition o na.

Keyword: same volume → multiple instances → same AZ = Multi-Attach

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Using EBS Multi-Attach, you can attach the same EBS volume to multiple EC2 instances in the same AZ. Each EC2 instance has full read/write permissions.

7·6U07-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q6

You would like to encrypt an unencrypted EBS volume attached to your EC2 instance. What should you do?

আপনি আপনার EC2 instance-এ attach করা একটি unencrypted EBS volume encrypt করতে চান। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Existing unencrypted EBS volume in-place encrypt hoy na. Course er dhap (PDF p. 112): volume er snapshot nao → snapshot copy koro ar encrypt tick dao (KMS key) → encrypted snapshot theke notun volume banao → original instance e attach.

Encrypted snapshot theke banano volume automatic encrypted. Hands-on shortcut: unencrypted snapshot theke create volume er somoy-i encryption on kora jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Edit Attributes e emon kono encrypt option nai; existing volume er encryption in-place on kora jay na.
  • C. Kaj kore, kintu nijer haate sob data copy korte onek time, manual kaj ar khoroch lage (boro volume e aro beshi). Snapshot route shohoj, AWS er documented way.
  • D. AWS Support tomar volume encrypt kore dey na; snapshot route e tumi nijei korte paro.

Keyword: encrypt an unencrypted EBS volume → snapshot → copy + encrypt → new volume

Instructor feedback (English)

C: This can be done but it'll take a lot of manual work, time, and will be costly especially if your EBS volume has a lot of data.

7·7U07-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q7

You have a fleet of EC2 instances distributes across AZs that process a large data set. What do you recommend to make the same data to be accessible as an NFS drive to all of your EC2 instances?

AZ-গুলো জুড়ে ছড়ানো আপনার EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet আছে, যেগুলো একটি বড় data set process করে। একই data আপনার সব EC2 instance-এর কাছে একটি NFS drive হিসেবে accessible করতে আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

EFS holo managed NFS (network file system): onek EC2 te mount hoy, ar instance gulo alada AZ e thakleo cholbe (PDF p. 113). Sob instance ek-i data dekhe.

Keyword: 'NFS drive' + 'across AZs' + 'same data' = EFS.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EBS ek AZ e bandha ar sadharonoto ek instance e mount hoy (Multi-Attach o shudhu same AZ); eta NFS na.
  • C. Instance Store oi host er physical disk, shudhu oi instance er; share kora jay na, ar ephemeral.

Keyword: across AZs + same data + NFS drive → EFS

Instructor feedback (English)

A: EBS volumes are locked to an AZ and can only be mounted on one EC2 instance at a time.

B: EFS is a network file system (NFS) that allows you to mount the same file system on EC2 instances that are in different AZs.

C: EC2 Instance Store is a storage located on disks that are physically attached to the host of your EC2 instance. Can be used only on one EC2 instance at a time. It's ideal for temporary storage such as buffers, caches, etc.

7·8U07-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q8

You would like to have a high-performance local cache for your application hosted on an EC2 instance. You don't mind losing the cache upon the termination of your EC2 instance. Which storage mechanism do you recommend as a Solutions Architect?

একটি EC2 instance-এ hosted আপনার application-এর জন্য আপনি একটি high-performance local cache চান। আপনার EC2 instance terminate হলে cache হারালে আপনার আপত্তি নেই। একজন Solutions Architect হিসেবে আপনি কোন storage mechanism recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EC2 Instance Store = host server e physically lagano disk: better I/O performance, sobcheye beshi IOPS (PDF p. 103-104). Kintu ephemeral: stop/terminate e data jay.

Course bole eta buffer / cache / scratch / temporary content er jonno bhalo. Ekhane cache harale problem nai, tai perfect fit.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EBS network drive, ektu latency ache; performance bhalo kintu 'limited', Instance Store er moto local na.
  • B. EFS network file system, onek instance share kore; local high-performance cache er jonno na, ar dami.

Keyword: high-performance local cache + don't mind losing on termination → Instance Store

Instructor feedback (English)

A: EBS are network drives which makes it provides less I/O performance than Instance Store.

B: EFS volumes are network file systems that can be mounted on many EC2 instances. EFS volumes are accessible through network which makes it provides less performance I/O than Instance Store.

C: EC2 Instance Store provides the best disk I/O performance.

7·9U07-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 07 · Q9

You are running a high-performance database that requires an IOPS of 310,000 for its underlying storage. What do you recommend?

আপনি একটি high-performance database চালাচ্ছেন, যার underlying storage-এর জন্য 310,000 IOPS দরকার। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EBS er sobcheye fast volume io2 Block Express er max 256,000 IOPS (PDF p. 107). 310,000 IOPS kono EBS volume dite pare na, tai EC2 Instance Store (i3 er moto instance e millions IOPS, PDF p. 104).

Shorto: instance store ephemeral, tai replication/backup tomar dayitto (PDF p. 103). Note: explanation er 'io2 64,000' purono; ekhon sob io2 volume-i Block Express (256,000).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. gp2 er max 16,000 IOPS (PDF p. 106), 310,000 er dhare kache na.
  • B. io1 er max 64,000 IOPS (Nitro), onno instance e 32,000 (PDF p. 107); onek kom.
  • D. io2 Block Express er max 256,000 IOPS; 310,000 er cheye kom.

Keyword: 310,000 IOPS (EBS max 256,000 er beshi) → Instance Store

Instructor feedback (English)

A: 16,000 is the maximum IOPS you can achieve when you're using the EBS gp2 volume type.

B: 64,000 is the maximum IOPS you can achieve when you're using EBS io1 or io2 volume types.

C: You can run a database on an EC2 instance that uses an Instance Store, but you'll have a problem that the data will be lost if the EC2 instance is stopped (it can be restarted without problems). One solution is that you can set up a replication mechanism on another EC2 instance with an Instance Store to have a standby copy. Another solution is to set up backup mechanisms for your data. It's all up to you how you want to set up your architecture to validate your requirements. In this use case, it's around IOPS, so we have to choose an EC2 Instance Store.

D: 256,000 is the maximum IOPS you can achieve when you're using the EBS io2 Block Express volume type.

Section 08: High Availability and Scalability ELB & ASG (21)

Note: Section 08 note. Answer first, then press Check.

8·1U08-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q1

Scaling an EC2 instance from r4.large to r4.4xlarge is called .....................

Correct: B. Why it is right

Instance er size barano (r4.large → r4.4xlarge) = Vertical Scalability, mane scale up (PDF p. 121, 124). Instance er sonkhya same thake, shudhu ekta-i boro hoy.

Vertical scaling er hardware limit ache, ar eta DB er moto non-distributed system e common.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Horizontal = instance er sonkhya barano (scale out/in). Ekhane sonkhya na, size barche.

Keyword: r4.large → r4.4xlarge (boro size) → Vertical (scale up)

8·2U08-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q2

Running an application on an Auto Scaling Group that scales the number of EC2 instances in and out is called .....................

Correct: A. Why it is right

ASG instance er sonkhya barano-komano kore (scale out / scale in) = Horizontal Scalability (PDF p. 122, 124). Horizontal scaling distributed system e hoy, web app e common.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Vertical = ekta instance er size barano (scale up/down). ASG instance er size bodlay na, sonkhya bodlay.

Keyword: ASG number of instances in/out → Horizontal

8·3U08-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q3

Elastic Load Balancers provide a .......................

Correct: B. Why it is right

Sob ELB ekta static DNS name dey (XXX.region.elb.amazonaws.com), app ei ek endpoint use kore (PDF p. 126, 137). Pichoner infrastructure AWS bodlaleo DNS name same thake.

Static IP dey shudhu NLB (AZ proti ekta, Elastic IP o dewa jay). Tai general ELB er uttor: static DNS name.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Static IPv4 shob ELB dey na; shudhu NLB dey. ALB/CLB er IP bodlate pare, tai DNS name use koro.
  • C. ELB static IPv6 dey na. Fixed endpoint holo DNS name.

Keyword: Elastic Load Balancers provide (sob type) → static DNS name

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Only Network Load Balancer provides both static DNS name and static IP. While, Application Load Balancer provides a static DNS name but it does NOT provide a static IP. The reason being that AWS wants your Elastic Load Balancer to be accessible using a static endpoint, even if the underlying infrastructure that AWS manages changes.

8·4U08-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q4

You are running a website on 10 EC2 instances fronted by an Elastic Load Balancer. Your users are complaining about the fact that the website always asks them to re-authenticate when they are moving between website pages. You are puzzled because it's working just fine on your machine and in the Dev environment with 1 EC2 instance. What could be the reason?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Dev e 1 ta instance, tai sob request ek-i instance e jay, session thake. Production e 10 ta instance: ELB request gulo alada instance e pathay, notun instance e user er session nai, tai abar login chay.

Fix: Sticky Sessions (session affinity). Cookie diye ek client ke sob somoy ek-i instance e pathay, user session data harabe na (PDF p. 143).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Website e bug na; 1 instance e thik chole. Problem holo request alada instance e jacche, session share hocche na.
  • B. Instance client IP na dekhle-o logout kore na. Client IP lagle X-Forwarded-For header ache; login harano stickiness er problem.

Keyword: 10 instances + bar bar re-authenticate, 1 instance e thik → Sticky Sessions nai

Instructor feedback (English)

C: ELB Sticky Session feature ensures traffic for the same client is always redirected to the same target (e.g., EC2 instance). This helps that the client does not lose his session data.

8·5U08-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q5

You are using an Application Load Balancer to distribute traffic to your website hosted on EC2 instances. It turns out that your website only sees traffic coming from private IPv4 addresses which are in fact your Application Load Balancer's IP addresses. What should you do to get the IP address of clients connected to your website?

Correct: B. Why it is right

ALB connection termination kore: EC2 te notun connection khole nijer private IP diye. Tai app shudhu ALB er IP dekhe (PDF p. 137).

Ashol client IP ALB header X-Forwarded-For e boshay. Backend ei header theke client IP porbe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. User ke IP pathate bola lagbe na, ar user er pathano IP bishwasjoggo na. ALB nijei X-Forwarded-For e client IP dey.
  • C. X-Forwarded-Port e client er port thake, IP na.
  • D. X-Forwarded-Proto e protocol (HTTP/HTTPS) thake, IP na.

Keyword: ALB er private IP dekhe, client IP chai → X-Forwarded-For

Instructor feedback (English)

B: When using an Application Load Balancer to distribute traffic to your EC2 instances, the IP address you'll receive requests from will be the ALB's private IP addresses. To get the client's IP address, ALB adds an additional header called "X-Forwarded-For" contains the client's IP address.

C: "X-Forwarded-Port" used to get the client's requested port.

D: "X-Forwarded-Proto" used to get the client's requested protocol.

8·6U08-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q6

You hosted an application on a set of EC2 instances fronted by an Elastic Load Balancer. A week later, users begin complaining that sometimes the application just doesn't work. You investigate the issue and found that some EC2 instances crash from time to time. What should you do to protect users from connecting to the EC2 instances that are crashing?

Correct: A. Why it is right

ELB Health Check port ar route (ex: /health) e check kore; 200 OK na pele instance unhealthy, ELB oi instance e traffic pathano bondho kore (PDF p. 128).

Tai crash kora instance e user ar jabe na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Stickiness ek client ke ek-i instance e bendhe rakhe; crash kora instance e user atke jete pare, problem barbe.
  • C. SSL termination holo HTTPS decrypt kora LB e; instance healthy ki na ta dekhe na.
  • D. Cross-zone sob AZ er instance e shoman traffic bhag kore; unhealthy instance ke bad dey na.

Keyword: kichu instance crash, user jeno oikhane na jay → ELB Health Checks

Instructor feedback (English)

A: When you enable ELB Health Checks, your ELB won't send traffic to unhealthy (crashed) EC2 instances.

8·7U08-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q7

You are working as a Solutions Architect for a company and you are required to design an architecture for a high-performance, low-latency application that will receive millions of requests per second. Which type of Elastic Load Balancer should you choose?

Correct: C. Why it is right

NLB Layer 4 (TCP/UDP), millions of requests per second handle kore, ultra-low latency (PDF p. 138). Extreme performance lagle NLB.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ALB Layer 7, HTTP request er bhitor dekhe routing kore; flexible kintu NLB er moto extreme performance/lowest latency na.
  • B. CLB purono generation (deprecated); high performance er jonno na.

Keyword: high-performance, low-latency, millions of requests per second → NLB

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Network Load Balancer provides the highest performance and lowest latency if your application needs it.

8·8U08-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q8

Application Load Balancers support the following protocols, EXCEPT:

Correct: C. Why it is right

ALB Layer 7: HTTP, HTTPS, WebSocket (ar HTTP/2) support kore (PDF p. 129, 132). Plain TCP Layer 4, sheta NLB er kaj.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. HTTP ALB support kore (Layer 7 er mul protocol).
  • B. HTTPS ALB support kore; ACM certificate diye SSL termination o kore.
  • D. WebSocket ALB support kore (PDF p. 132).

Keyword: ALB EXCEPT → TCP (TCP = NLB)

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Application Load Balancers support HTTP, HTTPS and WebSocket

8·9U08-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q9

Application Load Balancers can route traffic to different Target Groups based on the following, EXCEPT:

Correct: A. Why it is right

ALB routing kore URL path, hostname, query string, header diye (PDF p. 133); listener rule condition e source IP ar HTTP method o ache (hands-on).

Client er geography (location) diye ALB route kore na; location based routing Route 53 er kaj.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Hostname routing ALB kore: one.example.com vs other.example.com (PDF p. 133).
  • C. Path routing ALB kore: example.com/users vs /posts (PDF p. 133).
  • D. Listener rule e source IP condition dewa jay, tai eta ALB support kore.

Keyword: ALB routing EXCEPT → Client Location (Geography)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: ALBs can route traffic to different Target Groups based on URL Path, Hostname, HTTP Headers, and Query Strings.

8·10U08-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q10

Registered targets in a Target Groups for an Application Load Balancer can be one of the following, EXCEPT:

Correct: B. Why it is right

ALB target group e thakte pare: EC2 instances, ECS tasks, Lambda functions, private IP addresses (PDF p. 135).

NLB ALB er target hote pare na. Ulto ta hoy: ALB NLB er target hote pare (PDF p. 140).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EC2 instance ALB er sobcheye common target (ASG manage korte pare).
  • C. Private IP target hoy, jemon on-premises server (PDF p. 135, 136).
  • D. Lambda ALB er target hoy; HTTP request JSON event e translate hoy (PDF p. 135).

Keyword: ALB target EXCEPT → Network Load Balancer (ulto ta valid: NLB → ALB)

8·11U08-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q11

For compliance purposes, you would like to expose a fixed static IP address to your end-users so that they can write firewall rules that will be stable and approved by regulators. What type of Elastic Load Balancer would you choose?

Correct: B. Why it is right

NLB e AZ proti ekta static IP, ar Elastic IP assign kora jay (PDF p. 138). User/regulator ei fixed IP diye firewall rule likhte pare, IP bodlabe na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ALB e Elastic IP attach kora jay na. ALB shudhu static DNS name dey, IP bodlate pare.
  • C. CLB o shudhu static DNS name dey, fixed static IP na. Ar CLB deprecated.

Keyword: fixed static IP + firewall rules → NLB

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You can't attach an Elastic IP address to Application Load Balancers.

B: Network Load Balancer has one static IP address per AZ and you can attach an Elastic IP address to it. Application Load Balancers and Classic Load Balancers have a static DNS name.

8·12U08-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q12

You want to create a custom application-based cookie in your Application Load Balancer. Which of the following you can use as a cookie name?

Custom application-based cookie er naam tumi dao, kintu AWSALB, AWSALBAPP, AWSALBTG use kora jabe na: ei naam gulo ELB er jonno reserved (PDF p. 144).

Tai APPUSERC er moto onno naam valid.

8·13U08-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q13

You have a Network Load Balancer that distributes traffic across a set of EC2 instances in us-east-1. You have 2 EC2 instances in us-east-1b AZ and 5 EC2 instances in us-east-1e AZ. You have noticed that the CPU utilization is higher in the EC2 instances in us-east-1b AZ. After more investigation, you noticed that the traffic is equally distributed across the two AZs. How would you solve this problem?

Correct: A. Why it is right

NLB e cross-zone default off (PDF p. 146). Off thakle traffic age AZ proti shoman (50% / 50%), tarpor AZ er bhitore. us-east-1b er 2 ta instance protiti 25% pay, 1e er 5 ta protiti 10%: tai 1b er CPU beshi.

Cross-Zone Load Balancing enable korle sob AZ er 7 ta instance e shoman bhag hoy (PDF p. 145). NLB e on korle inter-AZ data charge lage.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Sticky session ek client ke ek instance e bendhe rakhe; AZ er moddhe asoman bhag thik kore na, ulto imbalance barate pare.
  • C. Health check unhealthy instance bad dey; instance gulo healthy, problem traffic er bhag.
  • D. SSL termination encryption er bishoy; traffic distribution bodlay na.

Keyword: AZ e instance 2 vs 5, traffic AZ e equally → Cross-Zone Load Balancing

Instructor feedback (English)

A: When Cross-Zone Load Balancing is enabled, ELB distributes traffic evenly across all registered EC2 instances in all AZs.

8·14U08-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q14

Which feature in both Application Load Balancers and Network Load Balancers allows you to load multiple SSL certificates on one listener?

Correct: B. Why it is right

SNI (Server Name Indication): client SSL handshake e hostname bole dey, LB oi hostname er certificate khuje dey. Ete ek listener e onek SSL certificate load kora jay (PDF p. 149-150).

SNI kaj kore ALB, NLB ar CloudFront e; CLB e na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. TLS termination = LB e HTTPS decrypt kora; eta multiple certificate er feature na.
  • C. Security policy purono SSL/TLS version (legacy client) support er jonno (PDF p. 148); certificate select kore na.
  • D. Host header ALB er routing condition; certificate load kora na.

Keyword: multiple SSL certificates on one listener (ALB + NLB) → SNI

8·15U08-Q15Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q15

You have an Application Load Balancer that is configured to redirect traffic to 3 Target Groups based on the following hostnames: users.example.com, api.external.example.com, and checkout.example.com. You would like to configure HTTPS for each of these hostnames. How do you configure the ALB to make this work?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Tin ta hostname, protita te nijer certificate. HTTPS listener e onek certificate rakha jay, ar SNI diye client je hostname chay oi certificate dewa hoy (PDF p. 148-150). Tarpor host rule thik target group e pathay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. HTTP → HTTPS redirect user ke HTTPS e pathay, kintu protita hostname er certificate er problem solve kore na.
  • B. Security group firewall; 'security group SSL certificate' bole kono feature nai.

Keyword: ek ALB, 3 hostnames, sobai HTTPS → SNI

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Server Name Indication (SNI) allows you to expose multiple HTTPS applications each with its own SSL certificate on the same listener. Read more here: https://aws.amazon.com/blogs/aws/new-application-load-balancer-sni/

8·16U08-Q16Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q16

You have an application hosted on a set of EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group that you configured both desired and maximum capacity to 3. Also, you have created a CloudWatch Alarm that is configured to scale out your ASG when CPU Utilization reaches 60%. Your application suddenly received huge traffic and is now running at 80% CPU Utilization. What will happen?

Correct: A. Why it is right

ASG kokhono maximum capacity er upore jay na. Desired already 3 = max 3, tai CPU 80% e alarm trigger holeo notun instance add hobe na: kichui hoy na.

Scale out chaile max size barate hobe (PDF p. 153, 155).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Desired max er beshi hote pare na; max 3 thakle desired 4 hobe na.
  • C. Alarm nijer theke max capacity bodlay na; max tumi set koro.

Keyword: desired = maximum = 3 → scale out alarm e-o Nothing

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The Auto Scaling Group can't go over the maximum capacity (you configured) during scale-out events.

8·17U08-Q17Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q17

You have an Auto Scaling Group fronted by an Application Load Balancer. You have configured the ASG to use ALB Health Checks, then one EC2 instance has just been reported unhealthy. What will happen to the EC2 instance?

আপনার একটি Auto Scaling Group আছে, যার সামনে একটি Application Load Balancer রয়েছে। আপনি ASG-কে ALB Health Checks ব্যবহার করার জন্য configure করেছেন, তারপর একটি EC2 instance এইমাত্র unhealthy হিসেবে report হয়েছে। EC2 instance-টির কী হবে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

ASG ke ALB (ELB) health check use korte set korle, ALB unhealthy bolle ASG oi instance ke terminate kore, ar notun instance launch kore (PDF p. 152, 154).

Default (EC2 health check) e shudhu EC2 status dekhe; ELB check on thakle app level er failure o dhore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ASG application restart kore na; unhealthy instance terminate kore replace kore.
  • B. Detach kore chalu rakha ASG er health check er behavior na; unhealthy instance terminate hoy.

Keyword: ASG uses ALB Health Checks + instance unhealthy → terminate (ar replace)

Instructor feedback (English)

C: You can configure the Auto Scaling Group to determine the EC2 instances' health based on Application Load Balancer Health Checks instead of EC2 Status Checks (default). When an EC2 instance fails the ALB Health Checks, it is marked unhealthy and will be terminated while the ASG launches a new EC2 instance.

8·18U08-Q18Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q18

Your boss asked you to scale your Auto Scaling Group based on the number of requests per minute your application makes to your database. What should you do?

আপনার boss আপনাকে বলেছেন, আপনার application প্রতি মিনিটে database-এ যতগুলো request করে, সেই সংখ্যার ভিত্তিতে Auto Scaling Group scale করতে। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

'App theke database e request per minute' er kono built-in CloudWatch metric nai. Tai app theke CloudWatch custom metric push koro, tar upor CloudWatch Alarm baniye ASG scale koro (PDF p. 156, 159: 'any custom metric').

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Impossible na: custom metric + alarm diye je kono metric e scale kora jay.
  • C. Detailed monitoring shudhu EC2 metric 1 minute e dey; DB request count er moto app metric dey na.

Keyword: requests per minute to your database (built-in metric nai) → custom metric + alarm

Instructor feedback (English)

A: There's no CloudWatch Metric for "requests per minute" for backend-to-database connections. You need to create a CloudWatch Custom Metric, then create a CloudWatch Alarm.

8·19U08-Q19Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q19

An application is deployed with an Application Load Balancer and an Auto Scaling Group. Currently, you manually scale the ASG and you would like to define a Scaling Policy that will ensure the average number of connections to your EC2 instances is around 1000. Which Scaling Policy should you use?

একটি application একটি Application Load Balancer এবং একটি Auto Scaling Group দিয়ে deploy করা আছে। বর্তমানে আপনি ASG manually scale করেন, এবং আপনি এমন একটি Scaling Policy define করতে চান যা নিশ্চিত করবে যে আপনার EC2 instance-গুলোতে গড় connection সংখ্যা প্রায় 1000 থাকবে। কোন Scaling Policy ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

'Average ~1000 connection rakho' = ekta metric ke target value er kache rakha. Eta Target Tracking policy (PDF p. 157: 'average ASG CPU ~40% e rakho'). ASG nije alarm baniye scale out/in kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Simple scaling alarm threshold cross korle fixed unit add/remove kore; target value dhore rakhe na.
  • B. Step scaling alarm er breach er size onujayi step e add/remove kore; 'around 1000' dhore rakhar shohoj upay target tracking.
  • D. Scheduled scaling jana shomoy (ex: Friday 5 pm) e capacity bodlay; load er metric dekhe na.

Keyword: average connections around 1000 → Target Tracking

8·20U08-Q20Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q20

Instructor feedback (English)

A: this would work, but is not necessary

B: the NLB supports HTTP health checks as well as TCP and HTTPS

8·21U08-Q21Udemy quiz · sec 08 · Q21

আপনার একটি website একটি Auto Scaling Group-এর EC2 instance-গুলোতে hosted, যার সামনে একটি Application Load Balancer আছে। বর্তমানে website-টি HTTP-র মাধ্যমে serve হয়, এবং আপনাকে এটি HTTPS ব্যবহার করার জন্য configure করার দায়িত্ব দেওয়া হয়েছে। আপনি ACM-এ একটি certificate তৈরি করে Application Load Balancer-এ attach করেছেন। user-রা যাতে HTTP-র বদলে HTTPS দিয়ে website access করতে বাধ্য হয়, তার জন্য আপনি কী করতে পারেন?
