Keyword: force HTTPS on ALB → ALB HTTP → HTTPS redirect

Section 27: Networking - VPC (23)

Note: Section 27 note. Answer first, then press Check.

27·1U27-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q1

What does this CIDR 10.0.4.0/28 correspond to?

এই CIDR 10.0.4.0/28 কোন range নির্দেশ করে?

Correct: A. Why it is right

/28 mane 32 - 28 = 4 bit change hote pare, tai 2^4 = 16 ta IP (PDF p. 704). Base IP 10.0.4.0 theke shuru, tai range 10.0.4.0 - 10.0.4.15.

Shortcut: /24 er niche hole shudhu shesh octet bodlay; /28 = 16, /27 = 32, /26 = 64.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. 10.0.4.0 theke 10.0.32.0 holo hazar hazar IP; /28 e mattro 16 ta IP, third octet bodlay na.
  • C. /28 er 28 ta IP mane na; 28 holo mask bit. /28 = 2^4 = 16 IP, shesh IP .15.
  • D. Eta third octet change kora boro range (/20 er kachakachi); /28 e shudhu shesh octet er 16 ta value.

Keyword: /28 → 2^(32-28) = 16 IP → 10.0.4.0 - 10.0.4.15

Instructor feedback (English)

A: /28 means 16 IPs (=2^(32-28) = 2^4), means only the last digit can change.

27·2U27-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q2

You have a corporate network of size 10.0.0.0/8 and a satellite office of size 192.168.0.0/16. Which CIDR is acceptable for your AWS VPC if you plan on connecting your networks later on?

আপনার 10.0.0.0/8 size-এর একটি corporate network এবং 192.168.0.0/16 size-এর একটি satellite office আছে। পরে আপনার network-গুলো connect করার পরিকল্পনা থাকলে আপনার AWS VPC-র জন্য কোন CIDR গ্রহণযোগ্য?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Pore network jurte chaile VPC CIDR onno network er sathe overlap korbe na (PDF p. 708). 10.0.0.0/8 ar 192.168.0.0/16 baad dile baki private range 172.16.0.0/12.

Kintu VPC er ek CIDR er max size /16, tai /12 cholbe na. Uttor 172.16.0.0/16.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 172.16.0.0/12 overlap kore na thik, kintu VPC CIDR /16 er cheye boro hote pare na (max /16).
  • C. 10.0.16.0/16 corporate 10.0.0.0/8 er bhitore pore, overlap korbe.
  • D. 192.168.4.0/18 satellite office er 192.168.0.0/16 er bhitore, overlap korbe.

Keyword: connect later (no overlap) + VPC max /16 → 172.16.0.0/16

Instructor feedback (English)

B: CIDR not should overlap, and the max CIDR size in AWS is /16.

27·3U27-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q3

You plan on creating a subnet and want it to have at least capacity for 28 EC2 instances. What's the minimum size you need to have for your subnet?

আপনি একটি subnet তৈরি করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন এবং চান এতে কমপক্ষে 28টি EC2 instance-এর capacity থাকুক। আপনার subnet-এর ন্যূনতম size কত হতে হবে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS proti subnet e 5 ta IP reserve kore (first 4 + last 1), egulo EC2 ke dewa jay na (PDF p. 711).

  • /27 = 32 - 5 = 27 usable < 28, kom.
  • /26 = 64 - 5 = 59 usable ≥ 28, cholbe.

Slide er exam tip ei same hisab (29 IP er jonno /26).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. /28 = 16 IP, 5 reserved, mattro 11 usable; 28 instance er jonno onek kom.
  • B. /27 = 32 IP, 5 reserved, 27 usable; 28 ta instance dhorbe na.
  • D. /25 = 128 IP, kaj korbe kintu question minimum size chay; /26 ei jothesto.

Keyword: at least 28 instances + minimum size + 5 reserved IP → /26

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Too small, it's 16 IPs, 5 of them already reserved by AWS.

B: Too small, it's 32 IPs, 5 of them already reserved by AWS.

C: Perfect size, 64 IPs.

D: Too big, it's 128 IPs.

27·4U27-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q4

Security Groups operate at the ................. level while NACLs operate at the ................. level.

Security Group ................. level-এ কাজ করে, আর NACL ................. level-এ কাজ করে।

Correct: A. Why it is right

Security Group kaj kore instance (ENI) level e, ar NACL kaj kore subnet level e (PDF p. 731).

NACL subnet er sob instance e automatic lage; SG shudhu je instance e attach kora hoy tate lage.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Ulta bola hoyeche: NACL subnet level, SG instance level.

Keyword: SG vs NACL level → SG = instance, NACL = subnet

27·5U27-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q5

You have attached an Internet Gateway to your VPC, but your EC2 instances still don't have access to the internet. What is NOT a possible issue?

আপনি আপনার VPC-তে একটি Internet Gateway attach করেছেন, কিন্তু আপনার EC2 instance-গুলোর এখনও internet-এ access নেই। কোনটি সম্ভাব্য সমস্যা নয় (NOT a possible issue)?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Security Group stateful: instance theke outbound request gele tar return traffic automatic allowed, inbound rule lage na (PDF p. 731). Tai "SG inbound allow kore na" instance er internet access atkabe na, eta NOT possible issue.

Baki tin ta (route nai, public IP nai, NACL block) shotti karon hote pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. IGW attach korlei hoy na, route table e 0.0.0.0/0 → IGW route lage (PDF p. 712); route na thakle internet nai, tai eta possible issue.
  • B. Public IP (ba EIP) chara instance internet e reachable na, eta possible issue.
  • D. NACL stateless; outbound deny korle traffic ber-i hobe na, tai eta possible issue.

Keyword: NOT a possible issue + SG stateful → SG inbound

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Security groups are stateful and if traffic can go out, then it can go back in.

27·6U27-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q6

You would like to provide Internet access to your EC2 instances in private subnets with IPv4 while making sure this solution requires the least amount of administration and scales seamlessly. What should you use?

আপনি IPv4 দিয়ে private subnet-এর আপনার EC2 instance-গুলোকে Internet access দিতে চান, এবং নিশ্চিত করতে চান যে এই solution-এ সবচেয়ে কম administration লাগে এবং seamlessly scale করে। আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

NAT Gateway AWS-managed: high availability, beshi bandwidth (5 Gbps theke 100 Gbps porjonto auto scale), no administration, SG manage korte hoy na (PDF p. 719). Private subnet er IPv4 instance ke internet dey.

"Least administration" + "scales seamlessly" = NAT Gateway.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. NAT instance kaj korbe, kintu nijer EC2: patch, SG, failover script tomake manage korte hoy, bandwidth instance type e atka; auto scale hoy na.
  • B. Egress-only Internet Gateway shudhu IPv6 er jonno; question e IPv4.

Keyword: IPv4 + least administration + scales seamlessly → NAT Gateway

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This means creating a NAT EC2 Instance and although this would work, it would require some integration and would not scale seamlessly.

B: This is for IPv6 only.

27·7U27-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q7

VPC Peering has been enabled between VPC A and VPC B, and the route tables have been updated for VPC A. But, the EC2 instances cannot communicate. What is the likely issue?

VPC A ও VPC B-র মধ্যে VPC Peering enable করা হয়েছে, এবং VPC A-র route table update করা হয়েছে। কিন্তু EC2 instance-গুলো communicate করতে পারছে না। সম্ভাব্য সমস্যা কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Peering enable korar por duita VPC er subnet er route table-i update korte hoy (PDF p. 732). Shudhu VPC A update hoyeche, tai VPC B theke return traffic er route nai.

Fix: VPC B er route table e VPC A er CIDR → peering connection route jog koro.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. NACL problem hote pare kintu question e clue holo shudhu VPC A er route update hoyeche; likely issue VPC B route.
  • C. SG o block korte pare, kintu question sposhto bole ekdik er route table update baki.
  • D. DNS resolution shudhu DNS name resolve er jonno; IP diye communication er route problem solve kore na.

Keyword: route tables updated for VPC A (shudhu ekdik) → VPC B route table

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Route tables must be updated in both VPCs that are peered.

27·8U27-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q8

You have set up a Direct Connect connection between your corporate data center and your VPC A in your AWS account. You need to access VPC B in another AWS region from your corporate datacenter as well. What should you do?

আপনি আপনার corporate data center ও আপনার AWS account-এর VPC A-র মধ্যে একটি Direct Connect connection set up করেছেন। আপনার corporate datacenter থেকে আরেকটি AWS region-এর VPC B-ও access করতে হবে। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Ek Direct Connect connection diye onek Region er VPC te jete Direct Connect Gateway lage (PDF p. 752). VPC B onno Region e, tai DX Gateway er sathe duita VPC er private VIF jura hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. VPC Peering VPC-VPC er jonno; on-prem theke VPC A hoye VPC B te jawa jay na (peering transitive na, edge-to-edge routing nai).
  • B. Customer Gateway Site-to-Site VPN er on-prem side; DX diye onno Region e jaoar solution na.
  • D. NAT Gateway private subnet er outbound internet er jonno; on-prem → onno Region VPC te kaj nai.

Keyword: Direct Connect + VPC in another region → Direct Connect Gateway

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You can't create a VPC Peering connection between VPC and on-premises corporate data center. Also, the VPC Peering connection is not transitive.

C: This is the main use case of Direct Connect Gateways.

27·9U27-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q9

When using VPC Endpoints, what are the only two AWS services that have a Gateway Endpoint available?

VPC Endpoint ব্যবহার করার সময়, কোন দুটি AWS service-এর শুধু Gateway Endpoint পাওয়া যায়?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Gateway Endpoint shudhu S3 ar DynamoDB er jonno, route table e target hisebe boshe, free (PDF p. 737). Baki prai sob service Interface Endpoint (PrivateLink, ENI) diye.

Note: 2024 theke DynamoDB er Interface endpoint o ache, kintu Gateway endpoint ekhono shudhu S3 + DynamoDB.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SQS er Gateway endpoint nai; SQS Interface endpoint (PrivateLink) use kore.
  • B. SQS er Gateway endpoint nai, Interface endpoint diye hoy.

Keyword: Gateway Endpoint only two → S3 + DynamoDB

Instructor feedback (English)

C: These two services have a VPC Gateway Endpoint (remember it), all the other ones have an Interface endpoint (powered by Private Link - means a private IP).

27·10U27-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q10

AWS reserves 5 IP addresses each time you create a new subnet in a VPC. When you create a subnet with CIDR 10.0.0.0/24, the following IP addresses are reserved, EXCEPT ....................

VPC-তে প্রতিবার একটি নতুন subnet তৈরি করলে AWS 5টি IP address reserve করে। CIDR 10.0.0.0/24 সহ একটি subnet তৈরি করলে নিচের IP address-গুলো reserve করা হয়, শুধু .................... ছাড়া (EXCEPT)

Correct: D. Why it is right

10.0.0.0/24 e reserved: .0 network address, .1 VPC router, .2 Amazon DNS, .3 future use, .255 broadcast (PDF p. 711). Tai 10.0.0.4 reserved na, prothom usable IP.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 10.0.0.1 VPC router er jonno reserved.
  • B. 10.0.0.2 Amazon-provided DNS er jonno reserved.
  • C. 10.0.0.3 AWS future use er jonno reserved.

Keyword: reserved EXCEPT → first 4 (.0-.3) + last (.255), tai .4

27·11U27-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q11

You have 3 VPCs A, B, and C. You want to establish a VPC Peering connection between all the 3 VPCs. What should you do?

আপনার 3টি VPC আছে: A, B এবং C। আপনি 3টি VPC-র সবগুলোর মধ্যে VPC Peering connection স্থাপন করতে চান। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

VPC Peering transitive na (PDF p. 732). A-B ar B-C thakleo A-C kotha bolbe na. Tai tin VPC sob jurte 3 ta peering lage: A-B, A-C, B-C (full mesh).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul premise: VPC Peering transitive support kore na, tai 2 ta connection e A ar C kotha bolbe na.

Keyword: all 3 VPCs + peering not transitive → 3 connections

27·12U27-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q12

How can you capture information about IP traffic inside your VPCs?

আপনার VPC-গুলোর ভেতরের IP traffic সম্পর্কে তথ্য কীভাবে capture করতে পারেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

VPC Flow Logs interface (VPC / subnet / ENI) er IP traffic er information capture kore: src/dst IP, port, ACCEPT/REJECT (PDF p. 740, 742). S3, CloudWatch Logs ba Kinesis Data Firehose e pathano jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Traffic Mirroring ENI theke puro packet copy kore security appliance e pathay (content inspection); traffic er info/log er jonno Flow Logs.
  • C. "CloudWatch Traffic Logs" bole kono feature nai.

Keyword: capture information about IP traffic → VPC Flow Logs

Instructor feedback (English)

A: VPC Flow Logs is a VPC feature that enables you to capture information about the IP traffic going to and from network interfaces in your VPC.

B: VPC Traffic Mirroring is a VPC feature that you can use to copy network traffic from an Elastic Network Interface (ENI). Use cases include content inspection, threat monitoring, and troubleshooting.

C: There's no such feature.

27·13U27-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q13

If you want a 500 Mbps Direct Connect connection between your corporate datacenter to AWS, you would choose a .................. connection.

আপনার corporate datacenter থেকে AWS-এ একটি 500 Mbps Direct Connect connection চাইলে, আপনি একটি .................. connection বেছে নেবেন।

Correct: B. Why it is right

Slide onujayi Dedicated connection 1 Gbps theke 400 Gbps, ar Hosted connection 50 Mbps theke 25 Gbps (PDF p. 753). 500 Mbps 1 Gbps er kom, tai Hosted.

Hosted connection Direct Connect Partner diye request hoy, capacity on demand barano-komano jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Dedicated connection minimum 1 Gbps (physical port), tai 500 Mbps er jonno na.

Keyword: 500 Mbps (1 Gbps er kom) → Hosted

Instructor feedback (English)

A: A Dedicated Direct Connect connection supports 1Gbps and 10Gbps.

B: Hosted Direct Connect connection supports 50Mbps, 500Mbps, up to 10Gbps.

27·14U27-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q14

When you set up an AWS Site-to-Site VPN connection between your corporate on-premises datacenter and VPCs in AWS Cloud, what are the two major components you want to configure for this connection?

আপনার corporate on-premises datacenter ও AWS Cloud-এর VPC-গুলোর মধ্যে একটি AWS Site-to-Site VPN connection set up করার সময়, এই connection-এর জন্য কোন দুটি প্রধান component configure করতে হবে?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Site-to-Site VPN er duita part (PDF p. 747):

  • Virtual Private Gateway (VGW): AWS side er VPN concentrator, VPC te attach.
  • Customer Gateway (CGW): on-prem side er device ba software.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. NAT Gateway private subnet er outbound internet er jonno; VPN er AWS side VGW.
  • B. Internet Gateway VPC er internet access; VPN er AWS side component na.
  • C. VGW thik, kintu on-prem side e Internet Gateway na, Customer Gateway lage.

Keyword: Site-to-Site VPN two components → VGW (AWS) + CGW (on-prem)

27·15U27-Q15Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q15

Your company has several on-premises sites across the USA. These sites are currently linked using private connections, but your private connections provider has been recently quite unstable, making your IT architecture partially offline. You would like to create a backup connection that will use the public Internet to link your on-premises sites, that you can failover in case of issues with your provider. What do you recommend?

আপনার company-র USA জুড়ে কয়েকটি on-premises site আছে। এই site-গুলো বর্তমানে private connection দিয়ে linked, কিন্তু আপনার private connection provider সম্প্রতি বেশ অস্থির, ফলে আপনার IT architecture আংশিকভাবে offline হয়ে যাচ্ছে। আপনি এমন একটি backup connection তৈরি করতে চান, যেটি আপনার on-premises site-গুলো link করতে public Internet ব্যবহার করবে, এবং provider-এর সমস্যা হলে যেটিতে failover করতে পারবেন। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

AWS VPN CloudHub onek site ke VPN diye hub-and-spoke model e jure, low-cost, primary ba secondary (backup) connectivity er jonno (PDF p. 749). VPN tai public Internet diye jay.

Ek VGW e onek VPN connection, dynamic routing.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. VPC Peering duita VPC er moddhe; on-prem site gulo jure na.
  • C. Direct Connect private dedicated line, dami ar internet use kore na; question public Internet backup chay.
  • D. PrivateLink VPC theke service privately expose kore; on-prem site-to-site link na.

Keyword: multiple on-premises sites + backup over public Internet → VPN CloudHub

Instructor feedback (English)

A: VPC Peering connection is a networking connection between two VPCs that enables you to route traffic between them using private IPv4/IPv6 addresses. It can't be used with on-premises networks.

B: AWS VPN CloudHub allows you to securely communicate with multiple sites using AWS VPN. It operates on a simple hub-and-spoke model that you can use with or without a VPC.

C: AWS Direct Connect allows you to establish a dedicated network connection between AWS and your on-premises. This would work, but it will be very expensive. This is a private connection and the question states that the backup connection can use the public Internet.

27·16U27-Q16Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q16

You need to set up a dedicated connection between your on-premises corporate datacenter and AWS Cloud. This connection must be private, consistent, and traffic must not travel through the Internet. Which AWS service should you use?

আপনার on-premises corporate datacenter ও AWS Cloud-এর মধ্যে একটি dedicated connection set up করতে হবে। এই connection-কে private ও consistent হতে হবে, এবং traffic Internet-এর মধ্য দিয়ে যাবে না। আপনার কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Direct Connect on-prem theke AWS e dedicated private connection dey, traffic Internet e jay na, network experience consistent (PDF p. 750).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Site-to-Site VPN encrypted kintu public Internet diye jay, consistent na.
  • B. PrivateLink VPC theke AWS/partner service e private access; on-prem DC theke dedicated line na.
  • D. EventBridge event bus service, network connection na.

Keyword: dedicated, private, consistent, not through Internet → Direct Connect

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This would not be suitable, as traffic travels through the public Internet.

27·17U27-Q17Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q17

Using a Direct Connect connection, you can access both public and private AWS resources.

একটি Direct Connect connection ব্যবহার করে আপনি public ও private দুই ধরনের AWS resource-ই access করতে পারেন।

Correct: A. Why it is right

Thik. Ek DX connection e public virtual interface diye public AWS resource (S3, Glacier) ar private virtual interface diye VPC er private resource (EC2) access kora jay (PDF p. 750-751).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Bhul: DX same connection e public VIF (S3) ar private VIF (EC2 in VPC) duita-i support kore.

Keyword: DX public and private → public VIF + private VIF → True

27·18U27-Q18Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q18⚠ outdated

You want to scale up an AWS Site-to-Site VPN connection throughput, established between your on-premises data and AWS Cloud, beyond a single IPsec tunnel's maximum limit of 1.25 Gbps. What should you do?

আপনার on-premises data ও AWS Cloud-এর মধ্যে স্থাপিত একটি AWS Site-to-Site VPN connection-এর throughput আপনি একটি single IPsec tunnel-এর সর্বোচ্চ limit 1.25 Gbps-এর বেশি scale up করতে চান। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

⚠ Outdated: Ekhon Site-to-Site VPN e Large Bandwidth Tunnel (5 Gbps per tunnel) option ache, kintu sheta shudhu Transit Gateway/Cloud WAN attachment e; standard tunnel ekhono 1.25 Gbps. Tai course/exam er answer Transit Gateway-i thik. source

Correct: C. Why it is right

Ek VPN tunnel 1.25 Gbps. Transit Gateway e ECMP (Equal-cost multi-path) diye onek VPN tunnel/connection eksathe use kore bandwidth barano jay: 1 VPN = 2.5 Gbps, 2 = 5 Gbps, 3 = 7.5 Gbps (PDF p. 759-760). VGW te ECMP nai.

Ajker update: Large Bandwidth Tunnel (5 Gbps per tunnel) ache, kintu shudhu Transit Gateway/Cloud WAN attachment e; standard tunnel ekhono 1.25 Gbps, tai uttor Transit Gateway-i.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. VGW ECMP support kore na; VGW te VPN 1x = 1.25 Gbps, duita VGW o ek VPC te ekshathe attach hoy na.
  • B. Direct Connect Gateway DX ke onek Region er VPC te jure; VPN throughput barano tar kaj na.

Keyword: VPN throughput beyond 1.25 Gbps → Transit Gateway + ECMP

27·19U27-Q19Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q19

You have a VPC in your AWS account that runs in a dual-stack mode. You are continuously trying to launch an EC2 instance, but it fails. After further investigation, you have found that you are no longer have IPv4 addresses available. What should you do?

আপনার AWS account-এ একটি VPC আছে, যেটি dual-stack mode-এ চলে। আপনি বারবার একটি EC2 instance launch করার চেষ্টা করছেন, কিন্তু এটি fail করছে। আরও অনুসন্ধান করে আপনি দেখলেন যে আপনার কাছে আর IPv4 address available নেই। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

VPC te IPv4 disable kora jay na; dual-stack e-o EC2 ke ekta private IPv4 lagbe (PDF p. 764-765). Launch fail mane IPv6 er problem na (IPv6 space onek boro), IPv4 sesh.

Solution: VPC/subnet e notun IPv4 CIDR jog koro.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. IPv6-only mode e VPC convert kora jay na; IPv4 disable hoy na.
  • B. IPv4-only korle-o IPv4 address barbe na; problem IPv4 er abhab.

Keyword: no longer have IPv4 addresses + IPv4 can't be disabled → add IPv4 CIDR

27·20U27-Q20Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q20

A web application backend is hosted on EC2 instances in private subnets fronted by an Application Load Balancer in public subnets. There is a requirement to give some of the developers access to the backend EC2 instances but without exposing the backend EC2 instances to the Internet. You have created a bastion host EC2 instance in the public subnet and configured the backend EC2 instances Security Group to allow traffic from the bastion host. Which of the following is the best configuration for bastion host Security Group to make it secure?

একটি web application backend private subnet-এর EC2 instance-এ host করা, যার সামনে public subnet-এ একটি Application Load Balancer আছে। কিছু developer-কে backend EC2 instance-গুলোতে access দেওয়ার একটি requirement আছে, কিন্তু backend EC2 instance-গুলো Internet-এ expose না করে। আপনি public subnet-এ একটি bastion host EC2 instance তৈরি করেছেন এবং bastion host থেকে traffic allow করতে backend EC2 instance-গুলোর Security Group configure করেছেন। bastion host Security Group-কে secure করতে নিচের কোনটি সবচেয়ে ভালো (best) configuration?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Bastion host public subnet e, internet theke SSH ashe. Tai bastion SG te port 22 allow koro shudhu company er public CIDR theke (PDF p. 715).

Internet theke ashle source holo company er public IP, private CIDR na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Port 80 HTTP; bastion e SSH (22) diye login hoy.
  • C. Private CIDR internet e dekha jay na; internet theke ashle source holo company er public IP, tai rule match korbe na.
  • D. Port 80 bhul port, ar private CIDR o bhul source.

Keyword: bastion + secure → port 22 from company public CIDR

27·21U27-Q21Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q21

A company has set up a Direct Connect connection between their corporate data center to AWS. There is a requirement to prepare a cost-effective secure backup connection in case there are issues with this Direct Connect connection. What is the most cost effective and secure solution you recommend?

একটি company তাদের corporate data center থেকে AWS-এ একটি Direct Connect connection set up করেছে। এই Direct Connect connection-এ সমস্যা হলে তার জন্য একটি cost-effective secure backup connection প্রস্তুত করার requirement আছে। আপনি সবচেয়ে cost effective ও secure কোন solution recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

DX fail hole backup duibhabe: arekta DX (expensive) ba Site-to-Site VPN (PDF p. 756). VPN IPsec encrypted (secure) ar shosta, tai "most cost effective + secure" = Site-to-Site VPN backup.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Arekta DX resiliency barabe kintu dami, cost-effective na.
  • B. Onno Region e DX-o dami, ar question er cost-effective chahida mane na.

Keyword: cost-effective secure backup for DX → Site-to-Site VPN

27·22U27-Q22Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q22

Which AWS service allows you to protect and control traffic in your VPC from layer 3 to layer 7?

কোন AWS service আপনাকে আপনার VPC-র traffic layer 3 থেকে layer 7 পর্যন্ত protect ও control করতে দেয়?

Correct: A. Why it is right

AWS Network Firewall puro VPC protect kore Layer 3 theke Layer 7 porjonto, je kono direction e (VPC-VPC, internet in/out, DX/VPN) (PDF p. 776). Bhitore Gateway Load Balancer use kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. GuardDuty logs (VPC Flow Logs, DNS, CloudTrail) analyze kore threat detect kore; traffic control/block kore na.
  • C. Inspector EC2/ECR/Lambda er vulnerability scan kore; network traffic control kore na.
  • D. Shield DDoS protection; L3-L7 VPC firewall rule dey na.

Keyword: protect and control traffic in VPC + layer 3 to 7 → AWS Network Firewall

27·23U27-Q23Udemy quiz · sec 27 · Q23

A web application hosted on a fleet of EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group. You are exposing this application through an Application Load Balancer. Both the EC2 instances and the ALB are deployed on a VPC with the following CIDR 192.168.0.0/18. How do you configure the EC2 instances' security group to ensure only the ALB can access them on port 80?

একটি web application একটি Auto Scaling Group দিয়ে managed EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet-এ host করা। আপনি এই application একটি Application Load Balancer-এর মাধ্যমে expose করছেন। EC2 instance ও ALB দুটোই 192.168.0.0/18 CIDR সহ একটি VPC-তে deploy করা। শুধু ALB-ই যাতে port 80-এ EC2 instance-গুলো access করতে পারে, তা নিশ্চিত করতে EC2 instance-গুলোর security group কীভাবে configure করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EC2 SG er inbound rule e source hisebe ALB er Security Group reference koro (port 80). Tokhon shudhu oi SG wala ALB-i EC2 te ashte pare, IP bodlaleo kaj kore. Eta course er SG referencing er main pattern (ALB SG → EC2 SG).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 0.0.0.0/0 sobai ke port 80 e dhukte dey; shudhu ALB na.
  • B. VPC CIDR dile VPC er sob resource dhukte parbe, shudhu ALB na.
  • D. SSL certificate HTTPS encryption er jonno; ke EC2 access korbe ta control kore na.

Keyword: only the ALB can access EC2 → source = ALB's Security Group

Instructor feedback (English)

C: This is the most secure way of ensuring only the ALB can access the EC2 instances. Referencing by security groups in rules is an extremely powerful rule and many questions at the exam rely on it. Make sure you fully master the concepts behind it!

Section 09: AWS Fundamentals RDS + Aurora + ElastiCache (25)

Note: Section 09 note. Answer first, then press Check.

9·1U09-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q1

Amazon RDS supports the following databases, EXCEPT:

Amazon RDS নিচের database-গুলো support করে, শুধু এটি ছাড়া (EXCEPT):

Correct: A. Why it is right

RDS shudhu relational (SQL) database er managed service. Engine list: PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB, Oracle, Microsoft SQL Server, IBM DB2 ar Aurora (PDF p. 162).

MongoDB holo NoSQL document database, RDS engine na. Tai EXCEPT er uttor MongoDB.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. MySQL RDS er open source engine, support kore. EXCEPT prosno, tai eta uttor na.
  • C. MariaDB (MySQL er fork) RDS e ache, tai eta EXCEPT er uttor na.
  • D. Microsoft SQL Server RDS er commercial engine, support kore.

Keyword: RDS supports ... EXCEPT → NoSQL MongoDB

Instructor feedback (English)

A: RDS supports MySQL, PostgreSQL, MariaDB, Oracle, MS SQL Server, and Amazon Aurora.

9·2U09-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q2

You're planning for a new solution that requires a MySQL database that must be available even in case of a disaster in one of the Availability Zones. What should you use?

আপনি একটি নতুন solution-এর পরিকল্পনা করছেন, যার জন্য একটি MySQL database দরকার যা Availability Zone-গুলোর একটিতে disaster হলেও available থাকতে হবে। আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Multi-AZ = Disaster Recovery. Master ek AZ e, standby onno AZ e, SYNC replication. Master er AZ gele automatic failover standby te, ek-i DNS name thake (PDF p. 168).

Tai ek AZ e disaster holeo MySQL available thake.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Read Replica holo read scaling er jonno; replication ASYNC, failover automatic na (manual promote lage). AZ disaster er DR er jonno Multi-AZ.
  • B. Encryption data ke secure kore, kintu AZ down hole DB available rakhe na.

Keyword: available even in disaster of one Availability Zone → Multi-AZ

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Read Replicas don't help with disaster recovery, it helps then you want to scale reads across AZs.

C: Multi-AZ helps when you plan a disaster recovery for an entire AZ going down. If you plan against an entire AWS Region going down, you should use backups and replication across AWS Regions.

9·3U09-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q3

We have an RDS database that struggles to keep up with the demand of requests from our website. Our million users mostly read news, and we don't post news very often. Which solution is NOT adapted to this problem?

আমাদের একটি RDS database আছে যা আমাদের website থেকে আসা request-এর চাহিদা সামলাতে হিমশিম খাচ্ছে। আমাদের লক্ষ user মূলত news পড়ে, এবং আমরা খুব ঘন ঘন news post করি না। কোন solution এই সমস্যার জন্য উপযুক্ত নয় (NOT adapted)?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Prosno ta NOT chay. Problem holo onek read (million user news pore, kom post).

Multi-AZ er standby read-o kore na, write-o kore na; shudhu failover er jonno boshe thake (PDF p. 168). Tai read load komay na → eta-i "not adapted".

ElastiCache (common query cache) ar Read Replica duitai read load kome.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ElastiCache frequently read data in-memory cache kore, RDS er read load komay. Tai eta kaj kore, NOT er uttor na.
  • C. Read Replica (15 porjonto) read traffic niye ney, read-heavy load er jonno thik solution. NOT er uttor na.

Keyword: mostly read + NOT adapted → Multi-AZ (standby read kore na)

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Be very careful with the way you read questions at the exam. Here, the question is asking which solution is NOT adapted to this problem. ElastiCache and RDS Read Replicas do indeed help with scaling reads.

9·4U09-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q4

You have set up read replicas on your RDS database, but users are complaining that upon updating their social media posts, they do not see their updated posts right away. What is a possible cause for this?

আপনি আপনার RDS database-এ read replica set up করেছেন, কিন্তু user-রা অভিযোগ করছে যে তাদের social media post update করার পরে তারা সঙ্গে সঙ্গে updated post দেখতে পাচ্ছে না। এর একটি সম্ভাব্য কারণ কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Read Replica te replication ASYNC. Write main DB te hoy, replica te pouchate ektu shomoy lage. Tai reads eventually consistent: user post update korar por replica theke purono post dekhte pare (PDF p. 165).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. App er bug na; eta read replica er normal behaviour (async replication er lag).
  • C. Multi-AZ standby read serve kore na; shudhu DR er jonno. Read scale korte replica-i lage, ar lag tar nature.

Keyword: Read Replica + update er por purono data dekhay → ASYNC, eventual consistency

9·5U09-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q5

Which RDS (NOT Aurora) feature when used does not require you to change the SQL connection string?

RDS-এর (Aurora নয় / NOT Aurora) কোন feature ব্যবহার করলে আপনাকে SQL connection string পরিবর্তন করতে হয় না?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Multi-AZ e app ekta-i DNS name e connect kore. Failover hole oi DNS name standby ke point kore, tai connection string bodlate hoy na (PDF p. 168).

Read Replica te protita replica er nijer endpoint; app ke connection string update korte hoy (PDF p. 165).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Protita Read Replica er nijer DNS endpoint; read load balance korte app e oigulo add korte hoy, mane connection string bodlay.

Keyword: does not require changing connection string → Multi-AZ (ek DNS name)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Multi-AZ keeps the same connection string regardless of which database is up.

B: Read Replicas add new endpoints with their own DNS name. We need to change our application to reference them individually to balance the read load.

9·6U09-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q6

Your application running on a fleet of EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group behind an Application Load Balancer. Users have to constantly log back in and you don't want to enable Sticky Sessions on your ALB as you fear it will overload some EC2 instances. What should you do?

আপনার application একটি Application Load Balancer-এর পেছনে একটি Auto Scaling Group দিয়ে manage করা EC2 instance-এর fleet-এ চলছে। user-দের বারবার আবার log in করতে হচ্ছে, এবং আপনি আপনার ALB-তে Sticky Sessions enable করতে চান না, কারণ আপনার ভয় এতে কিছু EC2 instance overload হয়ে যাবে। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Session data ElastiCache e rakho (User Session Store pattern, PDF p. 189). User je instance e-i jak, oi instance ElastiCache theke session pore, abar login lage na.

Fole app stateless hoy, sticky session lage na. ElastiCache in-memory, tai RDS er cheye onek druto.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Nijer load balancer banale operational overhead bare, ar session sharing er problem thik hoy na.
  • B. RDS e session rakha jay, kintu disk-based DB, ElastiCache er moto high performance dey na.
  • D. EBS volume default e ek EC2 te-i attach hoy (Multi-Attach er limitation ache), ar ASG er instance gulo ek volume share korte pare na. Performance-o ElastiCache er moto na.

Keyword: bar bar log back in + Sticky Sessions chai na → session ElastiCache e

Instructor feedback (English)

B: RDS could work to store session data, but wouldn't provide you with high performance as ElastiCache.

C: Storing Session Data in ElastiCache is a common pattern to ensuring different EC2 instances can retrieve your user's state if needed.

D: By default, EBS volumes can not be attached to multiple EC2 instances. You can use EBS Multi-Attach, but it has certain limitations. Also, EBS volumes wouldn't provide you with high performance as ElastiCache.

9·7U09-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q7

An analytics application is currently performing its queries against your main production RDS database. These queries run at any time of the day and slow down the RDS database which impacts your users' experience. What should you do to improve the users' experience?

একটি analytics application বর্তমানে আপনার main production RDS database-এর বিরুদ্ধে তার query চালাচ্ছে। এই query-গুলো দিনের যেকোনো সময় চলে এবং RDS database-কে slow করে দেয়, যা আপনার user-দের experience-এ প্রভাব ফেলে। user-দের experience উন্নত করতে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Analytics app er jonno Read Replica banao, analytics query shudhu replica te chalao. Production main DB er upor load thake na, user experience thik thake (PDF p. 166).

Replica shudhu SELECT (read) er jonno, analytics er jonno ekdom fit.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Multi-AZ er standby read kore na; shudhu HA / failover er jonno. Analytics query okhane chalano jay na.
  • C. Question bole query din er jekono shomoy chole, tai shudhu raat e chalano possible na.

Keyword: analytics queries production DB slow kore → Read Replica

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Read Replicas will help as your analytics application can now perform queries against it, and these queries won't impact the main production RDS database.

B: Multi-AZ is used for high availability, meaning that the standby DB instance can't be used by our applications.

C: This won't be suitable here as the question mentioned that analytics queries can be run any time of the day.

9·8U09-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q8⚠ outdated

You would like to ensure you have a replica of your database available in another AWS Region if a disaster happens to your main AWS Region. Which database do you recommend to implement this easily?

আপনি নিশ্চিত করতে চান যে আপনার main AWS Region-এ disaster হলে অন্য একটি AWS Region-এ আপনার database-এর একটি replica available থাকবে। এটি সহজে implement করতে আপনি কোন database recommend করবেন?

⚠ Outdated: Explanation e bola 'up to 5 secondary regions', kintu May 2025 theke Aurora Global Database e up to 10 secondary (read-only) Region rakha jay (course slide v49 o 10 bole). Answer (Aurora Global Database) ekhono thik. source

Correct: D. Why it is right

Aurora Global Database: 1 primary Region (read/write) ar secondary Region gulo read-only, replication < 1 second. Main Region e disaster hole secondary ke promote kora jay, RTO < 1 minute (PDF p. 178). Setup easy, DR er jonno recommended.

Ajker fact: original explanation "5 secondary Region" bole, kintu ekhon 10 porjonto secondary Region rakha jay (slide-o 10 bole); answer ek-i.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS Read Replica cross-Region kora jay, kintu eta mainly read scaling; DR er jonno manual promote, "easily" er best uttor Aurora Global Database.
  • B. Multi-AZ ek Region er moddhe onno AZ e standby rakhe. Puro Region gele kaj kore na.
  • C. Aurora Read Replica mainly cluster er moddhe read scaling. Onno Region e DR er jonno recommended holo Global Database.

Keyword: replica in another AWS Region + disaster + easily → Aurora Global Database

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Read Replicas cannot help with disaster recovery.

B: Multi-AZ won't help when a disaster happens at the AWS region level. Multi-AZ helps when a disaster happens at the AZ level.

C: Read Replicas cannot help with disaster recovery.

D: Aurora Global Databases allows you to have an Aurora Replica in another AWS Region, with up to 5 secondary regions.

9·9U09-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q9

How can you enhance the security of your ElastiCache Redis Cluster by allowing users to access your ElastiCache Redis Cluster using their IAM Identities (e.g., Users, Roles)?

user-দের তাদের IAM Identities (যেমন, Users, Roles) ব্যবহার করে আপনার ElastiCache Redis Cluster access করতে দিয়ে আপনি কীভাবে ElastiCache Redis Cluster-এর security বাড়াতে পারেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

ElastiCache Redis e IAM Authentication support kore: user/role er IAM identity diye cluster e connect (PDF p. 191). Question e IAM Identities bola, tai IAM Authentication.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Redis AUTH holo cluster create er shomoy deya password/token. Eta IAM identity diye login na.
  • C. Security Group shudhu network traffic filter kore (kon IP/SG connect korte pare). Application-level authentication ba IAM identity dey na.

Keyword: access using their IAM Identities (Users, Roles) → IAM Authentication

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Using Redis Authentication, Redis requires a password before allowing clients to run commands.

C: Security Groups helps filter traffic going to your ElastiCache Redis Cluster instances but cannot help with application-level authentication.

9·10U09-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q10⚠ outdated

Your company has a production Node.js application that is using RDS MySQL 5.6 as its database. A new application programmed in Java will perform some heavy analytics workload to create a dashboard on a regular hourly basis. What is the most cost-effective solution you can implement to minimize disruption for the main application?

আপনার company-র একটি production Node.js application আছে, যা database হিসেবে RDS MySQL 5.6 ব্যবহার করছে। Java-তে programmed একটি নতুন application নিয়মিত প্রতি ঘণ্টায় একটি dashboard তৈরি করতে কিছু heavy analytics workload চালাবে। main application-এর disruption কমাতে আপনি সবচেয়ে cost-effective কোন solution implement করতে পারেন?

⚠ Outdated: Question e 'RDS MySQL 5.6' bola ache, kintu RDS for MySQL 5.6 deprecated (2022 theke notun 5.6 instance banano jay na, auto-upgrade hoyeche). Shudhu version ta purono; answer (Read Replica te analytics) ekhono thik. source

Correct: B. Why it is right

Onno AZ e Read Replica banao ar Java analytics app shudhu replica te chalao. Main Node.js app er DB (source) te load pore na, tai disruption kom (PDF p. 166).

Cost-effective: same Region e replica (AZ alada holeo) replication traffic free (PDF p. 167).

Ajker fact: RDS for MySQL 5.6 ekhon deprecated (purono version); answer ek-i.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Multi-AZ er standby te read kora jay na, tai okhane analytics chalano jay na. Standby shudhu failover er jonno.
  • C. Replica baniye analytics source DB te chalale main app er DB te-i load pore; disruption kome na. Workload replica te chalate hobe.

Keyword: heavy analytics + minimize disruption for main app → Read Replica te analytics

9·11U09-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q11

You would like to create a disaster recovery strategy for your RDS PostgreSQL database so that in case of a regional outage the database can be quickly made available for both read and write workloads in another AWS Region. The DR database must be highly available. What do you recommend?

আপনি আপনার RDS PostgreSQL database-এর জন্য একটি disaster recovery strategy তৈরি করতে চান, যাতে regional outage হলে database-টিকে অন্য একটি AWS Region-এ read এবং write দুই ধরনের workload-এর জন্যই দ্রুত available করা যায়। DR database-টিকে highly available হতে হবে। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Region outage er DR: onno Region e Read Replica banao. Outage hole replica ke promote korle read + write duita-i ney.

DR DB-o highly available hote hobe, tai oi replica tei Multi-AZ on koro. Read Replica-ke Multi-AZ hisebe setup kora jay (PDF p. 168, instructor: common exam question).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Same Region e replica Region outage e kono kaj e ashe na; main DB er Multi-AZ-o ek Region er moddhei.
  • C. Replica same Region e thakle Region outage e oita-o down hobe; onno Region e lagbe.
  • D. RDS e "Multi-Region option" bole kono feature nai.

Keyword: regional outage + read/write in another Region + DR highly available → cross-Region Read Replica + tar upor Multi-AZ

9·12U09-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q12

You have migrated the MySQL database from on-premises to RDS. You have a lot of applications and developers interacting with your database. Each developer has an IAM user in the company's AWS account. What is a suitable approach to give access to developers to the MySQL RDS DB instance instead of creating a DB user for each one?

আপনি MySQL database on-premises থেকে RDS-এ migrate করেছেন। আপনার database-এর সাথে অনেক application এবং developer কাজ করে। company-র AWS account-এ প্রত্যেক developer-এর একটি IAM user আছে। প্রত্যেকের জন্য একটি DB user তৈরি না করে developer-দের MySQL RDS DB instance-এ access দেওয়ার উপযুক্ত পদ্ধতি কোনটি?

Correct: C. Why it is right

IAM Database Authentication on koro: developer ra nijer IAM identity diye token niye MySQL RDS e connect kore, protiti jon er alada DB user banate hoy na. Sob access IAM e manage hoy (PDF p. 185).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Default e IAM user er RDS DB te login access thake na; IAM DB authentication on korte hoy.
  • B. Cognito holo app er end-user (mobile/web) login er jonno, developer der RDS DB access er jonno na.

Keyword: developer er IAM user diye DB access, DB user banabo na → IAM Database Authentication

9·13U09-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q13

Which of the following statement is true regarding replication in both RDS Read Replicas and Multi-AZ?

RDS Read Replicas এবং Multi-AZ দুটোতেই replication সম্পর্কে নিচের কোন statement সত্য?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Read Replica = ASYNC (eventually consistent, PDF p. 165). Multi-AZ = SYNC: master e write standby te-o lekha hole tobei accept (PDF p. 168).

Mone rakho: reads scale → async; DR standby → sync.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Multi-AZ async na; standby te sync replication hoy jate failover e data na harai.
  • C. Read Replica sync na; async, tai replica te purono data thakte pare.
  • D. Duto-i ulta: replica async, Multi-AZ sync.

Keyword: Read Replica ASYNC, Multi-AZ SYNC

9·14U09-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q14

How do you encrypt an unencrypted RDS DB instance?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Existing unencrypted RDS DB ke shorashori encrypt kora jay na; at-rest encryption launch time e-i define korte hoy (PDF p. 185).

  • Unencrypted DB er snapshot nao
  • Snapshot copy koro, "Enable encryption" (KMS) tick dao
  • Encrypted snapshot theke restore → notun encrypted DB, purono ta terminate

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Console e existing DB er jonno "Encrypt using KMS" action nai; encryption launch er shomoy-i thik korte hoy.
  • B. DB stop korleo encryption on kora jay na; snapshot → encrypted copy → restore-i rasta.

Keyword: encrypt an unencrypted RDS → snapshot → copy with encryption → restore

9·15U09-Q15Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q15

For your RDS database, you can have up to ............ Read Replicas.

Correct: B. Why it is right

Course: reads scale korte RDS e 15 porjonto Read Replica (PDF p. 165). Location same AZ, cross AZ ba cross Region hote pare. (Docs: MySQL, MariaDB, PostgreSQL e 15.)

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 5 kom; course bole RDS e 15 ta porjonto Read Replica.
  • C. 7 kono limit na; course er number 15.

Keyword: RDS Read Replicas up to → 15

9·16U09-Q16Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q16

Which RDS database technology does NOT support IAM Database Authentication?

Correct: A. Why it is right

IAM Database Authentication RDS er sob engine e kaj kore na: course/exam e MySQL ar PostgreSQL (docs e ekhon MariaDB-o, ar Aurora MySQL/PostgreSQL). Oracle e IAM DB auth nai.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. PostgreSQL IAM DB authentication support kore, tai NOT er uttor na.
  • C. MySQL IAM DB authentication support kore (U09-Q12 er moto), tai NOT er uttor na.

Keyword: NOT support IAM DB Authentication → Oracle

9·17U09-Q17Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q17

You have an un-encrypted RDS DB instance and you want to create Read Replicas. Can you configure the RDS Read Replicas to be encrypted?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Master unencrypted hole tar Read Replica encrypt kora jay na (PDF p. 185). Master encrypted hole replica-o encrypted hoy.

Encrypted replica chaile age master ke encrypt koro (snapshot → encrypted copy → restore), tarpor replica banao.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Unencrypted source theke encrypted Read Replica banano jay na; replica master er encryption state follow kore.

Keyword: un-encrypted master → replica encrypted? → No

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You can not create encrypted Read Replicas from an unencrypted RDS DB instance.

9·18U09-Q18Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q18

An application running in production is using an Aurora Cluster as its database. Your development team would like to run a version of the application in a scaled-down application with the ability to perform some heavy workload on a need-basis. Most of the time, the application will be unused. Your CIO has tasked you with helping the team to achieve this while minimizing costs. What do you suggest?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Aurora Serverless: actual usage onujayi nije DB instantiate ar auto-scale kore, capacity planning lage na, pay per second. Infrequent, intermittent ba unpredictable workload er jonno best (PDF p. 177).

Ekhane beshirbhag shomoy unused, majhe majhe heavy load, cost minimum → Serverless.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Global Database onno Region e DR/global read er jonno; cost barabe, unused shomoy cost komay na.
  • B. Normal RDS provisioned instance; unused thakleo instance er bill chole, nije usage onujayi scale kore na.
  • D. EC2 te Aurora chalano jay na (Aurora AWS er managed proprietary engine). Script maintain kora operational overhead-o.

Keyword: most of the time unused + heavy on need-basis + minimize cost → Aurora Serverless

9·19U09-Q19Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q19

How many Aurora Read Replicas can you have in a single Aurora DB Cluster?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Ek Aurora DB cluster e master er sathe 15 porjonto Aurora Read Replica; jekono replica failover e master hote pare (PDF p. 172).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 5 kom; Aurora cluster e 15 replica porjonto rakha jay.
  • B. 10 kom; Aurora replica limit 15.

Keyword: Aurora Read Replicas in single cluster → 15

9·20U09-Q20Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q20

Amazon Aurora supports both .......................... databases.

Correct: B. Why it is right

Aurora AWS er proprietary DB, kintu PostgreSQL ar MySQL compatible: oi driver diye-i kaj kore (PDF p. 171).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. MariaDB compatible Aurora nai; shudhu MySQL ar PostgreSQL.
  • C. Oracle ar MariaDB kono-tai Aurora edition na. Oracle RDS for Oracle e chole.
  • D. Oracle ba SQL Server Aurora compatible na. (SQL Server app er jonno Babelfish Aurora PostgreSQL e T-SQL bujhay, kintu eta alada.)

Keyword: Aurora compatible → MySQL ar PostgreSQL

9·21U09-Q21Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q21

You work as a Solutions Architect for a gaming company. One of the games mandates that players are ranked in real-time based on their score. Your boss asked you to design then implement an effective and highly available solution to create a gaming leaderboard. What should you use?

Correct: D. Why it is right

ElastiCache for Redis Sorted Sets: uniqueness ar element ordering duitai guarantee kore. Notun score add holei real-time e rank thik jaygay boshe; app e ranking logic likhte hoy na (PDF p. 193).

Redis Multi-AZ auto-failover dey, tai highly available-o.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS MySQL e proti muhurte ranking query computationally heavy ar slow; real-time leaderboard er jonno best na.
  • B. Aurora relational DB; real-time ranking er built-in data structure nai, query bhari.
  • C. Memcached e Sorted Sets nai, HA-o nai (replication nai).

Keyword: players ranked in real-time + leaderboard → Redis Sorted Sets

9·22U09-Q22Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q22

You need full customization of an Oracle Database on AWS. You would like to benefit from using the AWS services. What do you recommend? 

Correct: B. Why it is right

RDS Custom for Oracle: managed RDS er subidha (automated setup, operation, scaling) thake, sathe underlying OS ar database e full admin access (SSH / SSM), nijer moto customize kora jay (PDF p. 170).

Full customization + AWS managed service duitai chai → RDS Custom. (Note: AWS RDS Custom for Oracle er end of support 31 March 2027 announce korechhe.)

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Normal RDS for Oracle e OS/DB te admin access ba SSH nai, tai full customization hoy na.
  • C. EC2 te Oracle e full control pao, kintu sob managing nijeke korte hoy; AWS managed feature er subidha pao na.

Keyword: full customization of Oracle + benefit from AWS services → RDS Custom for Oracle

9·23U09-Q23Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q23

You need to store long-term backups for your Aurora database for disaster recovery and audit purposes. What do you recommend?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Automated backup max 35 din rakhe, tarpor expire hoy. Manual (On Demand) DB Snapshot user trigger kore ar joto din icche rakha jay (PDF p. 182).

Long-term DR ar audit er jonno → on-demand backup.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Automated backup er retention 1-35 din; long-term rakha jay na.
  • C. Cloning test/staging er jonno notun cluster banay (copy-on-write); eta backup strategy na.

Keyword: long-term backup, audit → On Demand (manual) snapshot

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The maximum retention is only 35 days

9·24U09-Q24Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q24

Your development team would like to perform a suite of read and write tests against your production Aurora database because they need access to production data as soon as possible. What do you advise?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Aurora Cloning: existing cluster theke notun cluster, copy-on-write protocol. Shuru te ek-i volume share kore, update hole tokhon alada copy hoy. Snapshot-restore er cheye druto ar cost-effective, production e prabhab nai (PDF p. 184).

Dev team read + write test korte pare, production data "as soon as possible" pay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Read Replica te write kora jay na, tai read+write test hobe na.
  • B. Production e shorashori test korle customer der upor impact porbe.
  • C. Snapshot nie restore kaj kore, kintu Cloning er cheye dhire.

Keyword: read and write tests on production data as soon as possible → Aurora Cloning

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This won't work for writes

B: This will impact your customers

C: This is slower than Aurora Cloning

9·25U09-Q25Udemy quiz · sec 09 · Q25

You have 100 EC2 instances connected to your RDS database and you see that upon a maintenance of the database, all your applications take a lot of time to reconnect to RDS, due to poor application logic. How do you improve this? 

Correct: D. Why it is right

RDS Proxy: app gulo proxy te connect kore, proxy DB connection pool kore. Failover/maintenance proxy handle kore, tai failover time 66% porjonto kome, app ke nije reconnect korte hoy na (PDF p. 186).

Beshirbhag app e code change lage na, shudhu proxy endpoint use koro.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 100 ta app fix kora onek kaj ar shomoy; RDS Proxy code change chara problem thik kore.
  • B. Multi-AZ off korle availability kome; reconnect problem thik hoy na.
  • C. Multi-AZ failover dey, kintu app er reconnect logic kharap hole tobu-o reconnect e shomoy lage.

Keyword: maintenance er por reconnect e onek shomoy, poor application logic → RDS Proxy

Instructor feedback (English)

D: This reduces the failover time by up to 66% and keeps connection actives for your applications

Section 10: Route 53 (7)

Note: Section 10 note. Answer first, then press Check.

10·1U10-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q1

You have purchased mycoolcompany.com on Amazon Route 53 Registrar and would like the domain to point to your Elastic Load Balancer my-elb-1234567890.us-west-2.elb.amazonaws.com. Which Route 53 Record type must you use here?

আপনি Amazon Route 53 Registrar-এ mycoolcompany.com কিনেছেন এবং চান domain-টি আপনার Elastic Load Balancer my-elb-1234567890.us-west-2.elb.amazonaws.com-কে point করুক। এখানে আপনাকে কোন Route 53 Record type ব্যবহার করতে হবে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

mycoolcompany.com holo zone apex (root domain). Apex e CNAME banano jay na, tai ELB er DNS name e point korte Alias record lagbe (PDF p. 200, 204-205).

Alias AWS resource (ELB, CloudFront, S3 website...) e point kore, root ar non-root dui jaygay kaj kore, query free, ar ELB er IP bodlaleo nije bujhe nay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CNAME shudhu non-root naam e chole (jemon www.mycoolcompany.com); zone apex mycoolcompany.com e CNAME allowed na.

Keyword: mycoolcompany.com (zone apex) → ELB → Alias

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You can't create a CNAME record that has the same name as the top node of the DNS namespace (Zone Apex), in our case "mycoolcompany.com."

10·2U10-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q2

You have deployed a new Elastic Beanstalk environment and would like to direct 5% of your production traffic to this new environment. This allows you to monitor for CloudWatch metrics and ensuring that there're no bugs exist with your new environment. Which Route 53 Record type allows you to do so?

আপনি একটি নতুন Elastic Beanstalk environment deploy করেছেন এবং আপনার production traffic-এর 5% এই নতুন environment-এ পাঠাতে চান। এতে আপনি CloudWatch metrics monitor করতে পারবেন এবং নিশ্চিত করতে পারবেন যে আপনার নতুন environment-এ কোনো bug নেই। কোন Route 53 Record type দিয়ে আপনি এটি করতে পারবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Weighted routing e proti record e ekta relative weight dao, traffic shei hishebe bhag hoy: traffic % = record er weight / sob record er weight er jogfol (PDF p. 209).

Notun environment e weight 5, purono te 95 dile ~5% request notun environment e jabe. Course er use case: testing new application versions. Sob thik thakle weight bariye full shift kora jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Simple e ekta record (onek value thakle client random nay); kono % control nai.
  • C. Latency user ke lowest latency er Region e pathay; nirdishto % traffic bhag kore na.
  • D. Failover active-passive: primary healthy thakle sob traffic primary te; % split kore na.

Keyword: 5% of production traffic notun environment e → Weighted

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Weighted Routing Policy allows you to redirect part of the traffic based on weight (e.g., percentage). It's a common use case to send part of traffic to a new version of your application.

10·3U10-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q3

You have updated a Route 53 Record's myapp.mydomain.com value to point to a new Elastic Load Balancer, but it looks like users are still redirected to the old ELB. What is a possible cause for this behavior?

আপনি একটি Route 53 Record-এর myapp.mydomain.com value update করে একটি নতুন Elastic Load Balancer-কে point করিয়েছেন, কিন্তু মনে হচ্ছে user-রা এখনও পুরনো ELB-তে redirect হচ্ছে। এই আচরণের সম্ভাব্য কারণ কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Proti DNS record er ekta TTL thake: client / DNS resolver answer ta TTL porjonto cache kore rakhe (PDF p. 203). Record update korar por-o jader kache purono answer cache e ache, tara TTL shesh na howa porjonto purono ELB e-i jabe.

Tip: record change er age TTL komiye nao, tahole change druto chhoray.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Alias record ELB er IP change nije bujhe nay; update korle notun target-i dey. Purono ELB e jawar karon cache.
  • B. CNAME update korle-o Route 53 notun value-i ferot dey; problem holo client er cache (TTL), record type na.
  • D. Health check unhealthy record ke answer theke baad dey; purono ELB e pathay na.

Keyword: Record updated, tobu user still purono ELB e → TTL (cache)

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Each DNS record has a TTL (Time To Live) which orders clients for how long to cache these values and not overload the DNS Resolver with DNS requests. The TTL value should be set to strike a balance between how long the value should be cached vs. how many requests should go to the DNS Resolver.

10·4U10-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q4

You have an application that's hosted in two different AWS Regions us-west-1 and eu-west-2. You want your users to get the best possible user experience by minimizing the response time from application servers to your users. Which Route 53 Routing Policy should you choose?

আপনার একটি application দুটি ভিন্ন AWS Regions (us-west-1 এবং eu-west-2)-এ hosted আছে। আপনি চান application server থেকে user-দের কাছে response time কমিয়ে user-রা সবচেয়ে ভালো user experience পাক। কোন Route 53 Routing Policy বেছে নেবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Latency-based routing user ke shei Region er resource e pathay jeta tar jonno lowest latency dey (PDF p. 210). Latency mapa hoy user ar AWS Region er moddher traffic dekhe.

Duita Region (us-west-1, eu-west-2), goal response time minimize: tai Latency.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Multi-Value onek healthy value return kore (max 8), client ekta nay; kon Region druto seta dekhe na.
  • B. Weighted fixed % e traffic bhag kore; user er jonno kon Region druto seta dekhe na.
  • D. Geolocation user er desh / continent er rule dhore route kore, latency dhore na; fastest Region guarantee kore na.

Keyword: minimizing the response time, duita Region → Latency

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Latency Routing Policy will evaluate the latency between your users and AWS Regions, and help them get a DNS response that will minimize their latency (e.g. response time)

10·5U10-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q5

You have a legal requirement that people in any country but France should NOT be able to access your website. Which Route 53 Routing Policy helps you in achieving this?

আপনার একটি legal requirement আছে: France ছাড়া অন্য যেকোনো দেশের মানুষ আপনার website access করতে পারবে না (NOT)। কোন Route 53 Routing Policy এটি অর্জনে সাহায্য করে?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Geolocation routing user er location (continent, country, US state) dhore answer dey (PDF p. 216). Shudhu France er jonno record banao ar Default record banio na: tahole onno desh er user kono answer pabe na, site e dhukte parbe na.

Course er use case: restrict content distribution.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Latency lowest latency er Region e pathay; user kon desh theke ashche seta dhore block korte pare na.
  • B. Simple sobaike ek-i answer dey; desh dhore alada kora jay na.
  • C. Multi-Value onek healthy IP return kore; location dhore filter kore na.

Keyword: any country but France access na → Geolocation (shudhu France record, Default na)

10·6U10-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q6

You have purchased a domain on GoDaddy and would like to use Route 53 as the DNS Service Provider. What should you do to make this work?

আপনি GoDaddy-তে একটি domain কিনেছেন এবং Route 53-কে DNS Service Provider হিসেবে ব্যবহার করতে চান। এটি কাজ করানোর জন্য আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Domain Registrar ar DNS service alada company hote pare (PDF p. 222-224). GoDaddy te kena domain er DNS Route 53 e manage korte:

  • Route 53 e Public Hosted Zone banao (internet theke query ashbe).
  • 3rd party registrar (GoDaddy) er website e NS record update kore Route 53 er name server boshao.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Domain transfer lage na; registrar GoDaddy thakleo Route 53 ke DNS service hishebe use kora jay.
  • B. Private Hosted Zone shudhu VPC er bhitorer query answer kore; internet user website khujle eta kaj korbe na.
  • C. NS update korte hoy registrar (GoDaddy) e, Route 53 e na; Route 53 hosted zone er NS record nije-i banay.

Keyword: purchased on GoDaddy, Route 53 = DNS Service → Public Hosted Zone + 3rd party registrar e NS update

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Private Hosted Zones are meant to be used for internal network (e.g., VPC) DNS queries and are not publicly accessible.

D: Public Hosted Zones are meant to be used for people requesting your website through the Internet. Finally, NS records must be updated on the 3rd party Registrar.

10·7U10-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 10 · Q7

Which of the following are NOT valid Route 53 Health Checks?

নিচের কোনগুলো valid Route 53 Health Check নয় (NOT)?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Route 53 health check 3 dhoroner (PDF p. 211):

  • Endpoint monitor (application, server, onno AWS resource)
  • Onno health check monitor (Calculated Health Check)
  • CloudWatch Alarm monitor

SQS queue monitor kora health check bole kichu nai, tai A valid na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Valid: endpoint monitor kora health check (HTTP, HTTPS, TCP), ~15 global health checker diye.
  • C. Valid: Calculated Health Check, onek child health check er result AND / OR / NOT diye jog kore.
  • D. Valid: CloudWatch Alarm monitor kora health check, private resource er jonno kaje lage.

Keyword: NOT valid → SQS Queue (valid: endpoint, other health checks, CloudWatch alarm)

Section 11: Classic Solutions Architecture Discussions (6)

Note: Section 11 note. Answer first, then press Check.

11·1U11-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 11 · Q1

Your website TriangleSunglasses.com is hosted on a fleet of EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group and fronted by an Application Load Balancer. Your ASG has been configured to scale on-demand based on the traffic going to your website. To reduce costs, you have configured the ASG to scale based on the traffic going through the ALB. To make the solution highly available, you have updated your ASG and set the minimum capacity to 2. How can you further reduce the costs while respecting the requirements?

আপনার website TriangleSunglasses.com একটি Auto Scaling Group দ্বারা managed EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet-এ hosted এবং সামনে একটি Application Load Balancer আছে। আপনার ASG আপনার website-এ আসা traffic-এর ভিত্তিতে on-demand scale করার জন্য configure করা হয়েছে। cost কমাতে, আপনি ASG-কে ALB দিয়ে যাওয়া traffic-এর ভিত্তিতে scale করার জন্য configure করেছেন। solution-টি highly available করতে, আপনি ASG update করে minimum capacity 2 সেট করেছেন। requirement-গুলো মেনে আপনি কীভাবে cost আরও কমাতে পারেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

ASG er minimum capacity 2 mane ei 2 ta instance sob somoy cholbe (protita AZ e ekta, HA er jonno). Je instance sara bochor cholbe, segulo Reserved Instance korle On-Demand er cheye onek sosta (PDF p. 239, WhatIsTheTime.com er shesh dhap).

Scale-out e je extra instance ashe, segulo temporary, On-Demand e thakuk. Ei bhabe cost kome, kintu HA ar scaling duita-i thake.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ALB soriye Elastic IP dile ek IP ek instance e bandha; load balancing, health check ar ASG er scaling er subidha harabe. HA ar scaling duita-i bhange.
  • C. Min 1 korle ek AZ e ekta instance thakbe; oi instance ba AZ gele app down. Requirement (highly available) bhange.
  • D. Min 0 korle traffic kom thakle kono instance-i thakbe na, app downtime pabe. HA to durer kotha.

Keyword: minimum capacity 2 (sob somoy chole) + further reduce costs + HA rakhte hobe → oi 2 ta Reserve koro

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is not efficient for scaling purposes.

B: This is the way to save further costs as we will run 2 EC2 instances no matter what.

C: Our solution won't be highly available anymore.

D: Our solution will experience downtime.

11·2U11-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 11 · Q2

Which of the following will NOT help us while designing a STATELESS application tier?

একটি STATELESS application tier design করার সময় নিচের কোনটি আমাদের সাহায্য করবে না (NOT)?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Prosno ta NOT chay. Stateless tier mane je kono EC2 je kono request serve korte parbe, tai session data shared jaygay ba client er kache rakhte hobe.

EBS volume ek AZ e bandha ar sadharonoto ek somoy ek instance e attach thake. Session EBS e rakhle shudhu oi instance session jane; onno instance pay na. Tai eta stateless design e help kore na (MyClothes.com, PDF p. 245).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS e session rakhle sob EC2 same database theke porte pare, tai web tier stateless thake. Eta help kore, NOT er uttor na.
  • B. ElastiCache course er main session store: cookie te session_id, data ElastiCache e, je kono EC2 pore (PDF p. 245). Help kore.
  • C. Client cookie te session rakhle EC2 ke kichu mone rakhte hoy na, tai stateless (PDF p. 244). Help kore (cookie 4 KB limit, validate lage).

Keyword: STATELESS + NOT help → session EBS e (ek instance/AZ e bandha, share hoy na)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This will help as the data can be accessed from multiple EC2 instances.

B: This will help as the data can be accessed from multiple EC2 instances.

C: This will help as the clients send their session data so that our EC2 instances don't need to remember/store session data.

D: EBS volumes are created in a specific AZ and can only be attached to one EC2 instance at a time.

11·3U11-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 11 · Q3

You want to install software updates on 100s of Linux EC2 instances that you manage. You want to store these updates on shared storage which should be dynamically loaded on the EC2 instances and shouldn't require heavy operations. What do you suggest?

আপনি যে 100s Linux EC2 instance manage করেন, সেগুলোতে software update install করতে চান। আপনি এই update-গুলো একটি shared storage-এ store করতে চান, যা EC2 instance-গুলোতে dynamically load হবে এবং যার জন্য ভারী operation লাগবে না। আপনি কী suggest করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

EFS holo managed NFS network file system. Onek (100s) Linux EC2 eki file system ekshathe mount korte pare, onek AZ theke-o (PDF p. 113, MyWordPress.com PDF p. 257).

Update gulo EFS e rakho, protita instance startup e EFS mount kore update pore nibe. Kono sync ba copy er jhamela nai = kom operation.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EBS share hoy na, tai protita AZ e master ar replication software lagbe. Eta native AWS feature na, ar heavy operation.
  • C. Protita update er jonno snapshot theke notun EBS volume banano kaj korbe, kintu onek manual kaj; ar EBS share hoy na. Heavy operation.
  • D. RDS relational data er jonno, software update er moto boro binary file rakhar ba mount korar jonno na.

Keyword: 100s of Linux EC2 + shared storage + no heavy operations → EFS mount

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This requires too many operations and is not a native AWS feature.

B: EFS is a network file system (NFS) that allows you to mount the same file system to 100s of EC2 instances. Storing software updates on an EFS allows each EC2 instance to access them.

C: This requires too many operations although it would work.

D: RDS is meant to store relational data, not big binary files.

11·4U11-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 11 · Q4

As a Solutions Architect, you're planning to migrate a complex ERP software suite to AWS Cloud. You're planning to host the software on a set of Linux EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group. The software traditionally takes over an hour to set up on a Linux machine. How do you recommend you speed up the installation process when there's a scale-out event?

একজন Solutions Architect হিসেবে, আপনি একটি complex ERP software suite AWS Cloud-এ migrate করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন। আপনি software-টি একটি Auto Scaling Group দ্বারা managed কিছু Linux EC2 instance-এ host করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন। software-টি সাধারণত একটি Linux machine-এ setup হতে এক ঘণ্টার বেশি সময় নেয়। scale-out event হলে installation process দ্রুত করতে আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Golden AMI = software age theke install ar configure kore banano AMI. ASG scale-out e notun EC2 oi AMI theke boot kore, tai 1 ghontar install ar lage na; sobcheye druto start (PDF p. 260).

Course er niyom: slow, ek-i rokom install AMI te; shudhu dynamic config User Data te.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. User Data install ta automate kore, kintu install protita notun instance e boot er somoy ghote, tai ekhono 1 ghonta lagbe.
  • C. RDS relational data rakhe, application install rakhe na ba chalay na.
  • D. Setup file EFS theke anle download hoyto shohoj, kintu install ar configure korte ekhono 1 ghonta lagbe.

Keyword: over an hour to set up + speed up scale-out → Golden AMI

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Golden AMI is an image that contains all your software installed and configured so that future EC2 instances can boot up quickly from that AMI.

B: EC2 User Data allows you to automate the installation steps, but the installation would still take one hour to complete.

C: RDS is used to store relational data, not applications.

D: Retrieving application setup files from EFS won't help speed up the installation and configuration time.

11·5U11-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 11 · Q5

You're developing an application and would like to deploy it to Elastic Beanstalk with minimal cost. You should run it in ..................

আপনি একটি application develop করছেন এবং minimal cost-এ এটি Elastic Beanstalk-এ deploy করতে চান। আপনার এটি .................. -এ চালানো উচিত

Correct: A. Why it is right

Beanstalk er Single Instance mode e ekta EC2 + Elastic IP (load balancer nai), optional RDS. Course bole eta dev er jonno great (PDF p. 267).

Development stage + minimal cost → Single Instance. Beanstalk nije free, tumi shudhu resource er dam dao, tai kom resource = kom dam.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. High Availability mode ALB + ASG e multi-AZ e onek EC2 (ar RDS Multi-AZ) banay. Production er jonno bhalo, kintu load balancer ar beshi instance er jonno beshi dam.

Keyword: developing + minimal cost → Single Instance mode

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The question mentions that you're still in the development stage and you want to reduce costs. Single Instance Mode will create one EC2 instance and one Elastic IP.

B: This will create one or more EC2 instances and one Elastic Load Balancer (this costs more).

11·6U11-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 11 · Q6

You're deploying your application to an Elastic Beanstalk environment but you notice that the deployment process is painfully slow. After reviewing the logs, you found that your dependencies are resolved on each EC2 instance each time you deploy. How can you speed up the deployment process with minimal impact?

আপনি আপনার application একটি Elastic Beanstalk environment-এ deploy করছেন, কিন্তু দেখছেন deployment process খুবই ধীর। log review করে দেখলেন, প্রতিবার deploy করার সময় প্রতিটি EC2 instance-এ আপনার dependency-গুলো resolve হচ্ছে। minimal impact-এ আপনি কীভাবে deployment process দ্রুত করতে পারেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Problem: protita deploy e protita EC2 te dependency resolve/download hocche. Dependency gulo Golden AMI te age theke install kore rakho, Beanstalk ke oi custom AMI diye EC2 launch korte dao. Tahole deploy e shudhu code jay, dependency step bad (PDF p. 260).

Code e kono change lage na = minimal impact. Beanstalk nijei Golden AMI + User Data hybrid principle use kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Dependency soriye dile app bhenge jete pare; code change lage. Minimal impact na.
  • B. EFS e rakhle file shared hoy, kintu dependency resolve/install er step protita instance e ekhono chole; ar network drive theke load slow hote pare.

Keyword: dependencies resolved on each EC2 each deploy + minimal impact → dependency soho Golden AMI

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Golden AMI is an image that contains all your software, dependencies, and configurations, so that future EC2 instances can boot up quickly from that AMI.
