12·1U12-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q1

You're getting errors while trying to create a new S3 bucket named "dev". You're using a new AWS Account with no S3 buckets created before. And you double-checked and found that you have the correct IAM permissions to create S3 Buckets. What is a possible cause for this?

"dev" নামে একটি নতুন S3 bucket তৈরি করার চেষ্টা করার সময় আপনি error পাচ্ছেন। আপনি একটি নতুন AWS Account ব্যবহার করছেন, যেখানে আগে কোনো S3 bucket তৈরি করা হয়নি। এবং আপনি আবার check করে দেখেছেন যে S3 Buckets তৈরি করার সঠিক IAM permission আপনার আছে। এর সম্ভাব্য কারণ কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

General purpose bucket er nam (default) globally unique: sob Region ar sob AWS account jure (PDF p. 271). dev er moto choto, common nam onek age-i keu niye feleche, tai create e error ashe, permission thik thakleo.

2026 er notun Account Regional Namespace opt-in feature; default global namespace e ei answer ekhono thik.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Root user lage na; je IAM user/role er s3:CreateBucket permission ache se-i bucket banate pare. Question e bola-i ache permission thik.

Keyword: "dev" nam e error + permission thik → nam globally unique, already taken

12·2U12-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q2

You have enabled versioning in your S3 bucket which already contains a lot of files. Which version will the existing files have?

আপনি আপনার S3 bucket-এ versioning enable করেছেন, যেখানে আগে থেকেই অনেক file আছে। existing file-গুলোর version কী হবে?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Versioning enable korar age theke thaka file er version ID hoy null (PDF p. 282). Enable er por notun upload/overwrite e notun version ID ashe.

Hands-on e-o dekha geche: coffee.jpg, beach.jpg er version ID null.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 version ID 1, 2, 3 erokom number na; ar purono file kono number pay na, pay null.
  • B. 0 bole kono version ID S3 dey na; versioning er age er file er version null.
  • C. -1 S3 er kono version ID na; existing file er version null.

Keyword: existing files + versioning enabled later → version null

12·3U12-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q3

You have updated an S3 bucket policy to allow IAM users to read/write files in the S3 bucket, but one of the users complain that he can't perform a PutObject API call. What is a possible cause for this?

আপনি IAM user-দের S3 bucket-এ file read/write করতে allow করার জন্য একটি S3 bucket policy update করেছেন, কিন্তু একজন user অভিযোগ করছে যে সে PutObject API call করতে পারছে না। এর সম্ভাব্য কারণ কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 access rule: IAM policy ALLOW ba resource (bucket) policy ALLOW korle hoy, kintu kono explicit DENY thakle DENY jite (PDF p. 274).

Bucket policy allow korche, tobu ekjon user PutObject parche na → tar attached IAM policy te explicit Deny ache.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bucket policy bhul hole sob user-i parto na; ekhane shudhu ekjon user er problem, tai user er nijer policy te kichu ache.
  • B. Bucket policy already user ke allow korche; IAM permission na thakleo bucket policy er Allow-i jothesto. Tai 'permission nai' karon na.
  • D. PutObject er kono limit AWS Support diye tulte hoy na; eta permission er problem, service limit na.

Keyword: bucket policy allow kore + ekjon user parche na → IAM policy te explicit DENY

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Even if the user doesn't have permissions, in the question the S3 bucket is explicitly allowing the user and that's enough from a permission's perspective

C: Explicit DENY in an IAM Policy will take precedence over an S3 bucket policy.

12·4U12-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q4

You want the content of an S3 bucket to be fully available in different AWS Regions. That will help your team perform data analysis at the lowest latency and cost possible. What S3 feature should you use?

আপনি চান একটি S3 bucket-এর content বিভিন্ন AWS Regions-এ পুরোপুরি available থাকুক। এতে আপনার team সম্ভাব্য সবচেয়ে কম latency ও cost-এ data analysis করতে পারবে। কোন S3 feature ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: D. Why it is right

S3 Replication (CRR) bucket er content asynchronously onno Region er bucket e copy kore (PDF p. 283). Tai protita Region e pura data thake, team kache theke lowest latency te analysis korte pare.

CRR use case: compliance, lower latency access, cross-account replication.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CloudFront edge location e cache kore, kintu bucket er pura content onno Region er bucket e copy kore na; analysis er jonno full data available hoy na.
  • B. Versioning shudhu overwrite/delete e purono version rakhe; onno Region e copy kore na (tobe replication er prerequisite).
  • C. Static Website Hosting bucket ke website hisebe serve kore; onno Region e data copy kore na.

Keyword: content fully available in different Regions → S3 Replication (CRR)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon CloudFront caches your data in different Edge Locations across the world, but it won't copy the content of your S3 bucket to a different AWS Region.

B: S3 Versioning allows you to keep different versions of a file in case of overwrites.

D: S3 Replication allows you to replicate data from an S3 bucket to another in the same/different AWS Region.

12·5U12-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q5

You have 3 S3 buckets. One source bucket A, and two destination buckets B and C in different AWS Regions. You want to replicate objects from bucket A to both bucket B and C. How would you achieve this?

আপনার 3টি S3 bucket আছে। একটি source bucket A, এবং ভিন্ন ভিন্ন AWS Regions-এ দুটি destination bucket B এবং C। আপনি bucket A থেকে bucket B ও C দুটোতেই object replicate করতে চান। কীভাবে এটি করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

S3 Replication e chaining nai: bucket 1 → 2 → 3 thakle bucket 1 er object bucket 3 e jabe na (PDF p. 284).

Tai A theke B ar C dui jaygay chaile source A te duita rule dao: A → B ar A → C.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. B → C chain kaj kore na: A theke B te asha replica object B → C rule e replicate hoy na, tai C te A er object ashbe na.
  • C. Ek-i chaining problem: A → C er replica C → B diye abar replicate hoy na, B te A er object pouchabe na.

Keyword: A theke B ar C dutoi + chaining nai → A→B, A→C

Instructor feedback (English)

B: S3 Replication doesn't support "chaining" of replication.

C: S3 Replication doesn't support "chaining" of replication.

12·6U12-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q6

Which of the following is NOT a Glacier Deep Archive retrieval mode?

নিচের কোনটি Glacier Deep Archive-এর retrieval mode নয় (NOT)?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Question NOT chacche. Glacier Deep Archive e retrieval mode shudhu duita: Standard (12 hours) ar Bulk (48 hours) (PDF p. 289).

Expedited (1-5 min) Deep Archive e nai; eta Glacier Flexible Retrieval er mode.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Standard (12 hours) Deep Archive er valid retrieval mode, tai 'NOT' er answer na.
  • C. Bulk (48 hours) Deep Archive er valid (sobcheye shosta) retrieval mode, tai 'NOT' er answer na.

Keyword: NOT + Deep Archive → Expedited nai (shudhu Standard 12 h, Bulk 48 h)

12·7U12-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 12 · Q7

Which of the following is NOT a Glacier Flexible retrieval mode?

নিচের কোনটি Glacier Flexible-এর retrieval mode নয় (NOT)?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Question NOT chacche. Glacier Flexible Retrieval er mode: Expedited (1-5 min), Standard (3-5 h), Bulk (5-12 h, free) (PDF p. 289).

'Instant' bole kono mode nai; millisecond access chaile alada class Glacier Instant Retrieval.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Expedited (1-5 minutes) Glacier Flexible er valid mode, tai 'NOT' er answer na.
  • C. Standard (3-5 hours) Glacier Flexible er valid mode.
  • D. Bulk (5-12 hours) Glacier Flexible er valid, free mode.

Keyword: NOT + Glacier Flexible → Instant bole mode nai (eta Glacier Instant Retrieval class)

Section 13: Advanced Amazon S3 (9)

13·1U13-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q1

How can you be notified when there's an object uploaded to your S3 bucket?

আপনার S3 bucket-এ কোনো object upload হলে আপনি কীভাবে notification পেতে পারেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 Event Notifications: bucket e S3:ObjectCreated, ObjectRemoved, ObjectRestore, Replication event hole SNS, SQS ba Lambda te notification jay (PDF p. 301). Object upload = ObjectCreated event.

Chaile sob event EventBridge e-o pathano jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 Select object er moddhe SQL diye kichu data filter kore pore; notification dey na. (Ar S3 Select ekhon notun customer der jonno bondho.)
  • B. Access Logs bucket e asha request er log onno bucket e likhe rakhe; pore analysis er jonno, upload e notify kore na.
  • D. S3 Analytics (Storage Class Analysis) koto din por transition korbe tar recommendation dey; event notification na.

Keyword: notified when object uploaded → S3 Event Notifications

13·2U13-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q2

You have an S3 bucket that has S3 Versioning enabled. This S3 bucket has a lot of objects, and you would like to remove old object versions to reduce costs. What's the best approach to automate the deletion of these old object versions?

আপনার একটি S3 bucket আছে, যেখানে S3 Versioning enabled। এই S3 bucket-এ অনেক object আছে, এবং cost কমাতে আপনি পুরনো object version-গুলো সরিয়ে ফেলতে চান। এই পুরনো object version-গুলোর deletion automate করার সবচেয়ে ভালো approach কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Lifecycle Rule e Expiration actions object ke nirdishto din por delete kore, ar versioning on thakle old (noncurrent) version o delete korte pare (PDF p. 296).

Console e action: 'Permanently delete noncurrent versions of objects'. Purono version jome cost barche → expiration diye automatic porishkar.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Transition actions object ke sostar storage class e shoray, delete kore na. Cost kichu kome, kintu purono version theke-i jay.
  • C. Access Logs shudhu request er log rakhe; kono object ba version delete kore na.

Keyword: remove old object versions + automate → Lifecycle Expiration actions

13·3U13-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q3

How can you automate the transition of S3 objects between their different tiers?

S3 object-গুলোর বিভিন্ন tier-এর মধ্যে transition আপনি কীভাবে automate করতে পারেন?

Renamed: CloudWatch Events -> Amazon EventBridge (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 Lifecycle Rules er transition actions object ke automatic onno storage class e shoray, ex: 60 din por Standard-IA, 6 mash por Glacier (PDF p. 295-296).

Native S3 feature, kono code ba server lage na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda diye nije code likhe class bodlano somvob, kintu custom code, maintenance ar cost; native automatic upay Lifecycle Rules.
  • B. CloudWatch Events (ekhon Amazon EventBridge) event route ba schedule kore; nije storage class transition kore na.

Keyword: automate transition between tiers → S3 Lifecycle Rules

13·4U13-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q4

While you're uploading large files to an S3 bucket using Multi-part Upload, there are a lot of unfinished parts stored in the S3 bucket due to network issues. You are not using these unfinished parts and they cost you money. What is the best approach to remove these unfinished parts?

Multi-part Upload ব্যবহার করে একটি S3 bucket-এ বড় file upload করার সময় network সমস্যার কারণে S3 bucket-এ অনেক unfinished part store হয়ে আছে। আপনি এই unfinished part-গুলো ব্যবহার করছেন না এবং এগুলোর জন্য আপনার টাকা খরচ হচ্ছে। এই unfinished part-গুলো সরানোর সবচেয়ে ভালো approach কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Lifecycle Rule er expiration diye incomplete multi-part upload nirdishto din por automatic delete kora jay (PDF p. 296; console action: 'Delete expired object delete markers or incomplete multipart uploads').

Ekbar rule dile S3 nije porishkar kore, kono code ba manual kaj lage na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda loop kaj korbe, kintu onek manual code, maintenance ar beshi cost. Native lifecycle rule thakte eta best na.
  • B. AWS Support tomar bucket er data delete kore dey na; eta tomar nijer kaj, lifecycle rule diye automate koro.

Keyword: unfinished parts of Multi-part Upload + cost → Lifecycle Policy

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This would work but it includes a lot of manual work and will cost you more money.

13·5U13-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q5

You are looking to get recommendations for S3 Lifecycle Rules. How can you analyze the optimal number of days to move objects between different storage tiers?

আপনি S3 Lifecycle Rules-এর জন্য recommendation পেতে চাইছেন। বিভিন্ন storage tier-এর মধ্যে object সরানোর optimal দিনের সংখ্যা আপনি কীভাবে analyze করতে পারেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

S3 Analytics (Storage Class Analysis) bucket er access pattern dekhe daily .csv report dey: koto din por object onno class e nile bhalo (PDF p. 299).

Lifecycle Rule banano ba improve korar prothom step. Mone rakho: recommendation shudhu Standard ar Standard-IA er jonno, report shuru hote 24-48 ghonta.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 Inventory bucket er object er list ar metadata report dey (ex: Batch Operations er jonno); koto din por transition korbe tar recommendation dey na.
  • C. 'S3 Lifecycle Rules Advisor' namer kono feature nai; distractor.

Keyword: recommendations + optimal number of days to move → S3 Analytics

13·6U13-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q6⚠ outdated

You are looking to build an index of your files in S3, using Amazon RDS PostgreSQL. To build this index, it is necessary to read the first 250 bytes of each object in S3, which contains some metadata about the content of the file itself. There are over 100,000 files in your S3 bucket, amounting to 50 TB of data. How can you build this index efficiently?

আপনি Amazon RDS PostgreSQL ব্যবহার করে S3-এ থাকা আপনার file-গুলোর একটি index তৈরি করতে চান। এই index তৈরি করতে S3-এর প্রতিটি object-এর প্রথম 250 bytes read করা দরকার, যেখানে file-এর content সম্পর্কে কিছু metadata আছে। আপনার S3 bucket-এ 100,000-এর বেশি file আছে, মোট 50 TB data। কীভাবে আপনি এই index efficiently তৈরি করতে পারেন?

⚠ Outdated: Option D er S3 Select July 2024 theke new customer der jonno available na (shudhu existing customer use korte pare), course slide eo ar nai. Tai ei type kaje Byte-Range Fetch (key) i thik. source

Correct: C. Why it is right

Byte-Range Fetch: GET request e shudhu nirdishto byte range chao. Course e use case: file er prothom kichu byte e header thakle shudhu oi range ano (PDF p. 306).

100,000 file er prothom 250 byte = khub choto data; 50 TB puro download kora lage na. Tai efficient.

Aj: option D er S3 Select July 2024 theke notun customer der jonno available na (shudhu existing customer), tai ei kaje Byte-Range Fetch-i thik.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS PostgreSQL S3 theke import korte pare (aws_s3 extension), kintu tate puro 50 TB data load hobe; slow ar dami, index er jonno shudhu 250 byte lage.
  • B. Sob file puro read korle 50 TB download hobe; kaj korbe, kintu khub slow ar dami. Efficient na.
  • D. S3 Select SQL diye filter kore, 'first 250 bytes' anar tool na (eta ScanRange use kore); ar ekhon notun customer der jonno bondho. Simple ranged GET-i bhalo.

Keyword: first 250 bytes of each object + efficiently → Byte Range Fetch

13·7U13-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q7

You have a large dataset stored on-premises that you want to upload to the S3 bucket. The dataset is divided into 10 GB files. You have good bandwidth but your Internet connection isn't stable. What is the best way to upload this dataset to S3 and ensure that the process is fast and avoid any problems with the Internet connection?

আপনার on-premises-এ store করা একটি বড় dataset আছে, যা আপনি S3 bucket-এ upload করতে চান। dataset-টি 10 GB-র file-এ ভাগ করা। আপনার bandwidth ভালো, কিন্তু Internet connection stable নয়। এই dataset S3-এ upload করার এবং process দ্রুত রাখা ও Internet connection-এর যেকোনো সমস্যা এড়ানো নিশ্চিত করার সবচেয়ে ভালো উপায় কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

  • Multi-part Upload: 10 GB file part e bhag hoye parallel upload; connection unstable hole shudhu fail kora part abar pathao (5 GB er beshi file e must, PDF p. 305).
  • Transfer Acceleration: kacher edge location e upload, tarpor private AWS network e bucket e, tai fast (PDF p. 305).

Duita compatible; fast + unstable internet duita problem-i mitay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Multi-part unstable connection handle kore, kintu Transfer Acceleration chara speed er extra boost nai. 'Fast' requirement puro hoy na.
  • B. S3 Select data query kore, upload er sathe somporko nai (ar notun customer der jonno bondho). Multi-part chara unstable internet e 10 GB file fail hole abar shuru theke.

Keyword: 10 GB files + Internet not stable + fast → Multi-part Upload + Transfer Acceleration

13·8U13-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q8

A company is preparing for compliance and regulatory review on its infrastructure on AWS. Currently, they have their files stored on S3 buckets encrypted using S3 Default Encryption, which must be encrypted using KMS as required for compliance and regulatory review. Which S3 feature allows them to encrypt all files in their S3 buckets in the most efficient and cost-effective way?

একটি company AWS-এ তাদের infrastructure-এর compliance ও regulatory review-এর জন্য প্রস্তুতি নিচ্ছে। বর্তমানে তাদের file-গুলো S3 Default Encryption দিয়ে encrypt করা S3 bucket-এ store করা আছে, কিন্তু compliance ও regulatory review-এর requirement অনুযায়ী এগুলো KMS দিয়ে encrypt করতে হবে। কোন S3 feature তাদের S3 bucket-এর সব file সবচেয়ে efficient ও cost-effective উপায়ে encrypt করতে দেয়?

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 Batch Operations existing onek object e ek job e bulk kaj kore; course e main use case: unencrypted/existing object encrypt kora (PDF p. 307).

Retry, progress tracking, completion notification, report nije kore, tai script er cheye efficient. Sob file KMS e re-encrypt → Batch Operations.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Access Points bucket e access manage korar alada entry point ar policy dey; object encrypt kore na.
  • B. Cross-Region Replication onno region er bucket e copy kore, shudhu enable korar por notun object; existing file encrypt korar efficient upay na.
  • D. Lifecycle Rules shudhu transition ba expire kore; encryption bodlay na.

Keyword: encrypt all (existing) files with KMS + efficient, cost-effective → S3 Batch Operations

13·9U13-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 13 · Q9⚠ outdated

You have a 25 GB file that you're trying to upload to S3 but you're getting errors. What is a possible solution for this?

আপনার একটি 25 GB file আছে, যা আপনি S3-এ upload করার চেষ্টা করছেন কিন্তু error পাচ্ছেন। এর একটি সম্ভাব্য solution কী?

⚠ Outdated: Explanation e bola 'S3 file size limit 5 TB', kintu Dec 2025 theke S3 er max object size 50 TB (course slide v49 o 50TB bole). Single PUT limit ekhono 5 GB, tai 25 GB file e Multi-part Upload lagbe, answer thik. source

Correct: C. Why it is right

Ek single PUT e max 5 GB upload hoy. 25 GB file tai single upload e error dey. Multi-part upload 100 MB er beshi e recommended, 5 GB er beshi e must (PDF p. 305).

Aj: S3 er max object size Dec 2025 theke 50 TB (explanation er '5 TB' purono), kintu single PUT limit ekhono 5 GB, tai answer same.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul: S3 object er limit 5 GB na (ekhon max 50 TB). 5 GB holo shudhu single PUT er limit.
  • B. Bucket policy access permission dey; file size limit bodlay na.
  • D. Encryption security er jonno; upload size limit er sathe somporko nai.

Keyword: 25 GB file + upload errors → Multi-Part upload (> 5 GB must)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The file size limit on S3 is 5 TB.

C: Multi-Part Upload is recommended as soon as the file is over 100 MB.

Section 14: Amazon S3 Security (12)

14·1U14-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q1⚠ outdated

Your client wants to make sure that file encryption is happening in S3, but he wants to fully manage the encryption keys and never store them in AWS. You recommend him to use ............................

আপনার client নিশ্চিত হতে চান যে S3-এ file encryption হচ্ছে, কিন্তু তিনি encryption key পুরোপুরি নিজে manage করতে চান এবং কখনও AWS-এ store করতে চান না। আপনি তাঁকে ............................ ব্যবহার করার recommend করবেন

⚠ Outdated: April 2026 theke notun S3 general purpose bucket e SSE-C by default blocked. SSE-C use korte hole PutBucketEncryption diye (BlockedEncryptionTypes) SSE-C allow korte hoy. Concept hisebe answer SSE-C ekhono thik. source

Correct: C. Why it is right

SSE-C e key customer nije manage kore, AWS er baire rakhe, ar proti request e HTTPS header e key pathay. S3 oi key diye server-side encrypt kore, kintu key store kore na (PDF p. 320).

Tai "encryption S3 e hobe" + "key puro nijer, AWS e kokhono store hobe na" → SSE-C.

Aajker update: April 2026 theke notun general purpose bucket e SSE-C default blocked; use korte hole PutBucketEncryption (BlockedEncryptionTypes) diye allow korte hoy. Concept/exam answer ekhono SSE-C.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SSE-S3 e key AWS (S3) nije own ar manage kore; customer er kono key control nai.
  • B. SSE-KMS e key AWS KMS e store thake; customer control pay kintu key AWS er bhitore-i thake.
  • D. Client-side e encryption S3 e hoy na, client nije kore upload er age. Question chay encryption S3 e hok.

Keyword: encryption S3 e + key fully manage + never store in AWS → SSE-C

Instructor feedback (English)

A: With SSE-S3, the encryption happens in AWS and you the encryption keys managed by AWS. Encryption keys stored in AWS.

B: With SSE-KMS, the encryption happens in AWS, and the encryption keys are managed by AWS but you have full control over the rotation policy of the encryption key. Encryption keys stored in AWS.

C: With SSE-C, the encryption happens in AWS and you have full control over the encryption keys.

D: With Client-Side Encryption, you have to do the encryption yourself and you have full control over the encryption keys.

14·2U14-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q2

A company you're working for wants their data stored in S3 to be encrypted. They don't mind the encryption keys stored and managed by AWS, but they want to maintain control over the rotation policy of the encryption keys. You recommend them to use ....................

আপনি যে company-তে কাজ করছেন, তারা চায় S3-এ store করা তাদের data encrypt করা থাকুক। encryption key AWS store ও manage করলে তাদের আপত্তি নেই, কিন্তু তারা encryption key-গুলোর rotation policy-র ওপর নিয়ন্ত্রণ রাখতে চায়। আপনি তাদের .................... ব্যবহার করার recommend করবেন

Correct: B. Why it is right

SSE-KMS e key AWS KMS e store ar manage hoy, kintu tumi KMS key er upor user control pao (rotation policy shoho), ar CloudTrail e key usage audit hoy (PDF p. 317).

Question: key AWS e thakle somossa nai, kintu rotation nije control korte chay → SSE-KMS.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SSE-S3 e key puro S3 er owned; rotation ba key er upor tomar kono control nai.
  • C. SSE-C e key customer AWS er baire rakhe; question bolche key AWS store/manage korbe.
  • D. Client-side e key ar encryption duto-i client er hate; AWS key manage kore na, requirement er ulta.

Keyword: key AWS e store/manage OK + rotation policy control → SSE-KMS

Instructor feedback (English)

A: With SSE-S3, the encryption happens in AWS and you don't manage the encryption keys. Encryption keys stored in AWS.

B: With SSE-KMS, the encryption happens in AWS, and the encryption keys are managed by AWS but you have full control over the rotation policy of the encryption key. Encryption keys stored in AWS.

C: With SSE-C, the encryption happens in AWS and you have full control over the encryption keys. You could define a rotation policy but this is against the question requirements (encryption keys must be stored and managed by AWS).

D: With Client-Side Encryption, you have to do the encryption yourself and you have full control over the encryption keys. You could define a rotation policy but this is against the question requirements (encryption keys must be stored and managed by AWS).

14·3U14-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q3

Your company does not trust AWS for the encryption process and wants it to happen on the application. You recommend them to use ....................

আপনার company encryption process-এর জন্য AWS-কে বিশ্বাস করে না এবং চায় এটি application-এ হোক। আপনি তাদের .................... ব্যবহার করার recommend করবেন

Correct: D. Why it is right

Client-Side Encryption e client (application) nije data encrypt kore tarpor S3 te upload kore, ar download er por nije decrypt kore (PDF p. 321).

AWS kokhono plaintext ba key dekhe na. "AWS ke encryption process e trust kori na, application e hok" → client-side.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SSE-S3 e encryption AWS er server-side e hoy; question chay encryption application e hok.
  • B. SSE-KMS-o server-side: S3 AWS er bhitore encrypt kore, tai AWS ke trust korte hoy.
  • C. SSE-C te key tumi dao, kintu encryption ta S3 (AWS) e-i hoy; process AWS e thake.

Keyword: AWS ke trust kore na + encryption application e → Client-Side Encryption

Instructor feedback (English)

A: With SSE-S3, the encryption happens in AWS and you the encryption keys managed by AWS. Encryption keys stored in AWS.

B: With SSE-KMS, the encryption happens in AWS, and the encryption keys are managed by AWS but you have full control over the rotation policy of the encryption key. Encryption keys stored in AWS.

C: With SSE-C, the encryption happens in AWS and you have full control over the encryption keys.

D: With Client-Side Encryption, you have to do the encryption yourself and you have full control over the encryption keys. You perform the encryption yourself and send the encrypted data to AWS. AWS does not know your encryption keys and cannot decrypt your data.

14·4U14-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q4

You have a website that loads files from an S3 bucket. When you try the URL of the files directly in your Chrome browser it works, but when a website with a different domain tries to load these files it doesn't. What's the problem?

আপনার একটি website আছে, যা একটি S3 bucket থেকে file load করে। আপনার Chrome browser-এ সরাসরি file-গুলোর URL চেষ্টা করলে কাজ করে, কিন্তু ভিন্ন domain-এর একটি website এই file-গুলো load করার চেষ্টা করলে কাজ করে না। সমস্যাটি কী?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Browser e file er URL directly khulle kaj kore, mane permission thik ache. Kintu onno domain er website load korte parche na → eta CORS problem (PDF p. 325-327).

Browser cross-origin request tokhon-i allow kore jokhon S3 bucket Access-Control-Allow-Origin header pathay. Fix: bucket e oi origin (ba *) er jonno CORS rule add koro.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bucket policy bhul hole direct URL-o kaj korto na; ekhane direct access thik ache.
  • B. IAM policy bhul hole duto case-ei fail korto; direct access kaj korche, tai IAM na.
  • D. Encryption e problem thakle direct download-o fail korto; ekhane shudhu cross-domain fail.

Keyword: direct URL kaj kore + different domain theke fail → CORS

Instructor feedback (English)

A: If the S3 bucket policy is wrong, the files won't load in either case.

B: If the IAM policy was wrong, the files won't load in either case.

C: Cross-Origin Resource Sharing (CORS) defines a way for client web applications that are loaded in one domain to interact with resources in a different domain. To learn more about CORS, go here: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/dev/cors.html

D: If the encryption was wrong, the files won't load in either case.

14·5U14-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q5⚠ outdated

An e-commerce company has its customers and orders data stored in an S3 bucket. The company’s CEO wants to generate a report to show the list of customers and the revenue for each customer. Customer data stored in files on the S3 bucket has sensitive information that we don’t want to expose in the report. How do you recommend the report can be created without exposing sensitive information?

একটি e-commerce company-র customer ও order data একটি S3 bucket-এ store করা আছে। company-র CEO customer-দের list এবং প্রতিটি customer-এর revenue দেখানো একটি report generate করতে চান। S3 bucket-এর file-এ store করা customer data-তে sensitive information আছে, যা আমরা report-এ expose করতে চাই না। sensitive information expose না করে কীভাবে report তৈরি করা যায় বলে আপনি recommend করবেন?

⚠ Outdated: Nov 7, 2025 theke S3 Object Lambda new customer der jonno available na (shudhu existing customer). AWS ekhon bole Lambda ke function URL / API Gateway / CloudFront diye call koro, ba client e data process koro. Course/exam er hisebe answer Object Lambda. source

Correct: A. Why it is right

S3 Object Lambda: caller application object retrieve korar age Lambda function object ke change kore (PDF p. 336). Use case: PII/sensitive data redact kora analytics er jonno.

Ekta bucket-i lage; S3 Access Point + Object Lambda Access Point er upor kaj kore, data er second copy rakhte hoy na.

Aajker update: Nov 7, 2025 theke Object Lambda notun customer der jonno available na (shudhu existing); AWS ekhon Lambda function URL / API Gateway / CloudFront ba client-side processing bole. Exam answer ekhono Object Lambda.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Kaj korbe, kintu second bucket e data copy, extra storage ar sync/maintain korar jhamela; Object Lambda ei kaj on-the-fly kore ek bucket e.
  • C. Object Lock WORM protection (delete/overwrite atkay); file er bhitorer sensitive field lukate pare na.

Keyword: report e sensitive info expose na kore, retrieve er age data change → S3 Object Lambda

14·6U14-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q6

You suspect that some of your employees try to access files in an S3 bucket that they don't have access to. How can you verify this is indeed the case without them noticing?

আপনি সন্দেহ করছেন যে আপনার কিছু employee একটি S3 bucket-এর এমন file access করার চেষ্টা করছে, যেগুলোতে তাদের access নেই। তারা টের না পেয়ে কীভাবে আপনি যাচাই করতে পারেন যে আসলেই এটা ঘটছে?

Correct: A. Why it is right

S3 Access Logs S3 e asha shob request log kore, je account thekei hok, authorized ba denied, onno ekta S3 bucket e (PDF p. 329).

Tarpor Athena diye log file er upor serverless SQL query chalano jay. Kono access change hoy na, tai employee ra tero pay na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. IAM policy restrict korle employee ra access hariye bujhe jabe; question bole "without them noticing".
  • C. Bucket policy access block korbe, tai employee ra notice korbe; ar policy nije kono log dey na.

Keyword: unauthorized access verify + without them noticing → S3 Access Logs + Athena

Instructor feedback (English)

A: S3 Access Logs log all the requests made to S3 buckets and Amazon Athena can then be used to run serverless analytics on top of the log files.

B: This will be noticed by employees as they won't be able to access the files.

C: A bucket policy would prevent access to the user but they would notice it.

14·7U14-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q7

You are looking to provide temporary URLs to a growing list of federated users to allow them to perform a file upload on your S3 bucket to a specific location. What should you use?

আপনি ক্রমবর্ধমান federated user-দের একটি list-কে temporary URL দিতে চান, যাতে তারা আপনার S3 bucket-এর একটি নির্দিষ্ট location-এ file upload করতে পারে। আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Pre-Signed URL temporary URL: je user generate kore tar GET/PUT permission inherit kore, expiration thake (PDF p. 331).

Slide er example-i eta: "ever-changing list of users" ar "temporarily upload a file to a precise location". Tai growing federated user der specific location e upload er jonno pre-signed URL.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CORS shudhu browser ke onno origin er request allow kore; kono temporary permission ba URL dey na.
  • C. Bucket policy static; barte thaka dynamic user list ke temporary access deya jay na.

Keyword: temporary URL + growing list of users + upload to specific location → Pre-Signed URL

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Cross-Origin Resource Sharing (CORS) defines a way for client web applications that are loaded in one domain to interact with resources in a different domain. To learn more about CORS, go here: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/AmazonS3/latest/dev/cors.html

B: S3 Pre-Signed URLs are temporary URLs that you generate to grant time-limited access to some actions in your S3 bucket.

C: S3 Bucket Policies cannot help in giving temporary access to a dynamic list of federated users.

14·8U14-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q8⚠ outdated

For compliance reasons, your company has a policy mandate that database backups must be retained for 4 years. It shouldn't be possible to erase them. What do you recommend?

compliance-এর কারণে আপনার company-র একটি policy নির্দেশ আছে যে database backup 4 বছর রাখতে হবে। এগুলো মুছে ফেলা সম্ভব হওয়া উচিত নয়। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

⚠ Outdated: Purono vault-based Amazon Glacier service Dec 15, 2025 theke new customer ney na. Ekhon WORM backup er jonno S3 Glacier storage class + S3 Object Lock (Compliance mode) use kora hoy. Ei option gulor moddhe answer ekhono A. source

Correct: A. Why it is right

Glacier Vault Lock: WORM (Write Once Read Many) model. Vault Lock Policy banao, tarpor lock koro; ar edit/delete kora jay na, object delete hoy na (PDF p. 332). Compliance retention (4 bochor) er jonno perfect.

Aajker update: vault-based Amazon Glacier Dec 15, 2025 theke notun customer ney na; ekhon S3 Glacier storage class + S3 Object Lock (Compliance mode) use hoy. Ei option gulor moddhe answer ekhono A.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Linux permission root/admin chaile bodlate pare; WORM ba compliance guarantee nai, ar backup archive er jonno EFS dami.
  • C. Bucket policy pore keu edit ba remove korte pare; locked na, tai "erase kora possible na" guarantee dey na.

Keyword: retain 4 years + erase kora jabe na (compliance) → Glacier Vault Lock (WORM)

14·9U14-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q9

You would like all your files in an S3 bucket to be encrypted by default. What is the optimal way of achieving this?

আপনি চান একটি S3 bucket-এর আপনার সব file by default encrypted থাকুক। এটি অর্জনের optimal উপায় কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Jan 2023 theke S3 sob notun bucket ar notun object automatically SSE-S3 diye encrypt kore (PDF p. 316, 324). Tai kichu korte hobe na; eta-i optimal.

Bucket policy diye shudhu specific encryption force korte hoy (jemon SSE-KMS); default encryption er jonno lage na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. HTTPS force (aws:SecureTransport) shudhu in-flight encryption dey; at-rest encryption na.
  • C. Versioning purono version rakhe; encryption er sathe kono somporko nai.

Keyword: encrypted by default + optimal → kichu na, S3 auto SSE-S3

Instructor feedback (English)

14·10U14-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q10

You have enabled versioning and want to be extra careful when it comes to deleting files on an S3 bucket. What should you enable to prevent accidental permanent deletions?

আপনি versioning enable করেছেন এবং একটি S3 bucket-এ file delete করার ব্যাপারে বাড়তি সতর্ক থাকতে চান। accidental permanent deletion ঠেকাতে আপনার কী enable করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

MFA Delete chalu thakle object version permanently delete korte ba versioning suspend korte MFA code lage (PDF p. 328). Accidental permanent deletion er jonno extra protection.

Shorto: versioning enabled thakte hobe (ache), ar shudhu bucket owner (root) eta enable korte pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bucket policy diye delete puro block kora jay, kintu tokhon dorkari delete-o hobe na; "extra careful" MFA er moto confirmation na.
  • C. Encryption data ke unreadable rakhe; delete atkay na.
  • D. Versioning disable korle ulta protection kome jay; purono version theke recover korar upay thake na.

Keyword: versioning on + prevent accidental permanent deletion → MFA Delete

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The bucket policy would prevent deletes altogether.

B: MFA Delete forces users to use MFA codes before deleting S3 objects. It's an extra level of security to prevent accidental deletions.

14·11U14-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q11

A company has its data and files stored on some S3 buckets. Some of these files need to be kept for a predefined period of time and protected from being overwritten and deletion according to company compliance policy. Which S3 feature helps you in doing this?

একটি company-র data ও file কিছু S3 bucket-এ store করা আছে। company compliance policy অনুযায়ী এই file-গুলোর কিছু একটি পূর্বনির্ধারিত সময় পর্যন্ত রাখতে হবে এবং overwrite ও deletion থেকে protected রাখতে হবে। কোন S3 feature এটি করতে আপনাকে সাহায্য করে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 Object Lock WORM model; versioning lage. Compliance mode e retention period er moddhe kono user, root-o object version overwrite ba delete korte pare na, mode bodlano ba period komano jay na (PDF p. 333).

Company compliance policy + predefined period + overwrite/delete theke strict protection → Compliance mode.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Governance mode e special permission wala user retention bodlate ba delete korte pare; compliance er strict guarantee na.
  • B. Versioning purono version rakhe, kintu keu version delete korte pare; fixed period er lock nai.
  • D. Glacier Vault Lock Glacier vault er jonno; question S3 bucket e rakha file er kotha bolche, tai S3 Object Lock.

Keyword: predefined period + overwrite/delete theke protect + compliance policy → Object Lock Compliance mode

14·12U14-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 14 · Q12

Which of the following S3 Object Lock configuration allows you to prevent an object or its versions from being overwritten or deleted indefinitely and gives you the ability to remove it manually?

নিচের কোন S3 Object Lock configuration একটি object বা তার version-গুলোকে অনির্দিষ্টকাল overwrite বা delete হওয়া থেকে আটকাতে দেয় এবং আপনাকে manually এটি সরানোর সুবিধা দেয়?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Legal Hold object ke indefinitely protect kore, retention period theke independent. s3:PutObjectLegalHold IAM permission thakle freely lagano ar remove kora jay (PDF p. 333).

"Indefinitely" + "manually remove" → Legal Hold.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Governance mode e ekta fixed retention period thake (indefinite na); period shesh hole lock shesh.
  • B. Compliance mode fixed period er jonno, ar keu (root-o) shorate ba komate pare na; manual remove possible na.

Keyword: indefinitely + remove manually → Legal Hold

Section 15: CloudFront & AWS Global Accelerator (6)

Note: Section 15 note. Answer first, then press Check.

15·1U15-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 15 · Q1

You have a CloudFront Distribution that serves your website hosted on a fleet of EC2 instances behind an Application Load Balancer. All your clients are from the United States, but you found that some malicious requests are coming from other countries. What should you do to only allow users from the US and block other countries?

Correct: A. Why it is right

CloudFront Geo Restriction diye country onujayi access control kora jay: Allowlist (shudhu list er desh access pabe) ba Blocklist (list er desh block). Country ber kora hoy 3rd party Geo-IP database diye (PDF p. 345).

Ekhane allowlist e shudhu United States dile baki sob desh er request edge e-i block hoye jabe, ALB/EC2 porjonto ashbe na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. OAC S3 bucket ke private rekhe shudhu CloudFront ke read korte dey; country block kore na, ar ekhane origin S3 na, ALB.
  • C. CloudFront distribution e security group attach kora jay na; SG thake EC2/ALB e, ar SG country chene na.
  • D. Route 53 latency record user ke lowest latency region e pathay; kono desh ke block kore na.

Keyword: shudhu US allow, onno desh block + CloudFront → Geo Restriction (allowlist)

15·2U15-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 15 · Q2

You have a static website hosted on an S3 bucket. You have created a CloudFront Distribution that points to your S3 bucket to better serve your requests and improve performance. After a while, you noticed that users can still access your website directly from the S3 bucket. You want to enforce users to access the website only through CloudFront. How would you achieve that?

Correct: B. Why it is right

S3 origin secure korar course er upay: Origin Access Control (OAC) + S3 bucket policy (PDF p. 339, 341).

  • CloudFront OAC diye S3 e sign kora request pathay.
  • Bucket policy shudhu oi CloudFront distribution ke s3:GetObject allow kore, bucket public na.

Fole direct S3 URL e gele Access Denied, content shudhu CloudFront diye pawa jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Email kono technical enforcement na; bucket public thakle je keu direct S3 URL use korte parbe.
  • C. S3 Access Points bucket er jonno alada access policy wala entry point; client ke CloudFront e redirect kore na, direct access-o bondho kore na.

Keyword: user shudhu CloudFront diye, direct S3 na → OAC + bucket policy

15·3U15-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 15 · Q3

What does this S3 bucket policy do?

{

    "Version": "2012-10-17",

    "Id": "Mystery policy",

    "Statement": [{

        "Sid": "What could it be?",

        "Effect": "Allow",

        "Principal": {

           "Service": "cloudfront.amazonaws.com"

        },

        "Action": "s3:GetObject",

        "Resource": "arn:aws:s3:::examplebucket/*",
        "Condition": {
            "StringEquals": {
                "AWS:SourceArn": "arn:aws:cloudfront::123456789012:distribution/EDFDVBD6EXAMPLE"
            }
        }

    }]

}

Correct: B. Why it is right

Ei policy ta holo OAC pattern er bucket policy (Lecture 2 hands-on e CloudFront ei policy copy korte bole):

  • Principal = cloudfront.amazonaws.com (CloudFront service)
  • Action = s3:GetObject (file read)
  • Condition AWS:SourceArn = ekta nirdishto distribution er ARN

Mane: bucket er object shudhu oi CloudFront distribution theke read kora jabe, onno keu na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Policy te encryption er kono condition nai (jemon aws:SecureTransport ba SSE header); eta shudhu ke read korte parbe seta bole.
  • C. Principal * (anybody) na; principal CloudFront service ar Condition diye ekta distribution e limited.

Keyword: Principal cloudfront.amazonaws.com + AWS:SourceArn = distribution → shudhu oi distribution access pay

15·4U15-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 15 · Q4

A WordPress website is hosted in a set of EC2 instances in an EC2 Auto Scaling Group and fronted by a CloudFront Distribution which is configured to cache the content for 3 days. You have released a new version of the website and want to release it immediately to production without waiting for 3 days for the cached content to be expired. What is the easiest and most efficient way to solve this?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Origin update korle CloudFront jane na; TTL (ekhane 3 din) expire holei notun content ane. CloudFront Invalidation diye TTL bypass kore puro ba partial cache refresh force kora jay (PDF p. 346).

Path dao: /* (sob file) ba /images/* ba /index.html. Porer request edge cache e na peye origin theke notun version ane. Tai "easiest, ekhuni release" → Cache Invalidation.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Support ticket lagbe na; invalidation tumi nijei console/API theke korte paro, ticket slow ar unnecessary.
  • C. EC2 er kono "cache invalidation" feature nai; cache ta CloudFront edge e, tai invalidation CloudFront e korte hobe.

Keyword: cache 3 din + notun version ekhuni, wait na → CloudFront Cache Invalidation

15·5U15-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 15 · Q5

A company is deploying a media-sharing website to AWS. They are going to use CloudFront to deliver the content with low latency to their customers where they are located in both US and Europe only. After a while there a huge costs for CloudFront. Which CloudFront feature allows you to decrease costs by targeting only US and Europe?

Correct: B. Why it is right

CloudFront Price Classes edge location er sonkha komiye cost komay (Lecture 5):

  • Price Class All: sob region, best performance, beshi cost.
  • Price Class 200: sobcheye dami region bad.
  • Price Class 100: shudhu sobcheye shosta region (North America + Europe).

Customer shudhu US ar Europe e → Price Class 100 nile cost kome, oi user der latency o bhalo thake.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Invalidation cache theke file sorano, ulta origin e beshi request jay; cost region onujayi komay na.
  • C. Cache behavior path pattern onujayi caching/origin rule thik kore; kon region er edge use hobe seta thik kore na.
  • D. OAC S3 origin ke shudhu CloudFront er jonno secure kore; cost ba region er sathe somporko nai.

Keyword: user shudhu US + Europe + CloudFront cost komao → Price Classes (Price Class 100)

15·6U15-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 15 · Q6

A company is migrating a web application to AWS Cloud and they are going to use a set of EC2 instances in an EC2 Auto Scaling Group. The web application is made of multiple components so they will need a host-based routing feature to route to specific web application components. This web application is used by many customers and therefore the web application must have a static IP address so it can be whitelisted by the customers’ firewalls. As the customers are distributed around the world, the web application must also provide low latency to all customers. Which AWS service can help you to assign a static IP address and provide low latency across the globe?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Tin ta requirement: host-based routing, static IP (firewall whitelist), global low latency.

  • ALB host-based routing kore, kintu ALB er static IP nai.
  • Global Accelerator app er jonno 2 ta static Anycast IP dey, user nearest edge e dhoke, tarpor AWS internal network diye app e jay → low latency. Endpoint hishebe ALB support kore (PDF p. 349-350).

Tai GA + ALB: customer firewall e shudhu 2 ta IP whitelist, ar ALB component onujayi route kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. CloudFront CDN, HTTP content cache kore; customer der whitelist korar moto static IP dey na, host-based routing o kore na.
  • C. NLB e static IP/Elastic IP ache, kintu eta regional (global latency kome na) ar Layer 4, tai host-based routing kore na.
  • D. ALB host-based routing kore, kintu ALB er static IP nai ar eka global latency komay na.

Keyword: host-based routing + static IP whitelist + global low latency → Global Accelerator + ALB

Section 16: AWS Storage Extras (18)

Note: Section 16 note. Answer first, then press Check.

16·1U16-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q1⚠ outdated

You need to move hundreds of Terabytes into Amazon S3, then process the data using a fleet of EC2 instances. You have a 1 Gbit/s broadband. You would like to move the data faster and possibly processing it while in transit. What do you recommend?

আপনাকে শত শত Terabyte data Amazon S3-এ সরাতে হবে, তারপর EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet ব্যবহার করে data process করতে হবে। আপনার একটি 1 Gbit/s broadband আছে। আপনি data আরও দ্রুত সরাতে চান এবং সম্ভব হলে transit-এ থাকা অবস্থাতেই process করতে চান। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

⚠ Outdated: Nov 7, 2025 theke AWS Snowball Edge new customer der jonno available na (Snowcone 2024 e discontinued). New customer der jonno AWS bole DataSync (online) ba AWS Data Transfer Terminal (physical). Course/exam er hisebe answer Snowball Edge. source

Correct: D. Why it is right

Hundreds of TB, kintu line shudhu 1 Gbps: course er table e 100 TB 1 Gbps e 12 din lage (PDF p. 354). Rule of thumb: network e 1 soptaho er beshi lagle → Snowball.

Ar "processing it while in transit" → Snowball Edge e compute ache (104 vCPU, EC2 instance ba Lambda chalano jay), tai data load korar shomoy-i preprocess kora jay (PDF p. 353, 356).

Aajker update: Nov 7, 2025 theke Snowball Edge notun customer der jonno available na (existing ra use korte pare); notun customer ke AWS DataSync (online) ba AWS Data Transfer Terminal bole. Exam/course answer ekhono Snowball Edge.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 1 Gbps e hundreds of TB pathate soptaho lage, ar transit e process korar kono upay nai.
  • B. Snowcone khub choto device (DataSync agent pre-installed), hundreds of TB er jonno na; ar eta 2024 e discontinued.
  • C. Data/Database Migration Service database migrate kore, file data ship ba transit e process kore na.

Keyword: hundreds of TB + 1 Gbps + process while in transit → Snowball Edge

Instructor feedback (English)

D: Snowball Edge is the right answer as it comes with computing capabilities and allows you to pre-process the data while it's being moved into Snowball.

16·2U16-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q2

You want to expose virtually infinite storage for your tape backups. You want to keep the same software you're using and want an iSCSI compatible interface. What do you use?

আপনি আপনার tape backup-এর জন্য কার্যত অসীম storage দিতে চান। আপনি বর্তমানে যে software ব্যবহার করছেন সেটিই রাখতে চান এবং একটি iSCSI compatible interface চান। আপনি কী ব্যবহার করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Tape Gateway = Virtual Tape Library (VTL), backed by S3 ar Glacier (PDF p. 369).

  • Backup software same thake: leading backup vendor der sathe kaj kore, existing tape process cholte thake.
  • Interface iSCSI (VTL), tai backup server bhabe normal tape library.
  • Pichone S3/Glacier, tai storage virtually infinite.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Snowball ekbar data physically ship kore migrate kore; tape backup software er jonno ongoing iSCSI VTL interface dey na.
  • C. Volume Gateway iSCSI block volume (disk) dey, tape library na; backup software tape hishebe dekhte pabe na.
  • D. S3 File Gateway NFS/SMB file share dey, iSCSI na, tape interface o na.

Keyword: tape backups + same software + iSCSI → Tape Gateway

16·3U16-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q3

Your EC2 Windows Servers need to share some data by having a Network File System mounted on them which respects the Windows security mechanisms and has integration with Microsoft Active Directory. What do you recommend?

আপনার EC2 Windows Server-গুলোকে তাদের উপর mount করা একটি Network File System-এর মাধ্যমে কিছু data share করতে হবে, যেটি Windows security mechanism মেনে চলে এবং Microsoft Active Directory-র সাথে integration আছে। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

FSx for Windows File Server = fully managed Windows share drive: SMB protocol, Windows NTFS, Microsoft Active Directory integration, ACL, user quota (PDF p. 359).

Question e "Windows security mechanisms" (NTFS/ACL) + "Active Directory" = sorasori FSx for Windows er keyword.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. EFS holo Linux er jonno NFS/POSIX file system; Windows SMB/NTFS ba AD integration nai.
  • C. FSx for Lustre HPC/ML er jonno Linux parallel file system; Windows security ba AD er jonno na.
  • D. S3 File Gateway on-premises theke S3 ke file share hishebe dekhay; AWS e EC2 Windows server der shared Windows file system na.

Keyword: EC2 Windows + Windows security + Active Directory → FSx for Windows

Instructor feedback (English)

B: This is for Linux.

C: This is for HPC and Linux.

16·4U16-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q4⚠ outdated

You have hundreds of Terabytes that you want to migrate to AWS S3 as soon as possible. You tried to use your network bandwidth and it will take around 3 weeks to complete the upload process. What is the recommended approach to using in this situation?

আপনার কাছে শত শত Terabyte data আছে, যেটি যত দ্রুত সম্ভব AWS S3-এ migrate করতে চান। আপনি আপনার network bandwidth ব্যবহার করে দেখেছেন, upload process শেষ হতে প্রায় 3 সপ্তাহ লাগবে। এই পরিস্থিতিতে ব্যবহারের জন্য recommended approach কোনটি?

⚠ Outdated: Nov 7, 2025 theke AWS Snowball Edge new customer der jonno available na (existing customer use korte pare). New customer der jonno AWS bole DataSync (online) ba AWS Data Transfer Terminal. Course/exam er hisebe answer Snowball Edge. source

Correct: C. Why it is right

Network e upload korte ~3 soptaho lagbe. Course er rule: network e 1 soptaho er beshi lagle → Snowball device (offline migration) (PDF p. 354).

Snowball Edge Storage Optimized e 210 TB; data load kore ship back korle AWS data S3 e import kore (PDF p. 353, 355).

Aajker update: Nov 7, 2025 theke Snowball Edge notun customer der jonno available na (existing customer use korte pare); notun customer ke DataSync ba AWS Data Transfer Terminal. Exam/course answer ekhono Snowball Edge.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Volume Gateway on-premises volume ke iSCSI diye S3 backed kore; data network diyei jay, tai 3 soptaho somossa thekei jay.
  • B. Multi-part upload boro file upload ke parallel/reliable kore, kintu bandwidth bare na; total time prai same thake.
  • D. DMS database migrate korar jonno; hundreds of TB file data S3 e ana er jonno na, ar network diyei jay.

Keyword: hundreds of TB + network e ~3 weeks + ASAP → Snowball Edge

16·5U16-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q5

You have a large dataset stored in S3 that you want to access from on-premises servers using the NFS or SMB protocol. Also, you want to authenticate access to these files through on-premises Microsoft AD. What would you use?

আপনার S3-এ store করা একটি বড় dataset আছে, যেটি আপনি NFS বা SMB protocol ব্যবহার করে on-premises server থেকে access করতে চান। এছাড়া আপনি on-premises Microsoft AD-এর মাধ্যমে এই file-গুলোর access authenticate করতে চান। আপনি কী ব্যবহার করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

S3 File Gateway: configure kora S3 bucket gulo on-premises e NFS ba SMB diye access kora jay, recent data local cache e thake (PDF p. 367).

SMB use korle Active Directory (AD) integration diye user authentication hoy. Tai "S3 dataset + NFS/SMB + on-prem Microsoft AD" → S3 File Gateway.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Volume Gateway iSCSI block volume dey, NFS/SMB file share na; S3 er object file hishebe dekhay na.
  • C. Tape Gateway backup software er jonno virtual tape (iSCSI VTL), file access er jonno na.
  • D. DMS database migrate kore; S3 er file NFS/SMB diye on-prem e expose kore na.

Keyword: S3 dataset on-prem theke NFS / SMB + on-prem AD → S3 File Gateway

16·6U16-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q6

You are planning to migrate your company's infrastructure from on-premises to AWS Cloud. You have an on-premises Microsoft Windows File Server that you want to migrate. What is the most suitable AWS service you can use?

আপনি আপনার company-র infrastructure on-premises থেকে AWS Cloud-এ migrate করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন। আপনার একটি on-premises Microsoft Windows File Server আছে, যেটি migrate করতে চান। আপনি ব্যবহার করতে পারেন এমন সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত AWS service কোনটি?

Correct: A. Why it is right

On-premises Microsoft Windows File Server AWS e niye ashar shobcheye shohoj target holo Amazon FSx for Windows File Server: fully managed Windows share, SMB, NTFS, AD integration (PDF p. 359).

Same protocol ar permission model, tai app/user der kichu bodlate hoy na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. S3 File Gateway on-premises theke S3 ke file share hishebe dekhay (hybrid); Windows File Server ke AWS e migrate korar managed Windows file system na.
  • C. AWS Managed Microsoft AD holo directory service (user/authentication), file server na; file gulo kothao store kore na.

Keyword: Windows File Server migrate → FSx for Windows File Server

16·7U16-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q7

You would like to have a distributed POSIX compliant file system that will allow you to maximize the IOPS in order to perform some High-Performance Computing (HPC) and genomics computational research. This file system has to easily scale to millions of IOPS. What do you recommend?

আপনি একটি distributed POSIX compliant file system চান, যেটি কিছু High-Performance Computing (HPC) ও genomics computational research করার জন্য IOPS সর্বোচ্চ করতে দেবে। এই file system-কে সহজে লক্ষ লক্ষ (millions of) IOPS পর্যন্ত scale করতে হবে। আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

FSx for Lustre = parallel distributed file system, large-scale computing er jonno: HPC, machine learning, genomics/EDA type research (PDF p. 360).

  • Scale: 100s GB/s, millions of IOPS, sub-ms latency.
  • Linux client e POSIX file system hishebe mount hoy.

Tai "distributed POSIX + HPC + genomics + millions of IOPS" → FSx for Lustre.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EFS Max I/O mode beshi throughput ar parallelism dey kintu latency beshi; eta general Linux share, HPC er jonno na.
  • C. S3 object storage, POSIX compliant file system na; EC2 te "mount" kore HPC file system hishebe chalano jay na.
  • D. Instance Store onek IOPS dey, kintu eta ek instance er physical disk; distributed/shared file system na, ar instance stop hole data jay.

Keyword: distributed POSIX + HPC / genomics + millions of IOPS → FSx for Lustre

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is not meant for HPC.

C: This is not POSIX compliant.

D: This will give you the IOPS, but it is not a Distributed File System.

16·8U16-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q8

Which deployment option in the FSx file system provides you with long-term storage that's replicated within AZ?

FSx file system-এর কোন deployment option আপনাকে AZ-এর মধ্যে replicated long-term storage দেয়?

Correct: B. Why it is right

FSx for Lustre er duita deployment option (PDF p. 361):

  • Persistent File System: long-term storage, data same AZ er bhitore replicate hoy; file fail korle kichu minute e transparently replace. Long-term processing, sensitive data er jonno.

Tai "long-term + replicated within AZ" → Persistent.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Scratch file system temporary storage; data replicate hoy na, server fail korle data harabe. Short-term, cheap processing er jonno.

Keyword: long-term + replicated within AZ → Persistent File System

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Provides temporary storage where data is not replicated.

B: Provides long-term storage where data is replicated within the same AZ. Failed files were replaced within minutes.

16·9U16-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q9

Which of the following protocols is NOT supported by AWS Transfer Family?

নিচের কোন protocol AWS Transfer Family support করে না (NOT supported)?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Transfer Family S3 ba EFS e file in/out kore FTP protocol family diye: FTP, FTPS (FTP over SSL), SFTP (Secure FTP) (PDF p. 371).

TLS nije kono file transfer protocol na, eta encryption layer (FTPS er bhitore use hoy). Tai list e TLS-i "NOT supported".

Aajker update: Transfer Family e ekhon AS2 o ache, kintu TLS alada protocol hishebe na; answer same.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. FTP supported (unencrypted, Transfer Family e shudhu VPC er bhitore).
  • B. FTPS (FTP over SSL) supported, encrypted in flight.
  • D. SFTP (Secure File Transfer Protocol) supported, encrypted in flight.

Keyword: Transfer Family NOT supported → FTP/FTPS/SFTP ache, TLS na

Instructor feedback (English)

C: AWS Transfer Family is a managed service for file transfers into and out of S3 or EFS using the FTP protocol, thus TLS is not supported.

16·10U16-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q10⚠ outdated

A company uses a lot of files and data which is stored in an FSx for Windows File Server storage on AWS. Those files are currently used by the resources hosted on AWS. There’s a requirement for those files to be accessed on-premises with low latency. Which AWS service can help you achieve this?

একটি company অনেক file ও data ব্যবহার করে, যেগুলো AWS-এ একটি FSx for Windows File Server storage-এ store করা। ওই file-গুলো বর্তমানে AWS-এ host করা resource-গুলো ব্যবহার করে। এখন একটি requirement আছে যে ওই file-গুলো on-premises থেকে low latency-তে access করতে হবে। কোন AWS service এটি অর্জনে সাহায্য করতে পারে?

⚠ Outdated: Oct 28, 2024 theke Amazon FSx File Gateway new customer der jonno available na. AWS ekhon bole on-premises theke directly FSx for Windows File Server e connect koro (VPN/Direct Connect), ba cache lagle FSx for NetApp ONTAP FlexCache. Correct: C. Why it is right

FSx for Windows on-prem theke VPN/Direct Connect diye emnitei access kora jay, kintu gateway er main benefit-i holo local cache. Windows compatibility: SMB, NTFS, AD.

Aajker update: Oct 28, 2024 theke FSx File Gateway notun customer der jonno available na; AWS ekhon bole directly FSx for Windows e connect koro (VPN/DX), cache lagle FSx for NetApp ONTAP FlexCache. Exam/course answer ekhono FSx File Gateway.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 File Gateway er backend S3 bucket, FSx for Windows na; FSx er file cache kore na.
  • B. "FSx for Windows File Server On-Premises" bole kono service nai; FSx AWS e chole.
  • D. Volume Gateway iSCSI block volume (S3 backed, EBS snapshot) dey; FSx for Windows er file share cache kore na.

Keyword: FSx for Windows data on-premises e low latency → FSx File Gateway

16·11U16-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q11

A Solutions Architect is working on planning the migration of a startup company from on-premises to AWS. Currently, their infrastructure consists of many servers and 30 TB of data hosted on a shared NFS storage. He has decided to use Amazon S3 to host the data. Which AWS service can efficiently migrate the data from on-premises to S3?

একজন Solutions Architect একটি startup company-কে on-premises থেকে AWS-এ migrate করার পরিকল্পনা নিয়ে কাজ করছেন। বর্তমানে তাদের infrastructure-এ অনেক server এবং একটি shared NFS storage-এ host করা 30 TB data আছে। তিনি data host করতে Amazon S3 ব্যবহার করার সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছেন। কোন AWS service on-premises থেকে S3-এ data efficiently migrate করতে পারে?

Correct: D. Why it is right

AWS DataSync on-premises theke AWS e large amount of data move kore. On-prem e agent install korle se NFS ba SMB server theke data niye encrypted (TLS) bhabe S3/EFS/FSx e pathay (PDF p. 373-374).

Shared NFS e 30 TB → S3 = DataSync er classic use case; permission/metadata o thake.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Tape Gateway backup software er virtual tape library; NFS share theke S3 e data migrate korar tool na.
  • B. EBS ek EC2 er block volume; eta migration service na, ar target holo S3.
  • C. Transfer Family FTP/FTPS/SFTP endpoint dey; NFS share theke boro migration er jonno na, client ke FTP diye upload korte hobe.

Keyword: on-prem NFS 30 TB → S3 efficiently migrate → DataSync

16·12U16-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q12

Which AWS service is best suited to migrate a large amount of data from an S3 bucket to an EFS file system?

একটি S3 bucket থেকে একটি EFS file system-এ বিপুল পরিমাণ data migrate করতে কোন AWS service সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

DataSync shudhu on-prem → AWS na, AWS storage service gulor moddhe o data copy kore: S3 ↔ EFS ↔ FSx, ar ei khetre agent lage na (PDF p. 375).

Tai boro data S3 bucket theke EFS file system e → DataSync. Metadata o preserve hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Snowball on-prem theke physical device diye data AWS e ane; AWS er bhitore S3 theke EFS e copy er jonno na.
  • C. Transfer Family FTP/SFTP endpoint dey user der jonno; S3 theke EFS e bulk copy kore na.
  • D. AWS Backup backup/restore manage kore; ek storage service theke arek service e data migrate korar jonno na.

Keyword: S3 → EFS, large data → DataSync (AWS → AWS)

16·13U16-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q13

A Machine Learning company is working on a set of datasets that are hosted on S3 buckets. The company decided to release those datasets to the public to be useful for others in their research, but they don’t want to configure the S3 bucket to be public. And those datasets should be exposed over the FTP protocol. What can they do to do the requirement efficiently and with the least effort?

একটি Machine Learning company S3 bucket-এ host করা কিছু dataset নিয়ে কাজ করছে। company সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছে যে অন্যদের research-এ কাজে লাগার জন্য ওই dataset-গুলো public-এর জন্য release করবে, কিন্তু তারা S3 bucket-টি public হিসেবে configure করতে চায় না। আর ওই dataset-গুলো FTP protocol-এর মাধ্যমে expose করতে হবে। requirement-টি efficiently এবং সবচেয়ে কম effort-এ পূরণ করতে তারা কী করতে পারে?

Correct: A. Why it is right

AWS Transfer Family fully managed FTP/FTPS/SFTP endpoint dey, pichone S3 ba EFS (PDF p. 371-372). Service ekta IAM role diye bucket e read/write kore, tai bucket public korte hoy na.

Server chalano, patch, scale kichui nijer na → "least effort". Users nijeder FTP client diyei dataset nite pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. EC2 te nijer FTP server chalale install, patch, scale, availability sob nijer; data o copy korte hoy. Least effort na.
  • C. Storage Gateway on-premises ke AWS storage er sathe jore (NFS/SMB/iSCSI); public ke FTP endpoint dey na.
  • D. EFS e copy kora extra step, ar EFS nije FTP expose kore na; tobu Transfer Family ba nijer server lagto. Least effort na.

Keyword: S3 dataset + FTP + bucket public na + least effort → Transfer Family

16·14U16-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q14

Amazon FSx for NetApp ONTAP is compatible with the following protocols, EXCEPT ………………

Amazon FSx for NetApp ONTAP নিচের protocol-গুলোর সাথে compatible, শুধু ……………… ছাড়া (EXCEPT)

Correct: C. Why it is right

FSx for NetApp ONTAP tin ta protocol support kore: NFS, SMB, iSCSI (PDF p. 362). Tai Linux, Windows, macOS, VMware Cloud on AWS shob jaygay kaj kore.

FTP file system access protocol na, ONTAP er list e nai. Tai EXCEPT → FTP.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. NFS supported (Linux/Unix client).
  • B. SMB supported (Windows client).
  • D. iSCSI supported (block access).

Keyword: ONTAP EXCEPT → NFS + SMB + iSCSI ache, FTP na

16·15U16-Q15Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q15

Which AWS service is best suited when migrating from an on-premises ZFS file system to AWS?

on-premises ZFS file system থেকে AWS-এ migrate করার সময় কোন AWS service সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

FSx for OpenZFS = managed OpenZFS file system AWS e, ZFS e chola on-prem workload migrate korar jonno (PDF p. 363).

NFS (v3, v4, v4.1, v4.2), up to 1,000,000 IOPS, < 0.5 ms latency, snapshot, clone. "ZFS" shobdo dekhlei → OpenZFS.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. NetApp ONTAP on-prem NetApp ONTAP / NAS workload migrate er jonno; ZFS er jonno OpenZFS.
  • C. FSx for Windows File Server Windows SMB/NTFS/AD share er jonno, ZFS na.
  • D. FSx for Lustre HPC/ML parallel file system, ZFS migration er jonno na.

16·16U16-Q16Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q16

A company is running Amazon S3 File Gateway to host their data on S3 buckets and is able to mount them on-premises using SMB. The data currently is hosted on S3 Standard storage class and there is a requirement to reduce the costs for S3. So, they have decided to migrate some of those data to S3 Glacier. What is the most efficient way they can use to move the data to S3 Glacier automatically?

একটি company S3 bucket-এ তাদের data host করতে Amazon S3 File Gateway চালাচ্ছে এবং SMB ব্যবহার করে on-premises-এ সেগুলো mount করতে পারছে। data বর্তমানে S3 Standard storage class-এ host করা, এবং S3-এর খরচ কমানোর একটি requirement আছে। তাই তারা ওই data-র কিছু অংশ S3 Glacier-এ migrate করার সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছে। data automatically S3 Glacier-এ সরাতে তারা সবচেয়ে efficient কোন উপায় ব্যবহার করতে পারে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 File Gateway er data asole S3 bucket e thake (S3 Standard, Standard-IA, One Zone-IA, Intelligent-Tiering). Glacier e transition kora hoy S3 Lifecycle Policy diye (PDF p. 367).

Lifecycle rule ekbar set korle S3 nijei object gulo Glacier e soriye dey: kono code, schedule ba server lage na → "most efficient, automatically".

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda + EventBridge diye kora jay, kintu nijer code maintain korte hoy; built-in lifecycle thakte eta extra kaj.
  • B. Batch Operations ek dofa onek object e operation chalay; proti din nijer moto schedule korte hoy, lifecycle rule er cheye beshi effort.
  • D. DataSync scheduled copy kore, kintu eta notun copy banay, ager object soray na; S3 er bhitore class change er jonno lifecycle-i shohoj.
  • E. S3 File Gateway shorashori Glacier e lekhe na; supported class Standard, Standard-IA, One Zone-IA, Intelligent-Tiering. Glacier e jete lifecycle lage.

Keyword: S3 File Gateway data → S3 Glacier e automatically → S3 Lifecycle Policy

16·17U16-Q17Udemy quiz · sec 16 · Q17

You have on-premises sensitive files and documents that you want to regularly synchronize to AWS to keep another copy. Which AWS service can help you with that?

আপনার কিছু on-premises sensitive file ও document আছে, যেগুলোর আরেকটি copy রাখতে আপনি নিয়মিত AWS-এ synchronize করতে চান। কোন AWS service এতে আপনাকে সাহায্য করতে পারে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS DataSync on-premises (NFS, SMB, HDFS) theke AWS (S3, EFS, FSx) e data sync kore; task schedule kora jay: hourly, daily, weekly (PDF p. 373).

"Regularly synchronize" + "another copy" = scheduled sync. File permission ar metadata o preserve hoy, transfer TLS diye encrypted.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DMS database migrate/replicate kore; files ar documents er jonno na.
  • B. EFS nije ekta file system (destination hote pare), kintu on-prem theke sync korar service na.

Keyword: on-prem files / documents + regularly synchronize → DataSync

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS Database Migration Service (AWS DMS) helps you migrate databases to AWS quickly and securely.

B: Amazon EFS provides a simple, serverless, set-and-forget elastic file system for use with AWS and on-premises resources.

C: AWS DataSync is an online data transfer service that simplifies, automates, and accelerates moving data between on-premises storage systems and AWS Storage services, as well as between AWS Storage services.

AWS DataSync নিচের location-গুলো support করে, শুধু .................... ছাড়া (EXCEPT)

Section 18: Containers on AWS ECS, Fargate, ECR & EKS (8)

18·1U18-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q1

You have multiple Docker-based applications hosted on-premises that you want to migrate to AWS. You don't want to provision or manage any infrastructure; you just want to run your containers on AWS. Which AWS service should you choose?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Shorto: infrastructure provision ba manage korbo na, shudhu container chalabo. Eta-i Fargate er definition: kono EC2 instance nai, tumi shudhu task definition e CPU/RAM bolo, AWS task chalay (PDF p. 424).

Scale korte shudhu task er shonkha barao. Course e instructor bolechen exam Fargate pochondo kore, karon serverless ar manage kora shohoj.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ECS EC2 launch type e EC2 instance gulo tumi-i provision ar maintain koro (ECS Agent, patching, scaling), ja 'manage korbo na' shorto bhange.
  • B. ECR shudhu Docker image store kore; container run kore na.

Keyword: don't want to provision or manage any infrastructure + run containers → Fargate on ECS

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon ECS allows you to run your containers on AWS but you must provision and maintain the EC2 instances where your containers will be run on.

B: Amazon ECR is a fully managed container registry that makes it easy to store, manage, share, and deploy your container images. It won't help in running your Docker-based applications.

C: AWS Fargate allows you to run your containers on AWS without managing any servers.

18·2U18-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q2⚠ outdated

Amazon Elastic Container Service (ECS) has two Launch Types: .................. and ..................

⚠ Outdated: Course/exam e ECS er 2 ta launch type (EC2, Fargate) - ei answer-i dhoro. Kintu ekhon ECS e EXTERNAL launch type (ECS Anywhere, on-prem server) ar ECS Managed Instances o ache, tai 'shudhu 2 ta' kotha ta ar puro thik na. source

Correct: A. Why it is right

Course e ECS er duita launch type: EC2 launch type (tumi EC2 instance provision kore, protita te ECS Agent) ar Fargate launch type (serverless, instance nai) (PDF p. 423-424).

Aajker din e ECS docs e External (ECS Anywhere) ar ECS Managed Instances o ache, tai 'shudhu 2 ta' kotha ta ar puro thik na; tobu ei option gulor moddhe A-i ekmatro thik.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. EKS alada service (managed Kubernetes), ECS er launch type na.
  • C. Fargate thik, kintu EKS ECS er launch type na; EKS nije ekta orchestrator jeta Fargate use korte pare.

Keyword: ECS launch types → EC2 + Fargate (EKS kokhono ECS launch type na)

18·3U18-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q3

You have an application hosted on an ECS Cluster (EC2 Launch Type) where you want your ECS tasks to upload files to an S3 bucket. Which IAM Role for your ECS Tasks should you modify?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Container er bhitorer app je AWS API call kore (ekhane S3 e upload), tar permission ashe ECS Task Role theke. Task Role task definition e define hoy, EC2 ar Fargate duitai te kaj kore (PDF p. 425).

EC2 launch type holeo app er permission instance profile e dewa thik na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EC2 Instance Profile use kore ECS Agent: ECS API call, ECR theke image pull, CloudWatch Logs e log pathano. Task er app er S3 access er jonno na.

Keyword: ECS tasks upload files to S3 → ECS Task Role

Instructor feedback (English)

A: EC2 Instance Profile is the IAM Role used by the ECS Agent on the EC2 instance to execute ECS-specific actions such as pulling Docker images from ECR and storing the container logs into CloudWatch Logs.

B: ECS Task Role is the IAM Role used by the ECS task itself. Use when your container wants to call other AWS services like S3, SQS, etc.

18·4U18-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q4

You're planning to migrate a WordPress website running on Docker containers from on-premises to AWS. You have decided to run the application in an ECS Cluster, but you want your docker containers to access the same WordPress website content such as website files, images, videos, etc. What do you recommend to achieve this?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Shob container ke same WordPress content (files, images, videos) dekhte hobe = shared storage. EFS ECS task e mount kora jay, EC2 ar Fargate duitai e kaj kore, ar je kono AZ er task same data dekhe (PDF p. 427).

Course: EFS = container er jonno persistent multi-AZ shared storage.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. EBS volume ek AZ e bandha, sadharonoto ek instance e attach; onek task/instance jure same content share er jonno na. ECS e EBS ekhon task e attach hoy, kintu task proti alada notun volume, shared na.
  • C. Instance store ephemeral (instance stop/terminate hole data jay) ar shudhu oi ek EC2 er; share ba Fargate e use hoy na.

Keyword: docker containers access the same website content → shared storage → EFS

Instructor feedback (English)

A: EFS volume can be shared between different EC2 instances and different ECS Tasks. It can be used as a persistent multi-AZ shared storage for your containers.

B: This can be used when all your ECS tasks run on the same EC2 instance. It's not suitable when your tasks are spread across multiple EC2 instances and can't be used with AWS Fargate.

C: EC2 Instance Store can't be shared between different EC2 instances.

18·5U18-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q5

You are deploying an application on an ECS Cluster made of EC2 instances. Currently, the cluster is hosting one application that is issuing API calls to DynamoDB successfully. Upon adding a second application, which issues API calls to S3, you are getting authorization issues. What should you do to resolve the problem and ensure proper security?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Notun app S3 call kore kintu permission nai. Fix: oi app er jonno alada IAM task role (S3 permission shoho) baniye task definition e dao. Course: alada ECS service er jonno alada task role (PDF p. 425).

Constraint word: ensure proper security = least privilege: shudhu notun app S3 pabe, DynamoDB app pabe na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Instance role e S3 dile oi EC2 er shob task (DynamoDB app o) S3 access pabe; least privilege bhange, security risk.
  • C. Fargate e gele o permission ashe task role theke; launch type bodlale authorization problem solve hoy na, ar Fargate e instance role-i thake na.
  • D. Bucket policy te ECS task ke allow korte holeo task er ekta IAM identity (task role) lagbe; ar AWS API call er jonno identity-side permission (task role) i proper fix.

Keyword: second application S3 authorization issue + proper security → notun IAM task role oi app er jonno

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This would allow the first application to access S3 as well, which is a security risk.

18·6U18-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q6

You are migrating your on-premises Docker-based applications to Amazon ECS. You were using Docker Hub Container Image Library as your container image repository. Which is an alternative AWS service which is fully integrated with Amazon ECS?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Docker Hub er bodole AWS er container image registry = Amazon ECR. ECR Docker image store ar manage kore (private + ECR Public Gallery), ECS er sathe fully integrated, pichone S3, access IAM diye (PDF p. 436).

Course: 'storing Docker images' dekhlei ECR.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Fargate container chalay (serverless compute), image store kore na.
  • C. EKS managed Kubernetes, container chalanor orchestrator; image registry na.
  • D. EC2 virtual server; container image repository service na.

Keyword: Docker Hub alternative, fully integrated with ECS → ECR

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS Fargate allows you to run your containers on AWS without managing any servers.

B: Amazon ECR is a fully managed container registry that makes it easy to store, manage, share, and deploy your container images. ECR is fully integrated with Amazon ECS, allowing easy retrieval of container images from ECR while managing and running containers using ECS.

C: Amazon EKS allows you to start, run, and scale Kubernetes applications in AWS or on-premises and run fully-managed EKS clusters on AWS.

D: Amazon EC2 can't be used to store Docker container images.

18·8U18-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 18 · Q8⚠ outdated

A developer has a running website and APIs on his local machine using containers and he wants to deploy both of them on AWS. Aajker din: App Runner April 30, 2026 theke notun customer der jonno bondho, AWS ekhon ECS Express Mode recommend kore. Kintu course/exam er answer ekhono App Runner.
