Section 17: Decoupling applications SQS, SNS, Kinesis, Active MQ (12)

Note: Section 17 note. Answer first, then press Check.

17·1U17-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q1

You have an e-commerce website and you are preparing for Black Friday which is the biggest sale of the year. You expect that your traffic will increase by 100x. Your website already using an SQS Standard Queue, and you're running a fleet of EC2 instances in an Auto Scaling Group to consume SQS messages. What should you do to prepare your SQS Queue?

আপনার একটি e-commerce website আছে এবং আপনি Black Friday-র জন্য প্রস্তুতি নিচ্ছেন, যা বছরের সবচেয়ে বড় sale। আপনি আশা করছেন আপনার traffic 100x বাড়বে। আপনার website ইতিমধ্যে একটি SQS Standard Queue ব্যবহার করছে, এবং SQS message consume করতে আপনি একটি Auto Scaling Group-এ EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet চালাচ্ছেন। আপনার SQS Queue প্রস্তুত করতে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: D. Why it is right

SQS Standard fully managed, unlimited throughput ar queue te unlimited message (PDF p. 381). Tai traffic 100x holeo queue er jonno tomake kichui korte hoy na: SQS nije scale kore.

Scale korte hobe consumer side: ASG ta queue length (ApproximateNumberOfMessages) dekhe EC2 barabe (PDF p. 385, 392).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SQS e pre-warm bole kichu nai. Pre-warm purono ELB er concept; SQS ager thekei unlimited throughput dey.
  • B. SQS queue e Auto Scaling setting nai; queue nije-i scale kore. Auto Scaling hoy consumer EC2 der ASG te.
  • C. SQS queue er kono capacity setting nai (provision korte hoy na), throughput ar message count dui-i unlimited.

Keyword: SQS Standard + traffic 100x → do nothing, SQS scales automatically

17·2U17-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q2

You have an SQS Queue where each consumer polls 10 messages at a time and finishes processing them in 1 minute. After a while, you noticed that the same SQS messages are received by different consumers resulting in your messages being processed more than once. What should you do to resolve this issue?

আপনার একটি SQS Queue আছে, যেখানে প্রতিটি consumer একবারে 10টি message poll করে এবং 1 মিনিটে সেগুলোর processing শেষ করে। কিছুক্ষণ পরে আপনি লক্ষ করলেন যে একই SQS message বিভিন্ন consumer receive করছে, ফলে আপনার message একাধিকবার process হচ্ছে। এই সমস্যা সমাধানে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Consumer 10 ta message niye 1 minute dhore process kore, kintu default visibility timeout 30 s (PDF p. 388). 30 s por message delete hoy ni, tai abar visible hoye jay ar onno consumer o pay: duibar process.

Fix: visibility timeout barao processing time er cheye beshi (jemon 2 min). Process lomba hole consumer ChangeMessageVisibility call korte pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Long polling shudhu empty response ar API call komay (queue khali hole wait kore); receive korar por message lukano thake na.
  • B. DelaySeconds message queue te dhokar shomoy lukay (delay queue/timer); receive er por processing er shomoy duplicate atkay na.
  • D. Timeout komale message aro taratari abar visible hobe, duplicate processing aro barbe.

Keyword: same message different consumers + process 1 min → Visibility Timeout barao

Instructor feedback (English)

A: When SQS Long Polling is enabled, Amazon SQS reducing the number of empty responses when there are no messages available to return and eliminating false empty responses (when SQS messages are available but aren't included in a response).

B: SQS Delay Queues is a period of time during which Amazon SQS keeps new SQS messages invisible to consumers. In SQS Delay Queues, a message is hidden when it is first added to the queue. (default: 0 mins, max.: 15 mins)

C: SQS Visibility Timeout is a period of time during which Amazon SQS prevents other consumers from receiving and processing the message again. In Visibility Timeout, a message is hidden only after it is consumed from the queue. Increasing the Visibility Timeout gives more time to the consumer to process the message and prevent duplicate reading of the message. (default: 30 sec., min.: 0 sec., max.: 12 hours)

17·3U17-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q3

Which SQS Queue type allows your messages to be processed exactly once and in order?

কোন SQS Queue type আপনার message-গুলোকে ঠিক একবার (exactly once) এবং order অনুযায়ী process হতে দেয়?

Correct: D. Why it is right

SQS FIFO = strict ordering (Message Group ID er bhitore) + exactly-once (Deduplication ID diye 5 min er moddhe duplicate remove) (PDF p. 391).

Throughput limited: 300 msg/s, batch e 3,000 msg/s; queue naam .fifo diye shesh hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Standard queue at-least-once (duplicate hote pare) ar best-effort ordering; exactly-once ba strict order guarantee na.
  • B. DLQ alada queue jekhane bar bar fail kora message jay (debug er jonno); ordering ba exactly-once dey na.
  • C. Delay queue notun message ke kichukkhon (0-15 min) invisible rakhe; order ba dedup er sathe somporko nai.

Keyword: exactly once + in order → SQS FIFO

Instructor feedback (English)

A: SQS Standard Queue doesn't have ordering capability and doesn't guarantee to process your messages exactly once.

B: SQS Dead Letter Queue is where other SQS queues (source queues) can send messages that can't be processed (consumed) successfully. It's useful for debugging as it allows you to isolate problematic messages so you can debug why their processing doesn't succeed.

C: SQS Delay Queues is a period of time during which Amazon SQS keeps new SQS messages invisible to consumers. In SQS Delay Queues, a message is hidden when it is first added to the queue. (default: 0 mins, max.: 15 mins)

D: SQS FIFO (First-In-First-Out) Queues have all the capabilities of the SQS Standard Queue, plus the following two features. First, The order in which messages are sent and received are strictly preserved and a message is delivered once and remains available until a consumer process and deletes it. Second, duplicated messages are not introduced into the queue.

17·4U17-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q4

You have 3 different applications that you'd like to send them the same message. All 3 applications are using SQS. What is the best approach would you choose?

আপনার 3টি ভিন্ন application আছে, যেগুলোতে আপনি একই message পাঠাতে চান। 3টি application-ই SQS ব্যবহার করছে। আপনি কোন approach-টিকে সবচেয়ে ভালো (best) হিসেবে বেছে নেবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

SNS + SQS fan-out: message ekbar SNS topic e publish koro, 3 ta SQS queue topic e subscribe kora, prottek queue ekta copy pay (PDF p. 401).

  • Fully decoupled, no data loss (SQS e persist, retry).
  • Pore notun app/queue add korte shudhu subscribe.
  • Queue er access policy te SNS ke write allow korte hobe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SQS e "replication feature" bole kichu nai.
  • C. Producer nije 3 queue te alada alada pathale tightly coupled hoy, notun queue add korle code bodlate hoy, majhe crash korle kichu queue miss korte pare; scale kore na.

Keyword: same message to 3 apps all on SQS → SNS + SQS fan-out

Instructor feedback (English)

A: There's no such feature.

B: This is a common pattern where only one message is sent to the SNS topic and then "fan-out" to multiple SQS queues. This approach has the following features: it's fully decoupled, no data loss, and you have the ability to add more SQS queues (more applications) over time.

C: This is not a common pattern and doesn't scale well.

17·5U17-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q5

You have a Kinesis data stream with 6 shards provisioned. This data stream usually receiving 5 MB/s of data and sending out 8 MB/s. Occasionally, your traffic spikes up to 2x and you get a ProvisionedThroughputExceeded exception. What should you do to resolve the issue?

আপনার একটি Kinesis data stream আছে, যাতে 6টি shard provisioned। এই data stream সাধারণত 5 MB/s data receive করে এবং 8 MB/s পাঠায়। মাঝে মাঝে আপনার traffic 2x পর্যন্ত spike করে এবং আপনি একটি ProvisionedThroughputExceeded exception পান। এই সমস্যা সমাধানে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Provisioned mode e protita shard = 1 MB/s in, 2 MB/s out (PDF p. 409). 6 shard = 6 MB/s in, 12 MB/s out.

Normal 5 in / 8 out thik ache, kintu 2x spike e 10 MB/s in, 16 MB/s out lage → limit par, tai ProvisionedThroughputExceeded. Fix: shard barao (jemon 10+ shard). (On-demand mode o kaj korto, kintu option e nai.)

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. "Kinesis Replication" namer kono feature nai; ar replication throughput barato na.
  • C. SQS ke Kinesis er shamne buffer hishebe boshano bhalo design na; ordering/replay hariye jay, ar asol somossa shard capacity.

Keyword: 6 shards + 2x spike + ProvisionedThroughputExceeded → add shards

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The capacity limits of a Kinesis data stream are defined by the number of shards within the data stream. The limits can be exceeded by either data throughput or the number of reading data calls. Each shard allows for 1 MB/s incoming data and 2 MB/s outgoing data. You should increase the number of shards within your data stream to provide enough capacity.

B: Unfortunately, this feature doesn't exist!

C: This is not a good design.

17·6U17-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q6

You have a website where you want to analyze clickstream data such as the sequence of clicks a user makes, the amount of time a user spends, and where the navigation begins and how it ends. You decided to use Amazon Kinesis, so you have configured the website to send these clickstream data all the way to a Kinesis data stream. While you checking the data sent to your Kinesis data stream, you found that the users' data is not ordered and the data for one individual user is spread across many shards. How would you fix this problem?

আপনার একটি website আছে, যেখানে আপনি clickstream data analyze করতে চান, যেমন একজন user কোন sequence-এ click করেন, একজন user কত সময় কাটান, এবং navigation কোথায় শুরু হয় ও কীভাবে শেষ হয়। আপনি Amazon Kinesis ব্যবহারের সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছেন, তাই website-কে এই clickstream data সরাসরি একটি Kinesis data stream-এ পাঠানোর জন্য configure করেছেন। আপনার Kinesis data stream-এ পাঠানো data check করার সময় আপনি দেখলেন যে user-দের data ordered নয় এবং একজন user-এর data অনেকগুলো shard-এ ছড়িয়ে আছে। আপনি এই সমস্যা কীভাবে ঠিক করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Kinesis record er partition key dekhe thik kore kon shard e jabe. Same partition key → same shard, ar ek shard er bhitore data order e thake (PDF p. 408).

Tai partition key = user ID dile ek user er shob click ek shard e, order e thakbe, ar onek shard diye scale o korte parbe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 1 shard e total order paoya jay, kintu throughput 1 MB/s e atke jay; scale korte parbe na.
  • B. Ek consumer diye re-order korle scale kore na, ar asol problem consumer na, record onek shard e chhoriye pora (partition key).

Keyword: one user er data many shards e + not ordered → partition key = user identity

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Using 1 shard would guarantee total ordering but wouldn't allow us to scale.

B: This wouldn't allow us to scale.

C: Kinesis Data Stream uses the partition key associated with each data record to determine which shard a given data record belongs to. When you use the identity of each user as the partition key, this ensures the data for each user is ordered hence sent to the same shard.

17·7U17-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q7

You are running an application that produces a large amount of real-time data that you want to load into S3 and Redshift. Also, these data need to be transformed before being delivered to their destination. What is the best architecture would you choose?

আপনি এমন একটি application চালাচ্ছেন যা বিপুল পরিমাণ real-time data তৈরি করে, যা আপনি S3 ও Redshift-এ load করতে চান। এছাড়া এই data destination-এ deliver হওয়ার আগে transform করতে হবে। আপনি কোন architecture-কে সবচেয়ে ভালো (best) হিসেবে বেছে নেবেন?

Renamed: Kinesis Data Firehose -> Amazon Data Firehose (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

Kinesis Data Streams real-time data ingest kore; Firehose sheta S3 ar Redshift e load kore, ar Lambda diye transform korte pare (PDF p. 410-412).

Tai "real-time data + transform + S3/Redshift e load" = KDS + Firehose. Note: Kinesis Data Firehose er naam ekhon Amazon Data Firehose (Feb 2024), shudhu naam bodleche.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SQS + Lambda diye nije code likhe S3/Redshift e likhte hobe; SQS er S3/Redshift er sathe direct integration nai, ar streaming big data er jonno na.
  • B. SNS + HTTP endpoint notification push kore; S3/Redshift e load ba transform er built-in way nai.

Keyword: large real-time data + transform + load into S3 and Redshift → KDS + Firehose

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This doesn't have a direct integration with S3 or Redshift.

B: This doesn't have a direct integration with S3 or Redshift.

C: This is a perfect combo of technology for loading data near real-time data into S3 and Redshift. Kinesis Data Firehose supports custom data transformations using AWS Lambda.

17·8U17-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q8

Which of the following is NOT a supported subscriber for AWS SNS?

নিচের কোনটি AWS SNS-এর একটি supported subscriber নয় (NOT)?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Question NOT supported chacche. SNS subscriber holo: SQS, Lambda, Firehose, email, SMS, mobile push, HTTP(S) (PDF p. 397).

Kinesis Data Streams SNS subscriber na. Mone rakho: Firehose hoy, Data Streams hoy na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. SQS SNS er supported subscriber (fan-out pattern e eta-i use hoy), tai eta answer na.
  • C. HTTP(S) endpoint SNS er supported subscriber, tai eta answer na.
  • D. Lambda SNS er supported subscriber (message ele function chale), tai eta answer na.

Keyword: NOT a supported SNS subscriber → Kinesis Data Streams (Firehose supported)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Note: Kinesis Data Firehose is now supported, but not Kinesis Data Streams.

17·9U17-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q9

Which AWS service helps you when you want to send email notifications to your users?

আপনি যখন user-দের email notification পাঠাতে চান, তখন কোন AWS service আপনাকে সাহায্য করে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

SNS e email subscription built-in: topic e publish korle email subscriber ra mail pay (PDF p. 397; hands-on e email subscription confirm kore dekhano).

One-to-many notification (email/SMS/mobile push) = SNS.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SQS + Lambda diye email pathate gele nije code likhe SNS er feature abar banate hobe; SQS nijei email pathay na.
  • C. Kinesis streaming data er jonno; email pathate pare na, consumer chain kore oi SNS er moto kichu banate hobe.

Keyword: send email notifications to users → SNS

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You would be re-implementing a feature from SNS.

C: Amazon Kinesis on its own can not send emails. You would need to chain it with a consumer, and in the end, you would re-create something like SNS.

17·10U17-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q10

You're running many micro-services applications on-premises and they communicate using a message broker that supports MQTT protocol. You're planning to migrate these applications to AWS without re-engineering the applications and modifying the code. Which AWS service allows you to get a managed message broker that supports the MQTT protocol?

আপনি on-premises-এ অনেক micro-services application চালাচ্ছেন এবং সেগুলো MQTT protocol support করে এমন একটি message broker ব্যবহার করে communicate করে। আপনি application re-engineer না করে এবং code modify না করে এই application-গুলো AWS-এ migrate করার পরিকল্পনা করছেন। কোন AWS service আপনাকে MQTT protocol support করে এমন একটি managed message broker দেয়?

Correct: D. Why it is right

On-premises app MQTT (open protocol) broker use kore, ar code na bodlai migrate korte chay → Amazon MQ (PDF p. 414).

Amazon MQ = managed RabbitMQ / ActiveMQ broker: MQTT, AMQP, STOMP, Openwire, WSS support kore. SQS/SNS cloud-native AWS API, oigulote gele app re-engineer korte hoto.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SQS AWS er nijer API use kore, MQTT support kore na; app code bodlate hobe.
  • B. SNS o AWS proprietary API, MQTT broker na; re-engineering lagbe.
  • C. Kinesis streaming service, MQTT message broker na.

Keyword: MQTT + without re-engineering / modifying code → Amazon MQ

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon SQS does not support the MQTT protocol.

B: Amazon SNS does not support the MQTT protocol.

C: Amazon Kinesis does not support the MQTT protocol.

D: Amazon MQ supports industry-standard APIs such as JMS and NMS, and protocols for messaging, including AMQP, STOMP, MQTT, and WebSocket.

17·11U17-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q11

An e-commerce company is preparing for a big marketing promotion that will bring millions of transactions. Their website is hosted on EC2 instances in an Auto Scaling Group and they are using Amazon Aurora as their database. The Aurora database has a bottleneck and a lot of transactions have been failed in the last promotion they have made as they had a lot of transaction and the Aurora database wasn’t prepared to handle these too many transactions. What do you recommend to handle those transactions and prevent any failed transactions?

একটি e-commerce company একটি বড় marketing promotion-এর প্রস্তুতি নিচ্ছে, যা লক্ষ লক্ষ transaction নিয়ে আসবে। তাদের website একটি Auto Scaling Group-এর EC2 instance-এ hosted এবং তারা database হিসেবে Amazon Aurora ব্যবহার করছে। Aurora database-এ একটি bottleneck আছে, এবং তাদের আগের promotion-এ অনেক transaction fail হয়েছিল, কারণ transaction অনেক বেশি ছিল এবং Aurora database এত বেশি transaction handle করার জন্য প্রস্তুত ছিল না। এই transaction-গুলো handle করতে এবং কোনো failed transaction ঠেকাতে আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Course er common pattern: SQS as a buffer to database writes (PDF p. 394). App transaction shorashori Aurora te na likhe SQS e pathay; SQS infinitely scalable, message durably thake.

Alada worker (ASG) dequeue kore Aurora te insert kore, insert confirm hole message delete kore. Tai spike e transaction fail/harabe na, queue te wait korbe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Fargate e website nile shudhu compute bodlay; bottleneck Aurora te, DB te ek-i spike jabe, transaction fail hobe.
  • C. RDS for SQL Server e migrate korle o ek-i somossa (Aurora theke beshi bhalo na); write spike buffer hoy na.

Keyword: Aurora bottleneck + millions of transactions + prevent failed transactions → SQS buffer

17·12U17-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 17 · Q12

A company is using Amazon Kinesis Data Streams to ingest clickstream data and then do some analytical processes on it. There is a campaign in the next few days and the traffic is unpredictable which might grow up to 100x. What Kinesis Data Stream capacity mode do you recommend?

একটি company clickstream data ingest করে তার ওপর কিছু analytical process চালাতে Amazon Kinesis Data Streams ব্যবহার করছে। আগামী কয়েক দিনের মধ্যে একটি campaign আছে এবং traffic অনুমান করা যায় না, যা 100x পর্যন্ত বাড়তে পারে। আপনি কোন Kinesis Data Stream capacity mode recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

On-demand mode e shard provision/manage korte hoy na; gato 30 din er observed peak er upor automatically scale kore (default 4 MB/s ba 4,000 records/s write) (PDF p. 409).

"Traffic unpredictable, 100x hote pare" = on-demand er textbook case. Provisioned e nije shard hishab ar manually barate hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Provisioned mode e shard shonkha nije thik korte hoy ar manually scale korte hoy; unpredictable 100x spike e ProvisionedThroughputExceeded hobe.

Keyword: unpredictable traffic, 100x → On-demand mode

Section 19: Serverless Overviews from a Solution Architect Perspective (17)

Note: Section 19 note. Answer first, then press Check.

19·1U19-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q1

You have created a Lambda function that typically will take around 1 hour to process some data. The code works fine when you run it locally on your machine, but when you invoke the Lambda function it fails with a "timeout" error after 3 seconds. What should you do?

আপনি একটি Lambda function তৈরি করেছেন, যা সাধারণত কিছু data process করতে প্রায় 1 ঘণ্টা সময় নেবে। আপনার machine-এ locally চালালে code ঠিকভাবে কাজ করে, কিন্তু আপনি যখন Lambda function invoke করেন, তখন 3 সেকেন্ড পরে এটি একটি "timeout" error দিয়ে fail করে। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Lambda er maximum timeout 900 s = 15 minute (PDF p. 451). Job ta 1 ghonta lage, tai timeout jotoi barao, Lambda te eta kokhono shesh hobe na.

3 s e fail korche karon default timeout 3 s; kintu 15 min porjonto barale-o 1 ghontar kaj fit hoy na. Tai code ta EC2 (ba ECS / Fargate) e chalao, jekhane kono time limit nai.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda timeout max 15 min; 25 min set-i kora jay na, ar 1 ghontar job er jonno eta-o kom.
  • B. 10 GB RAM dile CPU bare, code ektu fast hoy, kintu 1 ghontar kaj 15 min er niche nambe na.

Keyword: process ~1 hour + Lambda timeout → Lambda max 15 min, tai EC2 / ECS e chalao

Instructor feedback (English)

A: The maximum timeout you can configure in Lambda is 15 minutes.

B: This may speed up the function processing time, but it wouldn't reduce execution time below 15 minutes.

C: Lambda's maximum execution time is 15 minutes. You can run your code somewhere else such as an EC2 instance or use Amazon ECS.

19·2U19-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q2

Before you create a DynamoDB table, you need to provision the EC2 instance the DynamoDB table will be running on.

একটি DynamoDB table তৈরি করার আগে, আপনাকে সেই EC2 instance provision করতে হবে, যেটিতে DynamoDB table চলবে।

Correct: B. Why it is right

DynamoDB fully managed, serverless NoSQL database (PDF p. 469). Tumi shudhu table banao; server provision, patch, maintenance kichui korte hoy na.

Capacity thik koro RCU/WCU (provisioned) ba on-demand diye, EC2 diye na. Tai statement ta False.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DynamoDB er jonno kono EC2 instance nai ba lage na; AWS pura infrastructure manage kore, tumi shudhu table ar capacity mode thik koro.

Keyword: DynamoDB + provision EC2? → False, DynamoDB serverless

Instructor feedback (English)

B: DynamoDB is serverless with no servers to provision, patch, or manage and no software to install, maintain or operate. It automatically scales tables up and down to adjust for capacity and maintain performance. It provides both provisioned (specify RCU & WCU) and on-demand (pay for what you use) capacity modes.

19·3U19-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q3

You have provisioned a DynamoDB table with 10 RCUs and 10 WCUs. A month later you want to increase the RCU to handle more read traffic. What should you do?

আপনি 10 RCUs এবং 10 WCUs সহ একটি DynamoDB table provision করেছেন। এক মাস পরে আপনি আরও বেশি read traffic handle করতে RCU বাড়াতে চান। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Provisioned mode e RCU ar WCU alada alada set kora jay (PDF p. 472). Read barbe, tai shudhu RCU barao; WCU jemon ache temon rakho.

Chaile auto scaling diye-o RCU/WCU alada vabe scale kora jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. RCU ar WCU decoupled; read barate WCU barano lage na, shudhu extra khoroch hobe.
  • C. Write traffic komeni, tai WCU komale write throttle hote pare; ar read barate WCU komano lage na.

Keyword: increase RCU for more read traffic → RCU barao, WCU same (independent)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: RCU and WCU are decoupled, so you can increase/decrease each value separately.

19·4U19-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q4

You have an e-commerce website where you are using DynamoDB as your database. You are about to enter the Christmas sale and you have a few items which are very popular and you expect that they will be read often. Unfortunately, last year due to the huge traffic you had the ProvisionedThroughputExceededException exception. What would you do to prevent this error from happening again?

আপনার একটি e-commerce website আছে, যেখানে আপনি database হিসেবে DynamoDB ব্যবহার করছেন। আপনি Christmas sale-এ প্রবেশ করতে যাচ্ছেন এবং আপনার কয়েকটি item আছে, যেগুলো খুব জনপ্রিয় এবং আপনি আশা করেন যে সেগুলো প্রায়ই read হবে। দুর্ভাগ্যবশত, গত বছর বিপুল traffic-এর কারণে আপনি ProvisionedThroughputExceededException exception পেয়েছিলেন। এই error যাতে আবার না ঘটে, তা প্রতিরোধ করতে আপনি কী করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Kichu popular item bar bar read hocche = hot key problem, tai ProvisionedThroughputExceededException.

DAX DynamoDB er samne fully managed in-memory cache, microsecond latency, code change lage na (PDF p. 473). Hot item gulo cache theke ashe, table e read kome jay, throttle bondho hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RCU onek barale khoroch onek bare, ar hot key ekta partition e pore bole RCU barale-o throttle puro thambe na.
  • C. Sale er jonno database migrate kora onek effort ar risk; DAX diye easily solve hoy.

Keyword: few popular items read often + ProvisionedThroughputExceeded → DAX cache

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is not a good solution to solve the Hot Key problem and it will cost you a lot of money.

B: DynamoDB Accelerator (DAX) is a fully managed, highly available, in-memory cache for DynamoDB that delivers up to 10x performance improvement. It caches the most frequently used data, thus offloading the heavy reads on hot keys off your DynamoDB table, hence preventing the "ProvisionedThroughputExceededException" exception.

C: This requires too much effort and it might not deliver the required performance.

19·5U19-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q5

You have developed a mobile application that uses DynamoDB as its datastore. You want to automate sending welcome emails to new users after they sign up. What is the most efficient way to achieve this?

আপনি একটি mobile application develop করেছেন, যা datastore হিসেবে DynamoDB ব্যবহার করে। নতুন user-রা sign up করার পরে আপনি তাদের welcome email পাঠানো automate করতে চান। এটি অর্জনের সবচেয়ে efficient উপায় কী?

Renamed: CloudWatch Events -> Amazon EventBridge (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

DynamoDB Streams table er protita item-level change (create/update/delete) er ordered stream (PDF p. 475). Stream e Lambda trigger lagale notun user insert holei Lambda real-time e welcome email pathay.

Course e exact ei use case: "React to changes in real-time (welcome email to users)".

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Prati minute pura table scan kora slow ar dami (RCU khay), table boro hole aro kharap; event-driven Streams efficient.
  • B. DynamoDB er direct 'SNS integration' bole kono feature nai; change dhorte Streams + Lambda lage.

Keyword: new user sign up e auto welcome email → DynamoDB Streams + Lambda

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This would work but it wouldn't be a scalable, cheap, or efficient solution.

B: There's no such feature.

C: DynamoDB Streams allows you to capture a time-ordered sequence of item-level modifications in a DynamoDB table. It's integrated with AWS Lambda so that you create triggers that automatically respond to events in real-time.

19·6U19-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q6

To create a serverless API, you should integrate Amazon API Gateway with ......................

একটি serverless API তৈরি করতে আপনার Amazon API Gateway-কে ...................... -এর সাথে integrate করা উচিত।

Correct: C. Why it is right

Serverless API er classic pattern: Client → API Gateway → Lambda → DynamoDB (PDF p. 481). API Gateway + Lambda e kono infrastructure manage korte hoy na (PDF p. 482).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EC2 instance tomake manage korte hoy (patch, scale), tai eta serverless na.
  • B. ELB shudhu traffic distribute kore, nije API banay na; ar pichone EC2 lage, serverless na.

Keyword: serverless API → API Gateway + Lambda

Instructor feedback (English)

A: EC2 instances are not serverless.

B: An ELB does not help create an API on its own.

19·7U19-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q7

When you are using an Edge-Optimized API Gateway, your API Gateway lives in CloudFront Edge Locations across all AWS Regions.

আপনি যখন একটি Edge-Optimized API Gateway ব্যবহার করছেন, তখন আপনার API Gateway সব AWS Regions জুড়ে CloudFront Edge Locations-এ থাকে।

Correct: A. Why it is right

Edge-Optimized (default) endpoint e request CloudFront edge location diye route hoy, latency kome. Kintu API Gateway nije ekta region e-i thake (PDF p. 485).

Tai "sob region er edge location e API Gateway thake" kotha ta False.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Edge location shudhu request route kore (CloudFront diye); API Gateway er deployment ekta region e-i thake.

Keyword: Edge-Optimized = edge diye route, kintu API ekta region e → False

Instructor feedback (English)

A: An Edge-Optimized API Gateway is best for geographically distributed clients. API requests are routed to the nearest CloudFront Edge Location which improves latency. The API Gateway still lives in one AWS Region.

19·8U19-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q8

You are running an application in production that is leveraging DynamoDB as its datastore and is experiencing smooth sustained usage. There is a need to make the application run in development mode as well, where it will experience the unpredictable volume of requests. What is the most cost-effective solution that you recommend?

আপনি production-এ একটি application চালাচ্ছেন, যা datastore হিসেবে DynamoDB ব্যবহার করছে এবং মসৃণ, স্থির (sustained) usage পাচ্ছে। application-টি development mode-এও চালানোর প্রয়োজন আছে, যেখানে এটি request-এর unpredictable পরিমাণের সম্মুখীন হবে। আপনি সবচেয়ে cost-effective কোন solution recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Prod e smooth sustained (predictable) traffic → Provisioned + Auto Scaling, eta sosta. Dev e unpredictable request → On-Demand, capacity plan lage na, use onujayi pay (PDF p. 472).

Ajker note: Nov 2024 e on-demand er dam ~50% komeche, kintu exam er logic (predictable → provisioned, unpredictable → on-demand) ekhono same.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Dev e traffic unpredictable; provisioned capacity hoy beshi set kore khoroch, noyto kom set kore throttle. On-demand dev er jonno better.
  • C. Ulta: prod predictable bole provisioned sosta, ar dev unpredictable bole on-demand.
  • D. Kaj korbe, kintu prod er smooth traffic e on-demand provisioned + auto scaling er cheye beshi dami; most cost-effective na.

Keyword: prod smooth sustained → Provisioned + Auto Scaling; dev unpredictable → On-Demand

Instructor feedback (English)

D: This will work, but it would be extremely expensive as On-Demand Capacity Mode is much more expensive than Provisioned Capacity Mode. As we know production has a smooth sustained curve, Provisioned Capacity Mode with Auto Scaling enabled would be perfect.

19·9U19-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q9

You have an application that is served globally using CloudFront Distribution. You want to authenticate users at the CloudFront Edge Locations instead of authentication requests go all the way to your origins. What should you use to satisfy this requirement?

আপনার একটি application আছে, যা CloudFront Distribution ব্যবহার করে globally serve করা হয়। authentication request আপনার origin পর্যন্ত যাওয়ার বদলে আপনি CloudFront Edge Locations-এ user authenticate করতে চান। এই requirement পূরণ করতে আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Lambda@Edge CloudFront er edge location e code chalay, origin e jaoyar agei (viewer request e) user authenticate kora jay (PDF p. 461). Course er use case list e "User Authentication and Authorization" ache (PDF p. 459).

(Simple JWT check CloudFront Functions diye-o hoy, kintu option e shudhu Lambda@Edge ache.)

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. API Gateway ekta region e API host kore; CloudFront edge e code chalay na.
  • C. DynamoDB database, edge e code ba authentication chalate pare na.
  • D. Global Accelerator AWS network diye traffic route kore (anycast IP); edge e custom code chalay na.

Keyword: authenticate at CloudFront Edge Locations → Lambda@Edge

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Lambda@Edge is a feature of CloudFront that lets you run code closer to your users, which improves performance and reduces latency.

B: API Gateway is a fully managed service that makes it easy to create, publish, maintain, monitor, and secure APIs at any scale. It can't be used to run code globally on CloudFront Edge Locations.

D: AWS Global Accelerator allows you to improve the performance of your users' traffic by up to 60% using AWS global network infrastructure. It can't be used to run code globally on CloudFront Edge Locations.

19·10U19-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q10

The maximum size of an item in a DynamoDB table is ...................

একটি DynamoDB table-এ একটি item-এর maximum size হলো ...................

Correct: C. Why it is right

DynamoDB te ekta item er maximum size 400 KB (PDF p. 470). Boro data (image, file) S3 e rakho, DynamoDB te tar pointer/metadata rakho.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 1 MB DynamoDB item limit na; item max 400 KB.
  • B. 500 KB na, exact limit 400 KB.
  • D. 400 MB onek beshi; limit 400 KB.

Keyword: max item size DynamoDB → 400 KB

19·11U19-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q11

Which AWS service allows you to build Serverless workflows using AWS services (e.g., Lambda) and supports human approval?

কোন AWS service আপনাকে AWS service (যেমন, Lambda) ব্যবহার করে Serverless workflow তৈরি করতে দেয় এবং human approval support করে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Step Functions diye serverless visual workflow banano jay jeta Lambda function ar onno AWS service orchestrate kore: sequence, parallel, condition, timeout, error handling (PDF p. 487).

Slide e clearly bola: "Possibility of implementing human approval feature".

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda ekta function chalay (max 15 min); nije workflow orchestration ba human approval step dey na, eta Step Functions er kaj.
  • B. ECS container chalanor service; serverless workflow ba human approval er tool na.
  • D. Storage Gateway on-premises ke S3/EBS/FSx storage er sathe connect kore; workflow er sathe somporko nai.

Keyword: serverless workflows + human approval → Step Functions

19·12U19-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q12

A company has a serverless application on AWS which consists of Lambda, DynamoDB, and Step Functions. In the last month, there are an increase in the number of requests against the application which results in an increase in DynamoDB costs, and requests started to be throttled. After further investigation, it shows that the majority of requests are read requests against some queries in the DynamoDB table. What do you recommend to prevent throttles and reduce costs efficiently?

একটি company-র AWS-এ একটি serverless application আছে, যা Lambda, DynamoDB এবং Step Functions নিয়ে গঠিত। গত মাসে application-এ request-এর সংখ্যা বেড়েছে, যার ফলে DynamoDB-র খরচ বেড়েছে, এবং request throttle হতে শুরু করেছে। আরও তদন্তে দেখা যায় যে বেশিরভাগ request হলো DynamoDB table-এর কিছু query-র বিপরীতে read request। throttle প্রতিরোধ করতে এবং দক্ষতার সাথে খরচ কমাতে আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Beshirbhag request read, kichu query bar bar → throttle ar cost barche. DAX DynamoDB er jonno fully managed in-memory cache, DynamoDB API compatible tai code logic bodlate hoy na, item ar query/scan result cache kore (PDF p. 473-474).

Cache theke read ashle table e RCU kom lage: throttle bondho + cost kome, kono migration chara.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. EC2 te Redis install korle server nije manage korte hobe (patch, HA), app code-o bodlate hobe; serverless app er jonno extra ops overhead.
  • B. Aurora te migrate kora onek boro kaj ar risk; shudhu read cache er jonno DB bodlano lage na.
  • C. S3 database na, query chalate pare na; DynamoDB theke S3 te migrate kora ei app er jonno thik na.

Keyword: majority read requests + throttled + reduce cost efficiently → DAX

19·13U19-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q13

You are a DevOps engineer in a football company that has a website that is backed by a DynamoDB table. The table stores viewers’ feedback for football matches. You have been tasked to work with the analytics team to generate reports on the viewers’ feedback. The analytics team wants the data in DynamoDB in json format and hosted in an S3 bucket to start working on it and create the reports. What is the best and most cost-effective way to convert DynamoDB data to json files?

আপনি একটি football company-র একজন DevOps engineer, যাদের একটি website আছে, যা একটি DynamoDB table দ্বারা backed। table-টি football match-এর জন্য viewer-দের feedback store করে। viewer-দের feedback-এর উপর report তৈরি করতে analytics team-এর সাথে কাজ করার দায়িত্ব আপনাকে দেওয়া হয়েছে। analytics team DynamoDB-র data json format-এ এবং একটি S3 bucket-এ host করা অবস্থায় চায়, যাতে তারা এটি নিয়ে কাজ শুরু করে report তৈরি করতে পারে। DynamoDB data json file-এ convert করার সবচেয়ে ভালো এবং সবচেয়ে cost-effective উপায় কী?

Correct: A. Why it is right

DynamoDB er native Export to S3 feature (PITR enable thakte hobe) table er data DynamoDB JSON (ba ION) format e S3 e likhe dey (PDF p. 480).

Kono code lage na, ar export table er read capacity khay na, tai cheapest + easiest. Tarpor analytics team Athena diye query korte pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Kaj korbe, kintu Lambda code likhte/maintain korte hobe ar scan e RCU khabe; built-in Export to S3 thakte eta beshi effort ar cost.
  • C. Transfer Family S3/EFS er upor FTP/SFTP interface dey; DynamoDB theke JSON export kore na.
  • D. DataSync NFS/SMB/HDFS/onno storage theke S3/EFS/FSx e file copy kore; DynamoDB table export kore na.

Keyword: DynamoDB data json format e S3 + most cost-effective → Export to S3

19·14U19-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q14

A website is currently in the development process and it is going to be hosted on AWS. There is a requirement to store user sessions for users logged in to the website with an automatic expiry and deletion of expired user sessions. Which of the following AWS services are best suited for this use case?

একটি website বর্তমানে development process-এ আছে এবং এটি AWS-এ host করা হবে। website-এ logged in user-দের user session store করার requirement আছে, সাথে expired user session-এর স্বয়ংক্রিয় expiry এবং deletion। এই use case-এর জন্য নিচের কোন AWS service সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

DynamoDB TTL item e ekta expiry timestamp (epoch) attribute rakhe; time par hole DynamoDB automatically item delete kore (PDF p. 478).

Slide er use case e "web session handling" ache: session table e TTL on korle expired session nije-i muche jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 Lifecycle minimum din hisebe kaj kore (object level), session er minute/ghonta expiry er jonno na; ar S3 session store er moto fast read/write na.
  • B. EC2 local e rakhle instance terminate/scale hole session harabe, ar automatic expiry nai.
  • D. EFS shared file system, kintu file er automatic expiry/delete feature nai; session store hisebe thik na.

Keyword: user sessions + automatic expiry and deletion → DynamoDB TTL

19·15U19-Q15Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q15

You have a mobile application and would like to give your users access to their own personal space in the S3 bucket. How do you achieve that?

আপনার একটি mobile application আছে এবং আপনি আপনার user-দের S3 bucket-এ তাদের নিজস্ব personal space-এ access দিতে চান। আপনি এটি কীভাবে অর্জন করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Cognito Identity Pools (Federated Identity) mobile user ke temporary AWS credentials dey, jate user direct S3 access korte pare (PDF p. 488, 491).

IAM policy te user_id diye customize kora jay (fine-grained), tai protita user shudhu nijer S3 folder/prefix access pay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Prati mobile user er jonno IAM user banano scale kore na, ar long-term credential app e rakha unsafe.
  • C. SAML federation enterprise IdP (jemon Active Directory) er jonno; mobile app er onek user er jonno Cognito best.
  • D. Bucket public korle sobai sob data dekhte parbe: data leak. Never do it.

Keyword: mobile app users + own personal space in S3 → Cognito Identity Federation

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This does not scale well, we cannot create an IAM user per mobile application user.

B: Amazon Cognito can be used to federate mobile user accounts and provide them with their own IAM permissions, so they can be able to access their own personal space in the S3 bucket.

C: SAML Identity Federation is used to integrate an Identity Provider service such as Microsoft Active Directory with AWS. It does not work for mobile applications.

D: Never make an S3 bucket public to your mobile application users. This would result in data leaks! Read horror stories here: https://businessinsights.bitdefender.com/worst-amazon-breaches

19·16U19-Q16Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q16

You are developing a new web and mobile application that will be hosted on AWS and currently, you are working on developing the login and signup page. The application backend is serverless and you are using Lambda, DynamoDB, and API Gateway. Which of the following is the best and easiest approach to configure the authentication for your backend?

আপনি একটি নতুন web ও mobile application develop করছেন, যা AWS-এ host করা হবে এবং বর্তমানে আপনি login ও signup page develop করার কাজ করছেন। application backend serverless এবং আপনি Lambda, DynamoDB ও API Gateway ব্যবহার করছেন। আপনার backend-এর জন্য authentication configure করার সবচেয়ে ভালো এবং সবচেয়ে সহজ পদ্ধতি নিচের কোনটি?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Cognito User Pools = app user der jonno serverless user database: sign-up, sign-in, password reset, email/phone verification, MFA (PDF p. 489).

User Pool API Gateway er sathe integrate kore: user login kore token pay, API Gateway token verify kore Lambda backend e pathay (PDF p. 490). Nijer credential store banano lage na: best ar easiest.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Nijer DynamoDB te credential rakhle login, hashing, reset, MFA sob nije code korte hobe; easiest na, security risk-o beshi.
  • B. S3 credential store na; nijer auth system banate hobe, onek kaj ar insecure.
  • D. Secrets Manager application er secret (DB password, API key) rakhar jonno, end-user sign-up/sign-in system na.

Keyword: login and signup + API Gateway backend + easiest → Cognito User Pools

19·17U19-Q17Udemy quiz · sec 19 · Q17

You are running a mobile application where you want each registered user to upload/download images to/from his own folder in the S3 bucket. Also, you want to give your users to sign-up and sign in using their social media accounts (e.g., Facebook). Which AWS service should you choose?

আপনি একটি mobile application চালাচ্ছেন, যেখানে আপনি চান প্রতিটি registered user S3 bucket-এ তার নিজের folder-এ/folder থেকে image upload/download করুক। এছাড়াও, আপনি আপনার user-দের তাদের social media account (যেমন, Facebook) ব্যবহার করে sign-up ও sign in করার সুযোগ দিতে চান। আপনার কোন AWS service বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Amazon Cognito duita need-i mitay (PDF p. 488-491):

  • User Pool: sign-up/sign-in, Facebook/Google social login (federated identities).
  • Identity Pool: user ke temporary AWS credentials, user_id diye policy, tai nijer S3 folder e upload/download.

Course: "hundreds of users, mobile users" → Cognito, IAM na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. IAM AWS account er user/role er jonno; millions mobile user ke social login dite pare na.
  • B. IAM Identity Center workforce (company employee) er SSO er jonno, mobile app er end user er jonno na.
  • D. CloudFront CDN, content deliver kore; user sign-up ba AWS credential dey na.

Keyword: mobile + sign in with Facebook + own S3 folder → Amazon Cognito

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS IAM enables you to manage access to AWS services and resources securely. It allows you to create and manage AWS users and groups, and use permissions to allow and deny their access to AWS resources. IAM can not be used to federate mobile users.

B: AWS IAM Identity Center makes it easy to centrally manage SSO access to all of your AWS accounts and cloud applications. It helps you manage SSO access and user permissions across all your AWS accounts in AWS Organizations. It also helps when you manage access and permissions to commonly used 3rd party software as a service (SaaS) applications, AWS SSO-integrated applications as well as custom applications that support Security Assertion Markup Language (SAML) 2.0. AWS SSO also includes built-in SAML integrations to many business applications, such as Salesforce, Box, and Office 365.

C: Amazon Cognito lets you add user sign-up, sign-in, and access control to your web and mobile apps quickly and easily. Amazon Cognito scales to millions of users and supports sign-in with social identity providers, such as Apple, Facebook, Google, and Amazon, and enterprise identity providers via SAML 2.0 and OpenID Connect.

D: Amazon CloudFront is a fast content delivery network (CDN) service that securely delivers data, videos, applications, and APIs to customers globally with low latency, high transfer speeds. Amazon CloudFront does not federate mobile users or generate security credentials.

Section 20: Serverless Solution Architecture Discussions (8)

Note: Section 20 note. Answer first, then press Check.

20·1U20-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q1

A startup company plans to run its application on AWS. As a solutions architect, the company hired you to design and implement a fully Serverless REST API. Which technology stack do you recommend?

একটি startup company AWS-এ তার application চালানোর পরিকল্পনা করছে। একটি সম্পূর্ণ Serverless REST API design ও implement করতে company আপনাকে একজন solutions architect হিসেবে নিয়োগ দিয়েছে। আপনি কোন technology stack recommend করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Fully serverless REST API er classic stack = API Gateway + Lambda (data lagle DynamoDB). API Gateway HTTPS REST endpoint dey, Lambda ke invoke kore; duita-i AWS manage kore, auto scale kore, pay per usage.

MyTodoList ar MyBlog.com duitai ei pattern e shuru (PDF p. 496, PDF p. 504).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. EC2 serverless na: instance tumi provision, patch, scale koro. ALB thakleo backend server-based.
  • C. ECS (EC2 launch type) ar EBS volume duita-i server / capacity manage kora jinish, serverless na.
  • D. CloudFront + S3 serverless thik, kintu shudhu static content serve kore; API er dynamic logic chalate pare na.

Keyword: fully Serverless REST API → API Gateway + Lambda

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is fully serverless.

B: EC2 is not serverless.

C: Both ECS and EBS are not serverless.

D: This is serverless but it won't help as you want to create an API. This combination allows you to distribute the static part of your application globally.

20·2U20-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q2

The following AWS services have an out of the box caching feature, EXCEPT .................

নিচের AWS service-গুলোর out of the box caching feature আছে, শুধু ................. ছাড়া (EXCEPT)। অর্থাৎ কোনটির নেই?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Lambda er kono out-of-the-box response cache nai. Tai EXCEPT er uttor Lambda.

  • API Gateway: stage e response caching on kora jay, hit hole Lambda call-i hoy na (MyTodoList, PDF p. 499).
  • DynamoDB: DAX = in-memory cache, microsecond latency (PDF p. 498).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. API Gateway e built-in caching ache: stage level e response TTL porjonto cache kore.
  • C. DynamoDB er built-in cache ache: DAX (DynamoDB Accelerator), same API, read cache kore.

Keyword: out of the box caching, EXCEPT → Lambda (API Gateway cache ache, DynamoDB e DAX ache)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You can enable API caching in Amazon API Gateway to cache your endpoint's responses. With caching, you can reduce the number of calls made to your endpoint and also improve the latency of requests to your API. When you enable caching for a stage, API Gateway caches responses from your endpoint for a specified time-to-live (TTL) period, in seconds. API Gateway then responds to the request by looking up the endpoint response from the cache instead of making a request to your endpoint.

B: Lambda does not have an out-of-the-box caching feature.

C: Amazon DynamoDB Accelerator (DAX) is a fully managed, highly available, in-memory cache for DynamoDB that delivers up to a 10x performance improvement – from milliseconds to microseconds – even at millions of requests per second. DAX does all the heavy lifting required to add in-memory acceleration to your DynamoDB tables, without requiring developers to manage cache invalidation, data population, or cluster management.

20·3U20-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q3

You have a lot of static files stored in an S3 bucket that you want to distribute globally to your users. Which AWS service should you use?

আপনার একটি S3 bucket-এ প্রচুর static file store করা আছে, যেগুলো আপনি আপনার user-দের কাছে globally distribute করতে চান। আপনার কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

S3 bucket ekta region e thake. Sara prithibir user ke druto dite CloudFront (CDN): edge location e static file cache kore, user kachakachi edge theke pay.

MyBlog.com er prothom dhap thik eta: S3 + CloudFront (PDF p. 502).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 CRR pura bucket onno region e copy kore; kaj korbe kintu onek copy, onek region, beshi dam, ar eta CDN na.
  • C. Route 53 shudhu DNS; content distribute ba cache kore na.
  • D. API Gateway API expose kore (dynamic content), S3 er static file global distribution er jonno na.

Keyword: static files in S3 + distribute globally → CloudFront

Instructor feedback (English)

A: S3 CRR enables automatic, asynchronous copying of objects across S3 buckets. This would work but it would be expensive as you'll have multiple copies of data in different S3 buckets around the globe.

B: Amazon CloudFront is a fast content delivery network (CDN) service that securely delivers data, videos, applications, and APIs to customers globally with low latency, high transfer speeds. This is a perfect use case for Amazon CloudFront.

C: Amazon Route 53 is a highly available and scalable Domain Name System (DNS) service.

D: Amazon API Gateway is a fully managed service that makes it easy to create, publish, maintain, monitor, and secure APIs at any scale. You can create REST APIs and WebSocket APIs. It helps you expose dynamic content, not static content from S3.

20·4U20-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q4⚠ outdated

You have created a DynamoDB table in ap-northeast-1 and would like to make it available in eu-west-1, so you decided to create a DynamoDB Global Table. What needs to be enabled first before you create a DynamoDB Global Table?

আপনি ap-northeast-1-এ একটি DynamoDB table তৈরি করেছেন এবং এটিকে eu-west-1-এ available করতে চান, তাই আপনি একটি DynamoDB Global Table তৈরি করার সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছেন। একটি DynamoDB Global Table তৈরি করার আগে প্রথমে কী enable করতে হবে?

⚠ Outdated: Ekhon global table (version 2019.11.21) e replica add korle MREC mode e DynamoDB nijei Streams on kore rakhe (off kora jay na), ar MRSC mode Streams use-i kore na. Tai age theke manually Streams enable korar dorkar nai. Course/exam er answer ekhono 'DynamoDB Streams'. source

Correct: A. Why it is right

Course (Section 19, PDF p. 477) bole: Global Table er pre-requisite holo DynamoDB Streams. Streams table er change log dey, sei change onno region er replica te replicate hoy. Exam answer: DynamoDB Streams.

Ajker din e: current version (2019.11.21) e MREC global table e DynamoDB nijei sob replica te Streams on rakhe (off kora jay na), ar MRSC mode Streams use-i kore na, tai manually age enable korar dorkar nai.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. DAX shudhu read cache; replication er sathe somporko nai.
  • C. DynamoDB te "Versioning" bole kono feature nai (eta S3 er feature).
  • D. Backup (on-demand / PITR) disaster recovery er jonno; Global Table er prerequisite na.

Keyword: enabled first before Global Table → DynamoDB Streams (course answer)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: DynamoDB Streams enable DynamoDB to get a changelog and use that changelog to replicate data across replica tables in other AWS Regions.

B: Amazon DynamoDB Accelerator (DAX) is a fully managed, highly available, in-memory cache for DynamoDB that delivers up to a 10x performance improvement – from milliseconds to microseconds – even at millions of requests per second. DAX does all the heavy lifting required to add in-memory acceleration to your DynamoDB tables, without requiring developers to manage cache invalidation, data population, or cluster management.

C: There's no such feature.

D: Amazon DynamoDB provides the on-demand backup capability to create full backups of your tables for long-term retention and archival for regulatory compliance needs.

20·5U20-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q5

You have configured a Lambda function to run each time an item is added to a DynamoDB table using DynamoDB Streams. The function is meant to insert messages into the SQS queue for further long processing jobs. Each time the Lambda function is invoked, it seems able to read from the DynamoDB Stream but it isn't able to insert the messages into the SQS queue. What do you think the problem is?

আপনি DynamoDB Streams ব্যবহার করে একটি DynamoDB table-এ প্রতিবার একটি item যোগ হলে চলার জন্য একটি Lambda function configure করেছেন। function-টির উদ্দেশ্য হলো পরবর্তী দীর্ঘ processing job-এর জন্য SQS queue-তে message insert করা। প্রতিবার Lambda function invoke হলে মনে হয় এটি DynamoDB Stream থেকে read করতে পারছে, কিন্তু SQS queue-তে message insert করতে পারছে না। আপনার মতে সমস্যাটি কী?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Lambda onno AWS service (SQS, SES, S3...) call kore tar execution IAM role diye. Stream pora kaj korche, tar mane trigger thik; kintu SQS e SendMessage fail → role e SQS permission nai.

MyBlog.com er welcome email flow e o same: Lambda er IAM role e SES allow thakte hoy (PDF p. 506).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda khub bhalo bhabe SQS e message pathate pare (SDK diye); EC2 lage na.
  • C. SQS API call er jonno security group e SQS ke point kora jay na; default Lambda outbound internet access thake, tai SG problem na.
  • D. SQS queue te kono security group attach hoy na; access control hoy IAM / queue policy diye.

Keyword: read from stream works kintu can't insert into SQS → Lambda execution role e permission nai

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS Lambda is a perfect fit with SQS.

C: Security Groups can not point to SQS queues.

D: Security Groups can not be attached to SQS queues.

20·6U20-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q6

You would like to create an architecture for a micro-services application whose sole purpose is to encode videos stored in an S3 bucket and store the encoded videos back into an S3 bucket. You would like to make this micro-services application reliable and has the ability to retry upon failures. Each video may take over 25 minutes to be processed. The services used in the architecture should be asynchronous and should have the capability to be stopped for a day and resume the next day from the videos that haven't been encoded yet. Which of the following AWS services would you recommend in this scenario?

আপনি একটি micro-services application-এর জন্য একটি architecture তৈরি করতে চান, যার একমাত্র উদ্দেশ্য হলো একটি S3 bucket-এ store করা video encode করা এবং encode করা video আবার একটি S3 bucket-এ store করা। আপনি এই micro-services application-কে reliable করতে চান এবং failure হলে retry করার সক্ষমতা রাখতে চান। প্রতিটি video process হতে 25 মিনিটের বেশি সময় লাগতে পারে। architecture-এ ব্যবহৃত service-গুলো asynchronous হওয়া উচিত এবং একদিনের জন্য stop করে পরদিন এখনো encode না হওয়া video থেকে resume করার সক্ষমতা থাকা উচিত। এই scenario-তে নিচের কোন AWS service-গুলো আপনি recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

  • Protita video > 25 min → Lambda bad, karon Lambda max 15 min (Section 19). Tai worker hobe EC2.
  • Async, reliable, retry, ar ekdin bondho rekhe porer din baki gulo theke shuru → SQS: message queue te thake (14 din porjonto), worker na thakleo harabe na; process fail hole message abar visible hoy, retry hoy.

Tai SQS + EC2: microservice er async pattern (PDF p. 511).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 event Lambda ke sathe sathe trigger kore, porer din e postpone kora jay na; ar Lambda 15 min er beshi chalte pare na.
  • B. SNS push kore, message dhore rakhe na; EC2 bondho thakle message hariye jay, queue te ferot diye retry o nai.
  • D. SQS thik, kintu Lambda er max execution time 15 min; 25 min er encoding sesh hobe na.

Keyword: over 25 minutes + retry, stopped for a day and resume → SQS + EC2

Instructor feedback (English)

A: When using a Lambda function with S3, the Lambda function would need to be triggered immediately and can't be postponed until tomorrow to be run.

B: Amazon SNS won't allow us to retry by putting back a message into a queue in case of EC2 failures.

C: Amazon SQS allows you to retain messages for days and process them later, while we can take down our EC2 instances.

D: This would work, but it can't be used in this scenario as the maximum execution time for a Lambda function is 15 minutes.

20·7U20-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q7

You are running a photo-sharing website where your images are downloaded from all over the world. Every month you publish a master pack of beautiful mountain images that are over 15 GB in size. The content is currently hosted on an Elastic File System (EFS) file system and distributed by an Application Load Balancer and a set of EC2 instances. Each month, you are experiencing very high traffic which increases the load on your EC2 instances and increases network costs. What do you recommend to reduce EC2 load and network costs without refactoring your website?

আপনি একটি photo-sharing website চালাচ্ছেন, যেখানে আপনার image-গুলো সারা বিশ্ব থেকে download করা হয়। প্রতি মাসে আপনি সুন্দর mountain image-এর একটি master pack publish করেন, যার size 15 GB-এর বেশি। content বর্তমানে একটি Elastic File System (EFS) file system-এ host করা এবং একটি Application Load Balancer ও EC2 instance-এর একটি set দিয়ে distribute করা হয়। প্রতি মাসে আপনি অনেক বেশি traffic পাচ্ছেন, যা আপনার EC2 instance-এর load বাড়ায় এবং network খরচ বাড়ায়। আপনার website refactor না করে EC2 load এবং network খরচ কমাতে আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Eta "software updates offloading" lecture er hubohu scenario (PDF p. 512-515): EC2 + ALB + EFS theke boro static file, monthly spike, EC2 load ar network cost beshi, ar refactor kora jabe na.

Solution: ALB er shamne CloudFront distribution. File edge e cache hoy, beshi request EC2 porjonto jay na → EC2 load ar network cost kome, app e kono change nai.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 e host korle app / link bodlate hobe (refactor), ar CloudFront chara S3 theke-o network cost beshi thakbe.
  • B. ALB te kono caching feature nai.
  • C. Boro instance = aro EC2 cost; network cost o komche na.

Keyword: static big pack + reduce EC2 load and network costs + without refactoring → CloudFront

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This would work, but it will require some kind of application refactoring. And you still may have high network costs.

B: This feature does not exist.

C: This would include higher costs for EC2 instances, and you still have high network costs.

D: Amazon CloudFront is a fast content delivery network (CDN) service that securely delivers data, videos, applications, and APIs to customers globally with low latency, high transfer speeds. Amazon CloudFront can be used in front of an Application Load Balancer.

20·8U20-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 20 · Q8

An AWS service allows you to capture gigabytes of data per second in real-time and deliver these data to multiple consuming applications, with a replay feature.

একটি AWS service আপনাকে real-time-এ প্রতি সেকেন্ডে gigabytes data capture করতে এবং এই data একাধিক consuming application-এ deliver করতে দেয়, একটি replay feature সহ।

Correct: A. Why it is right

Kinesis Data Streams: real-time e GB/s data capture kore, data retain kore (365 din porjonto, Section 17), tai replay kora jay, ar onek consumer same data porte pare.

Microservice er async pattern e Kinesis o ache (PDF p. 511).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. S3 object storage; real-time stream capture, multiple consumer e deliver, replay er feature nai.
  • C. Amazon MQ managed message broker (ActiveMQ / RabbitMQ), purono protocol er app migrate korte; GB/s real-time streaming ar replay er jonno na.

Keyword: gigabytes per second, real-time + multiple consumers + replay → Kinesis Data Streams

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon Kinesis Data Streams (KDS) is a massively scalable and durable real-time data streaming service. It can continuously capture gigabytes of data per second from hundreds of sources such as website clickstreams, database event streams, financial transactions, social media feeds, IT logs, and location-tracking events.

B: Amazon S3 is an object storage service and does not provide replay features.

C: Amazon MQ is a managed message broker service for Apache ActiveMQ and RabbitMQ that makes it easy to set up and operate message brokers on AWS. It's a replacement for Amazon SQS in case you need to support other protocols such as MQTT.

Section 21: Databases in AWS (10)

Note: Section 21 note. Answer first, then press Check.

21·1U21-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q1

Which database helps you store relational datasets, with SQL language compatibility and the capability of processing transactions such as insert, update, and delete?

কোন database আপনাকে relational dataset store করতে সাহায্য করে, যাতে SQL language compatibility এবং insert, update ও delete-এর মতো transaction process করার capability আছে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Relational dataset + SQL + transaction (insert, update, delete) = RDBMS / OLTP. Course er database type slide e RDBMS (SQL / OLTP) mane RDS ba Aurora (PDF p. 518).

RDS summary (PDF p. 519): managed PostgreSQL / MySQL / Oracle / SQL Server / DB2 / MariaDB, use case "store relational datasets, perform SQL queries, transactions". Tai Amazon RDS.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DocumentDB holo MongoDB-compatible NoSQL document (JSON) database; relational table ar SQL er jonno na.
  • C. DynamoDB key-value / document NoSQL; transaction kora gele-o relational format ar SQL language nai.
  • D. ElastiCache in-memory cache (Redis / Memcached); slide bole SQL use kora jay na, primary relational DB na.

Keyword: relational datasets + SQL + transactions → Amazon RDS

Instructor feedback (English)

C: Amazon DynamoDB is a key-value, document, NoSQL database. It does not have SQL and does not store the data in a relational format, but can be used for transactional processing.

D: Amazon ElastiCache is a caching technology and it doesn't have SQL capabilities.

21·2U21-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q2

Which AWS service provides you with caching capability that is compatible with Redis API?

কোন AWS service আপনাকে Redis API-compatible caching capability দেয়?

Correct: D. Why it is right

ElastiCache = managed Redis / Memcached (PDF p. 521): in-memory data store, sub-millisecond latency. Redis API compatible caching chaile ekmatro uttor eta-i.

Ajke Redis ke docs e "Redis OSS" bole, ar Valkey engine o ache (Section 09); concept ek-i.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS relational database, caching service na, Redis API o bojhe na.
  • B. DynamoDB key-value store hisabe ElastiCache er jaygay bosano jay, kintu Redis API support kore na (nijer API).
  • C. OpenSearch search / indexing service, HTTP API diye kaaj kore; Redis-compatible cache na.

Keyword: caching + Redis API → ElastiCache

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon RDS does not have caching capability and it is based on JDBC and not compatible with Redis API.

B: Amazon DynamoDB can be used as a substitute for Redis but it does not support Redis API.

C: Amazon OpenSearch helps you in indexing and searching through your data. It leverages HTTP for client interactions.

D: Amazon ElastiCache is a fully managed in-memory data store, compatible with Redis or Memcached.

21·3U21-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q3⚠ outdated

You want to migrate an on-premises MongoDB NoSQL database to AWS. You don't want to manage any database servers, so you want to use a managed NoSQL Serverless database, that provides you with high availability, durability, and reliability, and the capability to take your database global. Which database should you choose?

আপনি একটি on-premises MongoDB NoSQL database AWS-এ migrate করতে চান। আপনি কোনো database server manage করতে চান না, তাই একটি managed NoSQL Serverless database ব্যবহার করতে চান, যা আপনাকে high availability, durability ও reliability দেয়, এবং আপনার database-কে global করার capability দেয়। কোন database বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

⚠ Outdated: Explanation bole DocumentDB er serverless ar global feature nai - eta ekhon puran. DocumentDB Global Clusters (2021 theke) ar DocumentDB Serverless (July 2025 GA) dutoi ache, tai MongoDB migration e C (DocumentDB) o ekhon fit kore. Course/exam er answer ekhono DynamoDB. source

Correct: B. Why it is right

Course er answer DynamoDB: chawa holo managed NoSQL Serverless, HA + durability, ar global korar khomota. DynamoDB serverless, default e multi-AZ, ar Global Tables diye active-active multi-region (PDF p. 522).

MongoDB theke DynamoDB te gele data model / code bodlate hobe, kintu question er decisive shobdo "serverless" + "global". Course likhar shomoy DocumentDB e serverless / global option chilo na, tai DynamoDB.

Ajke: DocumentDB e Global Clusters (2021) ar DocumentDB Serverless (July 2025 GA) dutoi ache, tai C-o ekhon fit kore; exam / course key tobuo DynamoDB.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS relational, SQL-based; NoSQL na, serverless o na (instance provision lage).
  • C. Course er explanation: DocumentDB MongoDB-compatible, kintu tokhon serverless ar global feature chilo na. Ajke dutoi ache, tai bastobe defensible; tobu course key DynamoDB.
  • D. Aurora relational (MySQL / PostgreSQL compatible), NoSQL na.

Keyword: managed NoSQL Serverless + take your database global → DynamoDB (Global Tables)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon RDS is a relational, SQL-based database.

B: Amazon DynamoDB is a key-value, document, NoSQL database.

C: Amazon DocumentDB is a NoSQL databases and it might work perfectly here. But the questions states that the database must be Serverless and wants to take the database global. DocumentDB doesn't have a Serverless option and it doesn't have a global database feature.

D: Amazon Aurora is a relational, SQL-based database.

21·4U21-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q4

You are looking to perform Online Transaction Processing (OLTP). You would like to use a database that has built-in auto-scaling capabilities and provides you with the maximum number of replicas for its underlying storage. What AWS service do you recommend?

আপনি Online Transaction Processing (OLTP) করতে চাইছেন। আপনি এমন একটি database ব্যবহার করতে চান, যাতে built-in auto-scaling capability আছে এবং যা তার underlying storage-এর জন্য সর্বোচ্চ সংখ্যক replica দেয়। আপনি কোন AWS service recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Aurora storage e data 6 replica, 3 AZ te (bodlano jay na), self-healing, storage auto-scaling; read replica o auto-scale hoy (PDF p. 520). Tai "OLTP + built-in auto-scaling + underlying storage er maximum replica" → Aurora.

Aurora PostgreSQL / MySQL compatible relational DB, tai OLTP er jonno thik.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ElastiCache in-memory cache, OLTP database na, SQL nai.
  • B. Neptune graph database; highly connected data er jonno, OLTP relational workload er jonno na.
  • D. RDS e built-in storage replication ei rokom nai: read replica ar Multi-AZ nije enable korte hoy, data 6 copy hoy na.

Keyword: OLTP + built-in auto-scaling + maximum replicas for storage → Aurora (6 copies, 3 AZ)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon ElastiCache is an in-memory data store, provides you with caching capabilities. It is compatible with Redis and Memcached. It does not support OLTP.

C: Amazon Aurora is a MySQL and PostgreSQL-compatible relational database. It features a distributed, fault-tolerant, self-healing storage system that auto-scales up to 128TB per database instance. It delivers high performance and availability with up to 15 low-latency read replicas, point-in-time recovery, continuous backup to Amazon S3, and replication across 3 AZs.

D: Amazon RDS does not have built-in auto-scaling and data replication capabilities. You need to create RDS Read Replicas and enable Multi-AZ.

21·5U21-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q5

As a Solutions Architect, a startup company asked you for help as they are working on an architecture for a social media website where users can be friends with each other, and like each other's posts. The company plan on performing some complicated queries such as "What are the number of likes on the posts that have been posted by the friends of Mike?". Which database do you recommend?

একজন Solutions Architect হিসেবে, একটি startup company আপনার সাহায্য চেয়েছে, কারণ তারা একটি social media website-এর architecture নিয়ে কাজ করছে, যেখানে user-রা একে অপরের friend হতে পারবে এবং একে অপরের post like করতে পারবে। company কিছু জটিল query চালানোর পরিকল্পনা করছে, যেমন "What are the number of likes on the posts that have been posted by the friends of Mike?"। আপনি কোন database recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Friends, likes, posts sob ekta arektar shathe jora = graph dataset. "Mike er friend der post e koto like" = graph traversal query.

Neptune fully managed graph DB, highly connected dataset er complex query er jonno optimized, billions relation ms e query; use case social networking (PDF p. 525).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS diye join kore kora jay, kintu onek hop er relationship query kothin ar slow; graph DB beshi uposhukto.
  • B. QLDB immutable financial ledger, relationship query er jonno na.
  • D. OpenSearch free text / unstructured search er jonno; kaaj chalano jay kintu graph traversal er jonno banano na.

Keyword: social media, friends, likes, complex relationship query → Neptune

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This would work, but a graph database is better suited.

C: Amazon Neptune is a fast, reliable, fully-managed graph database service that makes it easy to build and run applications that work with highly connected datasets.

D: This would work, but a graph database is better suited.

21·6U21-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q6

You have a set of files, 100MB each, that you want to store in a reliable and durable key-value store. Which AWS service do you recommend?

আপনার কাছে কিছু file আছে, প্রতিটি 100MB, যেগুলো আপনি একটি reliable ও durable key-value store-এ store করতে চান। আপনি কোন AWS service recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

S3 = object er key-value store (key = bucket e object er puro path). Boro object er jonno great, serverless, infinitely scale, durable (PDF p. 523). 100 MB file rakhar jonno sothik.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Aurora relational database; boro file (BLOB) rakhar jonno na.
  • C. DynamoDB item max 400 KB, tai 100 MB file rakha jay na; choto document (100s KB) er jonno.
  • D. ElastiCache in-memory cache, durable na, boro object rakhar jonno na.

Keyword: files, 100 MB each + durable key-value store → S3

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Amazon S3 is indeed a key-value store! (where the key is the full path of the object in the bucket)

C: Amazon DynamoDB can not be used to store big objects. The maximum item size in DynamoDB is 400KB.

D: Amazon ElastiCache is a fully managed in-memory data store, compatible with Redis or Memcached. It can not be used to store big objects, in addition to it is not durable.

21·7U21-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q7

A company has an on-premises website that uses ReactJS as its frontend, NodeJS as its backend, and MongoDB for the database. There are some issues with the self-hosted MongoDB database as there is a lot of maintenance required and they don’t have and can’t afford the resources or experience to handle those issues. So, a decision was made to migrate the website to AWS. They have decided to host the frontend ReactJS application in an S3 bucket and the NodeJS backend on a set of EC2 instances. Which AWS service can they use to migrate the MongoDB database that provides them with high scalability and availability without making any code changes?

একটি company-র একটি on-premises website আছে, যা frontend হিসেবে ReactJS, backend হিসেবে NodeJS এবং database হিসেবে MongoDB ব্যবহার করে। self-hosted MongoDB database-এ কিছু সমস্যা আছে, কারণ অনেক maintenance লাগে, এবং এই সমস্যাগুলো handle করার মতো resource বা অভিজ্ঞতা তাদের নেই এবং তার খরচও তারা বহন করতে পারবে না। তাই website-টি AWS-এ migrate করার সিদ্ধান্ত নেওয়া হয়েছে। তারা frontend ReactJS application একটি S3 bucket-এ এবং NodeJS backend কিছু EC2 instance-এ host করার সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছে। কোনো code change না করেই high scalability ও availability দেয় এমনভাবে MongoDB database migrate করতে তারা কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করতে পারে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

DocumentDB holo MongoDB er jonno AWS er "Aurora": MongoDB-compatible, fully managed, 3 AZ replication, storage auto-grow, millions req/s porjonto scale (PDF p. 524).

MongoDB-compatible bole NodeJS backend er MongoDB driver / query ek-i thake: code change lage na, ar maintenance AWS er.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ElastiCache Redis / Memcached cache; MongoDB compatible na, primary database o na.
  • C. "RDS for MongoDB" bole kono service nai; RDS shudhu relational engine (PostgreSQL, MySQL, MariaDB, Oracle, SQL Server, DB2).
  • D. Neptune graph database; MongoDB document model ar API er shathe compatible na.

Keyword: MongoDB migrate + no code changes → DocumentDB

21·8U21-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q8

A company using a self-hosted on-premises Apache Cassandra database which they want to migrate to AWS. Which AWS service can they use which provides them with a fully managed, highly available, and scalable Apache Cassandra database?

একটি company একটি self-hosted on-premises Apache Cassandra database ব্যবহার করছে, যা তারা AWS-এ migrate করতে চায়। কোন AWS service তারা ব্যবহার করতে পারে, যা তাদের একটি fully managed, highly available ও scalable Apache Cassandra database দেয়?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Amazon Keyspaces = managed Apache Cassandra-compatible database: serverless, scalable, highly available, 3 copy across AZ, CQL diye query (PDF p. 527). Course: "Apache Cassandra dekhle Keyspaces".

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DocumentDB MongoDB-compatible, Cassandra na.
  • B. DynamoDB NoSQL ar serverless, kintu Cassandra / CQL compatible na; migrate korte code bodlate hobe.
  • C. Timestream time series database, Cassandra-compatible na.

Keyword: Apache Cassandra fully managed → Amazon Keyspaces

21·9U21-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q9⚠ outdated

An online payment company is using AWS to host its infrastructure. Due to the application’s nature, they have a strict requirement to store an accurate record of financial transactions such as credit and debit transactions. Those transactions must be stored in secured, immutable, encrypted storage which can be cryptographically verified. Which AWS service is best suited for this use case?

একটি online payment company তাদের infrastructure host করতে AWS ব্যবহার করছে। application-এর প্রকৃতির কারণে তাদের একটি কঠোর requirement আছে: credit ও debit transaction-এর মতো financial transaction-এর একটি নির্ভুল record store করতে হবে। এই transaction-গুলো secured, immutable, encrypted storage-এ store করতে হবে, যা cryptographically verify করা যায়। এই use case-এর জন্য কোন AWS service সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত (best suited)?

⚠ Outdated: Amazon QLDB 31 July 2025 e end of support (full shutdown) hoye geche, ekhon ar use kora jay na; AWS ledger/audit use case er jonno Aurora PostgreSQL e migrate korte bole. Course/exam er answer ekhono QLDB ('immutable, cryptographically verifiable' = QLDB). source

Correct: C. Why it is right

QLDB = ledger database: fully managed, serverless, 3 AZ. Journal e protita change er cryptographic hash hoy, tai data immutable ar je keu cryptographically verify korte pare kichu delete / modify hoy ni. Financial transaction er jonno course er answer.

Ajke: QLDB 31 July 2025 e end of support (full shutdown); AWS ledger / audit er jonno Aurora PostgreSQL e migrate korte bole. Course / exam answer tobuo QLDB.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DocumentDB JSON document DB; immutable journal ba cryptographic verification nai.
  • B. Aurora relational DB; data update / delete kora jay, built-in cryptographic verification nai.
  • D. Neptune graph DB, relationship query er jonno; ledger na.

Keyword: financial transactions + immutable + cryptographically verified → QLDB

21·10U21-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 21 · Q10⚠ outdated

A startup is working on developing a new project to reduce forest fires due to climate change. The startup is developing sensors that will be spread across the entire forest to make some readings such as temperature, humidity, and pressures which will help detect the forest fires before it happens. They are going to have thousands of sensors that are going to store a lot of readings each second. There is a requirement to store those readings and do fast analytics so they can predict if there is a fire. Which AWS service can they use to store those readings?

একটি startup climate change-এর কারণে হওয়া বনের আগুন (forest fire) কমাতে একটি নতুন project তৈরির কাজ করছে। startup এমন sensor তৈরি করছে, যেগুলো পুরো বন জুড়ে ছড়িয়ে রাখা হবে এবং temperature, humidity ও pressure-এর মতো কিছু reading নেবে, যা forest fire ঘটার আগেই তা detect করতে সাহায্য করবে। তাদের হাজার হাজার sensor থাকবে, যেগুলো প্রতি সেকেন্ডে অনেক reading store করবে। এই reading-গুলো store করে দ্রুত analytics করার requirement আছে, যাতে তারা আগুন লাগবে কিনা predict করতে পারে। এই reading-গুলো store করতে তারা কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করতে পারে?

⚠ Outdated: Amazon Timestream for LiveAnalytics 20 June 2025 theke new customer der jonno bondho; new user der jonno Timestream for InfluxDB ache. Time-series data = Timestream, course/exam er answer ekhono eta-i. source

Correct: A. Why it is right

Sensor er reading (temperature, humidity, pressure) protita second e = time series data. Timestream serverless time series DB: trillions events/day, recent data memory te, built-in time series analytics function diye near real-time pattern; use case IoT, real-time analytics (PDF p. 528).

Ajke: Timestream for LiveAnalytics 20 June 2025 theke notun customer der jonno bondho; notun user der jonno Timestream for InfluxDB. Exam answer tobuo Timestream.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Neptune graph database; sensor reading er time series data ar fast time-based analytics er jonno na.
  • C. S3 e store kora jay, kintu fast time series analytics database na.
  • D. ElastiCache in-memory cache, durable long-term store na, analytics o na.

Keyword: thousands of sensors, readings each second, fast analytics → Timestream
