22·1U22-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q1

You would like to have a database that is efficient at performing analytical queries on large sets of columnar data. You would like to connect to this Data Warehouse using a reporting and dashboard tool such as Amazon QuickSight. Which AWS technology do you recommend?

আপনি এমন একটি database চান, যা columnar data-র বড় set-এর ওপর analytical query চালাতে efficient। আপনি Amazon QuickSight-এর মতো একটি reporting ও dashboard tool দিয়ে এই Data Warehouse-এ connect করতে চান। আপনি কোন AWS technology recommend করবেন?

Renamed: Amazon QuickSight -> Amazon Quick Sight (part of Amazon Quick) (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

Redshift holo AWS er OLAP data warehouse: data columnar hisabe rakhe ar parallel query engine diye boro dataset e analytical query druto chalay (PDF p. 534).

QuickSight / Tableau er moto BI tool Redshift er sathe directly integrate kore, tai dashboard er jonno eta-i fit.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. RDS holo OLTP (transaction) er relational DB, row based; boro analytical / columnar query er data warehouse na.
  • B. S3 object storage, nije query engine na; S3 er data query korte Athena / Spectrum lage.
  • D. Neptune graph database (relationship, social network), columnar analytics warehouse na.

Keyword: analytical queries + columnar + Data Warehouse + QuickSight → Redshift

22·2U22-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q2

You have a lot of log files stored in an S3 bucket that you want to perform a quick analysis, if possible Serverless, to filter the logs and find users that attempted to make an unauthorized action. Which AWS service allows you to do so?

একটি S3 bucket-এ আপনার অনেক log file store করা আছে, যেগুলোর ওপর আপনি একটি দ্রুত analysis করতে চান, সম্ভব হলে Serverless-ভাবে, যাতে log filter করে unauthorized action করার চেষ্টা করা user-দের খুঁজে বের করা যায়। কোন AWS service আপনাকে তা করতে দেয়?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Athena serverless query service: S3 e rakha file (log, CSV, JSON, Parquet) e directly standard SQL chalay, kono server ba database provision lage na, data move korte hoy na (PDF p. 531).

Course er hands-on e S3 access log e Athena diye 403 (unauthorized) request khoja hoyechilo: ei proshner hubohu scenario.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DynamoDB NoSQL database; S3 er log query korte age DynamoDB te load korte hobe, quick analysis na.
  • B. Redshift e cluster (ba serverless warehouse) + data load / Spectrum lage; quick serverless log analysis er jonno Athena shohoj.
  • C. S3 Glacier archive storage class; ekhane query engine nai, data restore korte o shomoy lage.

Keyword: log files in S3 + quick analysis + Serverless → Athena

22·3U22-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q3

As a Solutions Architect, you have been instructed you to prepare a disaster recovery plan for a Redshift cluster. What should you do?

একজন Solutions Architect হিসেবে আপনাকে একটি Redshift cluster-এর জন্য একটি disaster recovery plan প্রস্তুত করতে নির্দেশ দেওয়া হয়েছে। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Redshift DR er course pattern: automated snapshot on koro (point-in-time, incremental, S3 e), ar Redshift ke configure koro snapshot automatically onno AWS Region e copy korte. Disaster hole oi Region e copied snapshot theke notun cluster restore (PDF p. 536).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Multi-AZ (kichu cluster type e, RA3) shudhu ek Region er AZ failure dhake; puro Region down hole kaaj kore na, tai full DR plan na.
  • C. "Redshift Global cluster" bole kono feature nai (Aurora Global Database er sathe gulano distractor).

Keyword: disaster recovery plan for Redshift → automated snapshot + cross-region snapshot copy

22·4U22-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q4

Which feature in Redshift forces all COPY and UNLOAD traffic moving between your cluster and data repositories through your VPCs?

Redshift-এর কোন feature আপনার cluster এবং data repository-গুলোর মধ্যে চলাচলকারী সব COPY ও UNLOAD traffic-কে আপনার VPC-র মধ্য দিয়ে যেতে বাধ্য করে?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Enhanced VPC Routing on korle Redshift er COPY / UNLOAD traffic (cluster ↔ S3 er moto data repository) internet diye na giye tomar VPC er moddhe diye jay (PDF p. 537).

Off thakle traffic internet diye jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. "Improved VPC Routing" naam e kono Redshift feature nai; naam milano trap.
  • C. Redshift Spectrum S3 er data load na kore query kore; traffic routing er sathe somporko nai.

Keyword: COPY and UNLOAD traffic through your VPC → Enhanced VPC Routing

22·5U22-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q5

You are running a gaming website that is using DynamoDB as its data store. Users have been asking for a search feature to find other gamers by name, with partial matches if possible. Which AWS technology do you recommend to implement this feature?

আপনি একটি gaming website চালাচ্ছেন, যা data store হিসেবে DynamoDB ব্যবহার করছে। user-রা নাম দিয়ে অন্য gamer-দের খুঁজে পেতে একটি search feature চাইছেন, সম্ভব হলে partial match-সহ। এই feature implement করতে আপনি কোন AWS technology recommend করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

DynamoDB te query shudhu primary key ba index diye; naam er ongsho diye khoja (partial match) jay na. OpenSearch je kono field e search kore, partial match o (PDF p. 539).

Course pattern: DynamoDB table → DynamoDB Stream → Lambda → OpenSearch; app OpenSearch e search kore item ID pay, tarpor DynamoDB theke full item ane (PDF p. 540).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DynamoDB nije shudhu key / index diye query kore; free-text partial match search dite pare na (Scan kora slow + expensive).
  • B. Redshift analytics data warehouse; app er real-time name search er jonno na.
  • D. Neptune graph database (relationship query); text search / partial match er tool na.

Keyword: search feature + partial matches + DynamoDB → OpenSearch

22·6U22-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q6

An AWS service allows you to create, run, and monitor ETL (extract, transform, and load) jobs in a few clicks.

একটি AWS service আপনাকে কয়েকটি click-এ ETL (extract, transform, and load) job তৈরি, চালানো ও monitor করতে দেয়।

Correct: A. Why it is right

AWS Glue fully serverless, managed ETL (extract, transform, load) service (PDF p. 548). Glue Studio GUI diye ETL job create, run ar monitor kora jay (PDF p. 551): "few clicks" er mane eta.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Redshift data warehouse (ETL er destination hote pare), ETL job banano / chalano service na.
  • C. RDS relational OLTP database; ETL job create / run kore na.
  • D. DynamoDB NoSQL database; ETL service na.

Keyword: create, run, and monitor ETL jobs → AWS Glue

22·7U22-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q7

A company is using AWS to host its public websites and internal applications. Those different websites and applications generate a lot of logs and traces. There is a requirement to centrally store those logs and efficiently search and analyze those logs in real-time for detection of any errors and if there is a threat. Which AWS service can help them efficiently store and analyze logs?

একটি company তাদের public website ও internal application host করতে AWS ব্যবহার করছে। এই বিভিন্ন website ও application প্রচুর log ও trace তৈরি করে। এই log-গুলো centrally store করার এবং কোনো error ও threat আছে কিনা detect করতে real-time-এ efficiently search ও analyze করার requirement আছে। কোন AWS service তাদের efficiently log store ও analyze করতে সাহায্য করতে পারে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

OpenSearch log ar trace index kore, je kono field e druto search + analyze kore, ar OpenSearch Dashboards diye visualize kore (PDF p. 539). CloudWatch Logs / Firehose theke real time ba near real time e ingest kora jay (PDF p. 541-542).

Tai "centrally store + efficiently search + analyze real time (error / threat)" = OpenSearch.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 log store korte pare, kintu nije search / real-time analysis kore na; query er jonno alada service lage.
  • C. ElastiCache in-memory cache (Redis / Memcached); log store + search er jonno na.
  • D. QLDB immutable ledger database (ar ekhon end of support); log search / analytics tool na.

Keyword: centrally store logs + search and analyze in real-time → OpenSearch

22·8U22-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q8

……………………….. makes it easy and cost-effective for data engineers and analysts to run applications built using open source big data frameworks such as Apache Spark, Hive, or Presto without having to operate or manage clusters.

……………………….. data engineer ও analyst-দের জন্য cluster operate বা manage না করেই Apache Spark, Hive, বা Presto-র মতো open source big data framework ব্যবহার করে তৈরি application চালানো সহজ ও cost-effective করে।

Correct: B. Why it is right

Amazon EMR (Elastic MapReduce) open-source big data framework Apache Spark, Hive, Presto (aro HBase, Flink) chalay; provisioning ar configuration EMR nije kore (PDF p. 543).

"Without having to operate or manage clusters" wording ta EMR Serverless (June 2022 GA, course e nai) er sathe mele; answer EMR-i.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Lambda event-driven function (max 15 min); Spark / Hive / Presto er moto big data framework chalay na.
  • C. Athena serverless SQL on S3 (Presto based), kintu Spark / Hive application chalanor platform na.
  • D. OpenSearch search / log analytics engine; Spark / Hive / Presto application chalay na.

Keyword: Apache Spark, Hive, Presto big data frameworks → EMR

22·9U22-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q9

An e-commerce company has all its historical data such as orders, customers, revenues, and sales for the previous years hosted on a Redshift cluster. The DevOps team is assigned to find an AWS service that can help define those dashboards and have native integration with Redshift. Which AWS service is best suited?

একটি e-commerce company-র আগের বছরগুলোর order, customer, revenue ও sales-এর মতো সব historical data একটি Redshift cluster-এ hosted। আগের বছরগুলোর revenue এবং মোট sales দেখায় এমন কিছু dashboard ও report তৈরি করার requirement আছে, যাতে পরের বছরের requirement নির্ধারণ করা সহজ হয়। DevOps team-কে এমন একটি AWS service খুঁজে বের করার দায়িত্ব দেওয়া হয়েছে, যা এই dashboard-গুলো define করতে সাহায্য করতে পারে এবং Redshift-এর সাথে native integration আছে। কোন AWS service সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত (best suited)?

Renamed: Amazon QuickSight -> Amazon Quick Sight (part of Amazon Quick) (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

QuickSight serverless, ML-powered BI service: interactive dashboard ar report banay (PDF p. 545). Redshift er sathe native integration ache (RDS, Aurora, Athena, S3 o) (PDF p. 546).

Ekhon naam Amazon Quick Sight (Amazon Quick er BI feature); kaaj ek-i.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. OpenSearch search / log analytics (OpenSearch Dashboards shudhu OpenSearch data er jonno); Redshift business dashboard er tool na.
  • B. Athena S3 e serverless SQL chalay; dashboard / visualization banay na (QuickSight er data source hoy).
  • D. EMR big data processing cluster (Spark / Hadoop); BI dashboard tool na.

Keyword: dashboards and reports + native integration with Redshift → QuickSight

22·10U22-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q10

Which AWS Glue feature allows you to save and track the data that has already been processed during a previous run of a Glue ETL job?

AWS Glue-এর কোন feature একটি Glue ETL job-এর আগের run-এ ইতিমধ্যে process হওয়া data save ও track করতে দেয়?

Glue Job Bookmarks previous run e kon data already process hoyeche sheta track kore, tai notun run e purono data abar process hoy na (PDF p. 551).

  • C. Glue Streaming ETL stream (KDS, Kafka, MSK) theke continuous ETL chalay; processed data er state rakha er feature na.
  • D. Glue DataBrew pre-built transformation diye data clean / normalize kore; previous run track kore na.

Keyword: track data already processed during a previous run → Glue Job Bookmarks

22·11U22-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q11

You are a DevOps engineer in a machine learning company which 3 TB of JSON files stored in an S3 bucket. There’s a requirement to do some analytics on those files using Amazon Athena and you have been tasked to find a way to convert those files’ format from JSON to Apache Parquet. Which AWS service is best suited?

আপনি একটি machine learning company-র DevOps engineer, which 3 TB JSON file একটি S3 bucket-এ store করা আছে। Amazon Athena ব্যবহার করে এই file-গুলোর ওপর কিছু analytics করার requirement আছে, এবং আপনাকে এই file-গুলোর format JSON থেকে Apache Parquet-এ convert করার উপায় খুঁজে বের করার দায়িত্ব দেওয়া হয়েছে। কোন AWS service সবচেয়ে উপযুক্ত (best suited)?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Glue ETL job S3 er JSON / CSV pore Apache Parquet (columnar) e convert kore output bucket e rakhe (PDF p. 549). Parquet e Athena shudhu dorkari column scan kore, tai query sosta ar druto (PDF p. 532).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 Versioning object er purono version rakhe; file format convert kore na.
  • B. Kinesis Data Streams real-time streaming ingest; S3 e already rakha 3 TB file er format convert er tool na.
  • C. Amazon MSK managed Kafka streaming; JSON → Parquet conversion kore na.

Keyword: convert JSON to Apache Parquet for Athena → AWS Glue (ETL)

22·12U22-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q12

You have an on-premises application that is used together with an on-premises Apache Kafka to receive a stream of clickstream events from multiple websites. You have been tasked to migrate this application as soon as possible without any code changes. You decided to host the application on an EC2 instance. What is the best option you recommend to migrate Apache Kafka?

আপনার একটি on-premises application আছে, যা একাধিক website থেকে clickstream event-এর একটি stream receive করতে একটি on-premises Apache Kafka-র সাথে ব্যবহৃত হয়। আপনাকে কোনো code change ছাড়াই যত দ্রুত সম্ভব এই application migrate করার দায়িত্ব দেওয়া হয়েছে। আপনি application-টি একটি EC2 instance-এ host করার সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছেন। Apache Kafka migrate করতে আপনি কোন option-টিকে সবচেয়ে ভালো (best) হিসেবে recommend করবেন?

Renamed: Kinesis Data Analytics -> Amazon Managed Service for Apache Flink (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

Amazon MSK = fully managed Apache Kafka AWS e (PDF p. 556). App Kafka API-i use korte thakbe, tai code change lage na; Kafka broker / Zookeeper MSK manage kore, migration druto.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Kinesis Data Streams Kafka er alternative, kintu API alada; app ke Kinesis SDK e rewrite korte hobe, "no code change" bhange.
  • B. Glue ETL service; Kafka broker hisabe kaaj kore na (Glue Streaming ETL Kafka theke porte pare matro).
  • D. Kinesis Data Analytics (ekhon Managed Service for Apache Flink) stream analyze kore, Kafka cluster replace kore na.

Keyword: on-premises Apache Kafka + without any code changes → Amazon MSK

22·13U22-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 22 · Q13

You have data stored in RDS, S3 buckets and you are using AWS Lake Formation as a data lake to collect, move and catalog data so you can do some analytics. You have a lot of big data and ML engineers in the company and you want to control access to part of the data as it might contain sensitive information. What can you use?

আপনার data RDS ও S3 bucket-এ store করা আছে, এবং আপনি data collect, move ও catalog করার জন্য data lake হিসেবে AWS Lake Formation ব্যবহার করছেন, যাতে কিছু analytics করতে পারেন। company-তে আপনার অনেক big data ও ML engineer আছেন, এবং আপনি data-র একটি অংশে access নিয়ন্ত্রণ করতে চান, কারণ এতে sensitive information থাকতে পারে। আপনি কী ব্যবহার করতে পারেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Lake Formation er boro shubidha: Fine-grained Access Control, row ar column-level security, ek jaygay (centralized) (PDF p. 552, 554). Sensitive column / row ke dekhbe sheta Lake Formation e set korle Athena, QuickSight, EMR shob service e kaaj kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Cognito app user er login / identity (user pool, identity pool); data lake er row / column permission dey na.
  • C. AWS Shield DDoS protection; data access control na.
  • D. S3 Object Lock WORM (delete / overwrite atkay); ke data porte parbe sheta control kore na.

Keyword: Lake Formation + control access to part of the data (sensitive) → Fine-grained Access Control

Real-time analytics on streams = Kinesis Data Analytics, jar naam ekhon Amazon Managed Service for Apache Flink (PDF p. 555). Eta Kinesis Data Streams ba MSK theke pore Flink application diye real time e transform / analyze kore.

Aaj: purono "Kinesis Data Analytics for SQL" discontinued; real-time stream analytics er jonno Managed Service for Apache Flink, exam answer ei service-i.

  • B. SNS pub/sub notification; data analyze kore na.

23·1U23-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q1

You should use Amazon Transcribe to turn text into lifelike speech using deep learning.

deep learning ব্যবহার করে text-কে প্রাণবন্ত (lifelike) speech-এ পরিণত করতে আপনার Amazon Transcribe ব্যবহার করা উচিত।

Correct: B. Why it is right

Statement ta False. Amazon Transcribe kore ulta kaaj: speech → text (ASR) (PDF p. 566).

Text ke lifelike speech e bodlay Amazon Polly (deep learning, "applications that talk") (PDF p. 567). Mone rakho: Transcribe shune likhe, Polly lekha pore shunay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Transcribe text → speech kore na; eta speech → text. Text → lifelike speech holo Polly er kaaj.

Keyword: text into lifelike speech → Polly, Transcribe na → False

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon Transcribe is an AWS service that makes it easy for customers to convert speech-to-text. Amazon Polly is a service that turns text into lifelike speech.

B: Amazon Transcribe is an AWS service that makes it easy for customers to convert speech-to-text. Amazon Polly is a service that turns text into lifelike speech.

23·2U23-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q2

A company would like to implement a chatbot that will convert speech-to-text and recognize the customers' intentions. What service should it use?

একটি company একটি chatbot implement করতে চায়, যা speech-to-text convert করবে এবং customer-দের intention চিনবে। কোন service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Amazon Lex (Alexa er same technology) e duita jinish ek sathe: ASR (speech → text) ar Natural Language Understanding diye text / caller er intent chena (PDF p. 570).

Ei duita mile-i chatbot / call center bot banano jay, tai Lex.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Transcribe speech → text kore, kintu NLU nai, customer er intention bujhe na.
  • B. Rekognition image ar video analyze kore; speech ba intent er sathe kaaj nai.
  • C. Connect cloud contact center (call receive, contact flow); intent bujhar kaaj Lex ke diye korano hoy, Connect nije kore na.

Keyword: chatbot + speech-to-text + recognize intentions → Lex

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon Transcribe is an AWS service that makes it easy for customers to convert speech-to-text. However, it does not have natural language understanding.

B: Amazon Rekognition makes it easy to add image and video analysis to your applications using proven, highly scalable, deep learning technology that requires no machine learning expertise to use.

C: Amazon Connect is a self-service, cloud-based contact center service that makes it easy for any business to deliver better customer service at lower cost. It does not provide speech-to-text conversion or natural language understanding.

D: Amazon Lex is a service for building conversational interfaces into any application using voice and text. Lex provides the advanced deep learning functionalities of automatic speech recognition (ASR) for converting speech to text, and natural language understanding (NLU) to recognize the intent of the text, to enable you to build applications with highly engaging user experiences and lifelike conversational interactions.

23·10U23-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q10

A company is managing an image and video sharing platform which is used by customers around the globe. The platform is running on AWS using an S3 bucket to host both images and videos and using CloudFront as the CDN to deliver content to customers all over the world with low latency. In the last couple of months, a lot of customers have complained that they have started to see inappropriate content on the platform which started to increase in the last week. It will be very expensive and time-consuming to manually approve those images and videos by employees before its published on the platform. There is a requirement to find a solution that can automatically detect inappropriate and offensive images and videos and give you the ability to set a minimum confidence threshold for items that will be flagged and allows for manual review. Which AWS service can fit the requirement?

একটি company একটি image ও video sharing platform manage করছে, যা সারা বিশ্বের customer-রা ব্যবহার করেন। platform-টি AWS-এ চলছে, image ও video দুটোই host করতে একটি S3 bucket ব্যবহার করছে, এবং low latency-তে সারা বিশ্বের customer-দের কাছে content deliver করতে CDN হিসেবে CloudFront ব্যবহার করছে। গত কয়েক মাসে অনেক customer অভিযোগ করেছেন যে তারা platform-এ inappropriate content দেখতে শুরু করেছেন, যা গত সপ্তাহে বাড়তে শুরু করেছে। platform-এ publish হওয়ার আগে employee-দের দিয়ে manually এই image ও video approve করানো খুব ব্যয়বহুল ও সময়সাপেক্ষ হবে। এমন একটি solution খুঁজে বের করার requirement আছে, যা automatically inappropriate ও offensive image ও video detect করতে পারে, flag করা item-গুলোর জন্য একটি minimum confidence threshold সেট করার সক্ষমতা দেয়, এবং manual review-এর সুযোগ দেয়। কোন AWS service এই requirement-এর সাথে মানানসই?

Correct: D. Why it is right

Amazon Rekognition Content Moderation image ar video te inappropriate, unwanted, offensive content automatically detect kore; social media / e-commerce e safer user experience (PDF p. 565).

  • Minimum Confidence Threshold set kora jay, tar upore hole item flagged.
  • Flagged item Amazon Augmented AI (A2I) e optional manual review.

Tai employee diye shob manually approve korar expensive kaaj lagbe na. (A2I ekhon notun customer nay na, kintu exam answer Rekognition.)

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Polly text → speech; image / video moderation kore na.
  • B. Translate bhasha anubad kore; offensive image detect kore na.
  • C. Lex chatbot banay; image / video analysis kore na.

Keyword: automatically detect inappropriate images and videos + minimum confidence threshold + manual review → Rekognition

23·11U23-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q11

An online medical company that allows you to book an appointment with doctors using through a phone call is using AWS to host their infrastructure. They are using Amazon Connect and Amazon Lex to receive calls and create a workflow, book an appointment, and pay. According to the company’s policy, all calls must be recorded for review. But, there is a requirement to remove any Personally Identifiable Information (PII) from the call before it's saved. What do you recommend to use which helps in removing PII from calls?

একটি online medical company, যা phone call-এর মাধ্যমে ডাক্তারের সাথে appointment book করতে দেয়, তাদের infrastructure host করতে AWS ব্যবহার করছে। call receive করতে এবং একটি workflow তৈরি করতে, appointment book করতে ও pay করতে তারা Amazon Connect ও Amazon Lex ব্যবহার করছে। company-র policy অনুযায়ী review-এর জন্য সব call record করতে হবে। কিন্তু save করার আগে call থেকে যেকোনো Personally Identifiable Information (PII) সরিয়ে ফেলার requirement আছে। call থেকে PII সরাতে সাহায্য করে এমন কী ব্যবহারের recommend আপনি করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Amazon Transcribe call er audio ke text kore, ar Redaction diye PII (naam, boyosh, phone number, SSN) automatically remove kore (PDF p. 566). Tai call save korar age PII sorano jay.

Connect + Lex call ar workflow dekhe; PII redaction er kaaj Transcribe er.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Polly text → speech; call theke PII remove kore na.
  • C. "Amazon Recognition" bhul banan (asol naam Rekognition); ar Rekognition image / video analyze kore, audio call er PII na.
  • D. Translate bhasha bodlay; PII redaction kore na.

Keyword: remove PII from calls → Transcribe (PII Redaction)

23·12U23-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q12

Amazon Polly allows you to turn text into speech. It has two important features. First is ……………….. which allows you to customize the pronunciation of words (e.g., “Amazon EC2” will be “Amazon Elastic Compute Cloud”). The second is ……………….. which allows you to emphasize words, including breathing sounds, whispering, and more.

Amazon Polly দিয়ে আপনি text-কে speech-এ রূপান্তর করতে পারেন। এর দুটি গুরুত্বপূর্ণ feature আছে। প্রথমটি হলো ……………….., যা দিয়ে আপনি শব্দের উচ্চারণ customize করতে পারেন (যেমন, “Amazon EC2” হয়ে যাবে “Amazon Elastic Compute Cloud”)। দ্বিতীয়টি হলো ……………….., যা দিয়ে আপনি শব্দে জোর (emphasize) দিতে পারেন, এর মধ্যে breathing sounds, whispering এবং আরও অনেক কিছু আছে।

Correct: C. Why it is right

Polly er duita feature (PDF p. 568):

  • Pronunciation Lexicons: word er uccharon customize: acronym ("AWS" → "Amazon Web Services", ekhane "Amazon EC2" → "Amazon Elastic Compute Cloud") ba stylized word.
  • SSML (Speech Synthesis Markup Language): emphasis, phonetic pronunciation, breathing sound, whispering, Newscaster style.

Tai order: prothome Lexicons, tarpor SSML.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Order ulta: pronunciation customize kore Lexicons, ar breathing / whispering kore SSML.
  • B. SAML (Security Assertion Markup Language) identity federation / SSO er jinish, Polly er feature na.
  • D. SAML Polly er feature na, ar order o bhul.

Keyword: customize pronunciation → Lexicons; emphasize, breathing, whispering → SSML

23·13U23-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q13⚠ outdated

Which fully managed service can deliver highly accurate forecasts?

কোন fully managed service অত্যন্ত নির্ভুল (highly accurate) forecast দিতে পারে?

⚠ Outdated: Amazon Forecast 29 July 2024 theke new customer der jonno bondho (existing ra use korte pare); AWS ekhon forecasting er jonno SageMaker Canvas recommend kore. Course slide e Forecast nai, tobe ei quiz/exam er answer ekhono Forecast. source

Correct: D. Why it is right

Amazon Forecast fully managed ML service ja highly accurate forecast dey; Stephane er ML summary lecture e "Forecast allows you to build highly accurate forecast" (slide e nai).

Ajker obostha: Forecast 29 July 2024 theke notun customer der jonno bondho (existing ra use korte pare); AWS forecasting er jonno SageMaker Canvas recommend kore. Tobuo ei quiz / exam er answer Forecast.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Personalize recommendation dey (user ke ki suggest korbo), forecast na.
  • B. SageMaker diye nije forecast model banano jay, kintu "forecast deliver kora fully managed service" hisabe answer Forecast.
  • C. Lex chatbot banay; forecast er sathe shomporko nai.

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon Personalize is a machine learning service that makes it easy for developers to create individualized recommendations for customers using their applications. It is not used to deliver highly accurate forecasts.

B: Amazon SageMaker is a fully managed service that provides every developer and data scientist with the ability to build, train, and deploy machine learning (ML) models quickly. It is not used to deliver highly accurate forecasts.

C: Amazon Lex is a service for building conversational interfaces into any application using voice and text. It is not used to deliver highly accurate forecasts.

D: Amazon Forecast is a fully managed service that uses machine learning to deliver highly accurate forecasts.

23·14U23-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 23 · Q14

A medical company is in the process of implementing a solution to detect, extract, and analyze information from unstructured medical text like doctors’ notes, clinical trial reports, and radiology reports. Those documents are uploaded and stored on S3 buckets. According to the company’s regulations, the solution must be designed and implemented to keep patients’ privacy by identifying Protected Health Information (PHI) so the solution will be eligible with HIPAA. Which AWS service should you use?

একটি medical company এমন একটি solution implement করছে, যা unstructured medical text থেকে তথ্য detect, extract ও analyze করবে, যেমন ডাক্তারদের notes, clinical trial reports এবং radiology reports। এই document-গুলো S3 bucket-এ upload ও store করা হয়। Company-র নিয়ম অনুযায়ী, solution-টি এমনভাবে design ও implement করতে হবে যাতে Protected Health Information (PHI) শনাক্ত করে রোগীদের privacy রক্ষা হয়, যাতে solution-টি HIPAA-র জন্য eligible হয়। আপনার কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Amazon Comprehend Medical unstructured clinical text (physician's notes, discharge summaries, test results, case notes) theke useful info detect kore, ar NLP diye Protected Health Information (PHI) khuje: DetectPHI API (PDF p. 572).

Document S3 e rakha thakle sorasori Comprehend Medical API call. AWS doc onujayi eta HIPAA Eligible Service.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Rekognition image / video analyze kore; medical text theke PHI ber kore na.
  • C. Polly text → speech; text analyze kore na.
  • D. Translate bhasha anubad kore; PHI detect kore na.

Keyword: unstructured medical text + identify PHI + HIPAA → Comprehend Medical

Section 24: AWS Monitoring & Audit CloudWatch, CloudTrail & Config (16)

Note: Section 24 note. Answer first, then press Check.

24·1U24-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q1

You have an RDS DB instance that's configured to push its database logs to CloudWatch. You want to create a CloudWatch alarm if there's an Error found in the logs. How would you do that?

আপনার একটি RDS DB instance আছে, যা তার database log CloudWatch-এ push করার জন্য configure করা। Log-এ কোনো Error পাওয়া গেলে আপনি একটি CloudWatch alarm তৈরি করতে চান। আপনি এটি কীভাবে করবেন?

Renamed: CloudWatch Events -> Amazon EventBridge (the answer is the same).

Correct: B. Why it is right

RDS er log already CloudWatch Logs e jacche. Log theke alarm banate Metric Filter lagbe: filter ta log e Error keyword khuje count kore ekta CloudWatch metric banay (PDF p. 596).

Tarpor oi metric er upor CloudWatch Alarm banao, alarm SNS e notify korbe. Kono code lage na, near real-time.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Scheduled EventBridge (ager CloudWatch Events) + Lambda diye log scan kora custom code ar extra kaaj; prottek 1 ghonta por check kore, tai delay o thake. Built-in metric filter thakte eta dorkar nai.
  • C. AWS Config resource er configuration ar compliance dekhe; log er bhitorer text (Error) pore na.

Keyword: log e Error paile alarm → Metric Filter + CloudWatch Alarm

24·2U24-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q2

You have an application hosted on a fleet of EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group that you configured its minimum capacity to 2. Also, you have created a CloudWatch Alarm that is configured to scale in your ASG when CPU Utilization is below 60%. Currently, your application runs on 2 EC2 instances and has low traffic and the CloudWatch Alarm is in the ALARM state. What will happen?

আপনার একটি application EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet-এ host করা, যা একটি Auto Scaling Group manage করে, এবং আপনি এর minimum capacity 2 configure করেছেন। এছাড়া, আপনি একটি CloudWatch Alarm তৈরি করেছেন, যা CPU Utilization 60%-এর নিচে হলে আপনার ASG scale in করার জন্য configure করা। বর্তমানে আপনার application 2টি EC2 instance-এ চলছে, traffic কম, এবং CloudWatch Alarm ALARM state-এ আছে। কী ঘটবে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

ASG kokhono minimum capacity er niche jay na (PDF p. 153). Min = 2, ekhon cholche 2 ta, tai scale-in alarm fire korleo ASG kono instance terminate korbe na.

Alarm er state change hoy metric dekhe: CPU 60% er niche thakle alarm ALARM state ei thakbe, kintu kono effect hobe na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Alarm ASG er minimum capacity change kore na. Min 2 thakle ASG 2 er niche namte parbe na.
  • C. Alarm nije nije ASG theke detach hoy na; scaling policy attached thakbe, shudhu kaaj korbe na karon min e ache.
  • D. Alarm state metric er upor nirbhor kore. CPU ekhono 60% er niche, tai OK hobe na, ALARM ei thakbe.

Keyword: minimum capacity 2 + already 2 instances → alarm ALARM e thake kintu scale-in hoy na

Instructor feedback (English)

B: The number of EC2 instances in an ASG can not go below the minimum capacity, even if the CloudWatch alarm would in theory trigger an EC2 instance termination.

24·3U24-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q3

How would you monitor your EC2 instance memory usage in CloudWatch?

CloudWatch-এ আপনি আপনার EC2 instance-এর memory usage কীভাবে monitor করবেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

EC2 er default metric shudhu CPU, disk, network (high level). Memory (RAM) OS er bhitorer jinish, AWS hypervisor theke dekhte pay na (PDF p. 591).

Tai EC2 te CloudWatch Unified Agent install koro; eta RAM, swap, disk space, processes collect kore custom metric hisebe CloudWatch e pathay (PDF p. 579, 590).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Detailed Monitoring shudhu default metric gulo 5 min er bodole 1 min e pathay; memory metric add kore na.
  • B. Bhul: EC2 default e memory usage pathay na, shudhu CPU, disk, network.

Keyword: EC2 memory usage → Unified CloudWatch Agent + custom metric

24·4U24-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q4

You have made a configuration change and would like to evaluate the impact of it on the performance of your application. Which AWS service should you use?

আপনি একটি configuration change করেছেন এবং আপনার application-এর performance-এ এর প্রভাব evaluate করতে চান। আপনার কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Application er performance (CPU, latency, error, request count) maapa hoy CloudWatch Metrics ar dashboard diye (PDF p. 620: CloudWatch = performance monitoring).

Change er age ar pore metric graph compare korlei impact bojha jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. CloudTrail shudhu API call record kore (ke, kokhon, ki change korlo); performance metric dey na.

Keyword: impact on performance → CloudWatch

Instructor feedback (English)

A: Amazon CloudWatch is a monitoring service that allows you to monitor your applications, respond to system-wide performance changes, optimize resource utilization, and get a unified view of operational health. It is used to monitor your applications' performance and metrics.

B: AWS CloudTrail allows you to log, continuously monitor, and retain account activity related to actions across your AWS infrastructure. It provides the event history of your AWS account activity, audit API calls made through the AWS Management Console, AWS SDKs, AWS CLI. You can use CloudTrail to detect unusual activity in your AWS accounts.

24·5U24-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q5

Someone has terminated an EC2 instance in your AWS account last week, which was hosting a critical database that contains sensitive data. Which AWS service helps you find who did that and when?

গত সপ্তাহে কেউ আপনার AWS account-এ একটি EC2 instance terminate করেছে, যেটি sensitive data থাকা একটি critical database host করছিল। কে এবং কখন এটি করেছে তা খুঁজে পেতে কোন AWS service আপনাকে সাহায্য করে?

Renamed: CloudWatch Events -> Amazon EventBridge (the answer is the same).

Correct: D. Why it is right

CloudTrail account er sob API call record kore: ke (IAM user/role), kokhon, kon IP theke (PDF p. 608). Instance terminate holo TerminateInstances API call, eta CloudTrail e thakbe.

Course er tip: resource delete hole age CloudTrail dekho. Event 90 din thake, tai last week er event pawa jabe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CloudWatch Metrics performance data (CPU, network); 'ke' terminate korlo ta bole na.
  • B. CloudWatch Alarm metric threshold e notify kore; kono user er action record kore na.
  • C. CloudWatch Events (ekhon EventBridge) event react kore real-time e, kintu purono 'who did it' history rakhe na. Eta CloudTrail er data dhoreo kaaj kore.

Keyword: who did that and when + terminated → CloudTrail

Instructor feedback (English)

A: CloudWatch Metrics are data about the performance of your systems, it won't indicate "who" did what.

B: CloudWatch Alarms allows you to trigger notifications/actions based on any CloudWatch Metrics or other CloudWatch Alarms, it won't indicate "who" did what.

C: CloudWatch Events delivers a stream of system events that describe changes in your AWS resources, it won't indicate "who" did what.

D: AWS CloudTrail allows you to log, continuously monitor, and retain account activity related to actions across your AWS infrastructure. It provides the event history of your AWS account activity, audit API calls made through the AWS Management Console, AWS SDKs, AWS CLI. So, the EC2 instance termination API call will appear here. You can use CloudTrail to detect unusual activity in your AWS accounts.

24·6U24-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q6

You have CloudTrail enabled for your AWS Account in all AWS Regions. What should you use to detect unusual activity in your AWS Account?

সব AWS Regions-এ আপনার AWS Account-এর জন্য CloudTrail enable করা আছে। আপনার AWS Account-এ অস্বাভাবিক activity detect করতে আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

CloudTrail Insights enable korle CloudTrail normal management event diye ekta baseline banay, tarpor write event continuously analyze kore unusual pattern dhore (PDF p. 611).

Example: hotat IAM action er burst, service limit hit, bhul resource provisioning. Anomaly console e dekhay, S3 te jay, EventBridge event o hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Data Events holo high-volume data operation (S3 GetObject/PutObject, Lambda Invoke) log kora; unusual activity detect kore na.
  • C. Management Events holo normal control-plane operation log (CreateSubnet, AttachRolePolicy); egulo record hoy, kintu nijer theke anomaly dhore na.

Keyword: detect unusual activity → CloudTrail Insights

24·7U24-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q7

One of your teammates terminated an EC2 instance 4 months ago which has critical data. You don't know who made this so you are going to review all API calls within this period using CloudTrail. You already have CloudTrail set up and configured to send logs to the S3 bucket. What should you do to find out who made this?

আপনার এক teammate 4 মাস আগে critical data থাকা একটি EC2 instance terminate করেছে। কে এটি করেছে আপনি জানেন না, তাই আপনি CloudTrail ব্যবহার করে এই সময়ের মধ্যে সব API call review করবেন। আপনার CloudTrail ইতিমধ্যে set up করা এবং S3 bucket-এ log পাঠানোর জন্য configure করা আছে। কে এটি করেছে তা বের করতে আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

CloudTrail Event History e event shudhu 90 din thake (PDF p. 612). 4 mash ager event oikhane nai.

Trail already S3 e log pathacche, tai long-term log S3 e ache. Athena diye S3 er CloudTrail log e SQL query chalao, TerminateInstances khujo.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Event History shudhu last 90 din dekhay; 4 mash (~120 din) ager event pabe na.

Keyword: 4 months ago (90 din er beshi) + logs in S3 → Athena

Instructor feedback (English)

B: You can use the CloudTrail Console to view the last 90 days of recorded API activity. For events older than 90 days, use Athena to analyze CloudTrail logs stored in S3.

24·8U24-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q8

You are running a website on a fleet of EC2 instances with OS that has a known vulnerability on port 84. You want to continuously monitor your EC2 instances if they have port 84 exposed. How should you do this?

আপনি EC2 instance-এর একটি fleet-এ একটি website চালাচ্ছেন, যার OS-এ port 84-এ একটি পরিচিত (known) vulnerability আছে। আপনার EC2 instance-গুলোতে port 84 exposed আছে কিনা, তা আপনি continuously monitor করতে চান। আপনার এটি কীভাবে করা উচিত?

Renamed: CloudWatch Events -> Amazon EventBridge (the answer is the same).

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Config Rules resource er configuration continuously evaluate kore, ar non-compliant hole flag kore (PDF p. 615-616).

Course er example: 'Is there unrestricted SSH access to my security groups?' Same bhabe rule likho: kono security group e port 84 open ache kina. Rule config change hole ba regular interval e check hoy.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CloudWatch Metrics performance number (CPU, network) dey; security group e kon port open ta bole na.
  • B. CloudTrail API call record kore (ke SG change korlo), kintu current configuration compliant kina evaluate kore na.
  • D. Scheduled EventBridge (ager CloudWatch Events) + Lambda diye nije scanner likhte hobe: extra code ar maintenance. Config Rules ei kaaj managed bhabe kore.

Keyword: continuously monitor configuration (port 84 exposed) → Config Rules

24·9U24-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q9

You would like to evaluate the compliance of your resource's configurations over time. Which AWS service will you choose?

আপনি সময়ের সাথে সাথে (over time) আপনার resource-এর configuration-এর compliance evaluate করতে চান। আপনি কোন AWS service বেছে নেবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

AWS Config resource er configuration ar change over time record kore, ar rule diye compliance evaluate kore (PDF p. 615, 617).

Config resource page e compliance timeline, configuration timeline ar related CloudTrail API call dekha jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. CloudWatch performance monitoring, alarm ar log; configuration compliance evaluate kore na.
  • C. CloudTrail API call er history (ke ki korlo) rakhe; resource compliant kina ta evaluate kore na.

Keyword: compliance of configurations over time → AWS Config

24·10U24-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q10

Someone changed the configuration of a resource and made it non-compliant. Which AWS service is responsible for logging who made modifications to resources?

কেউ একটি resource-এর configuration পরিবর্তন করে সেটিকে non-compliant করে ফেলেছে। Resource-এ কে modification করেছে তা log করার দায়িত্ব কোন AWS service-এর?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Prosno 'ke' modify korlo. Ei 'who' answer kore CloudTrail: sob API call er identity, time, source IP record kore (PDF p. 620).

Config bolbe ki change holo ar resource ekhon non-compliant, kintu je API call ta change korlo, tar user CloudTrail e thake (Config o oi CloudTrail event link kore dekhay).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CloudWatch metric ar log dey; ke resource change korlo ta log kore na.
  • C. Config ki change holo ar compliance dekhay; ke korlo er original record CloudTrail er.

Keyword: logging who made modifications → CloudTrail

24·11U24-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q11

You have enabled AWS Config to monitor Security Groups if there's unrestricted SSH access to any of your EC2 instances. Which AWS Config feature can you use to automatically re-configure your Security Groups to their correct state?

আপনার কোনো EC2 instance-এ unrestricted SSH access আছে কিনা, তা Security Group-এ monitor করতে আপনি AWS Config enable করেছেন। আপনার Security Group-গুলোকে স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে তাদের সঠিক state-এ re-configure করতে আপনি AWS Config-এর কোন feature ব্যবহার করতে পারেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Config Remediations non-compliant resource ke automatic thik kore, SSM Automation Document diye (AWS-managed ba custom, custom document Lambda o call korte pare) (PDF p. 618).

Ekhane rule SG te open SSH dhorbe, ar remediation action oi rule revoke kore SG ke correct state e ferot nebe. Remediation retries o set kora jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Config Rules shudhu evaluate kore compliant/non-compliant bole; nije kichu fix kore na, deny o kore na.
  • C. Notifications shudhu alert pathay (EventBridge/SNS); SG reconfigure kore na.

Keyword: automatically re-configure to correct state → Config Remediations

24·12U24-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q12

You are running a critical website on a set of EC2 instances with a tightened Security Group that has restricted SSH access. You have enabled AWS Config in your AWS Region and you want to be notified via email when someone modified your EC2 instances' Security Group. Which AWS Config feature helps you do this?

আপনি EC2 instance-এর একটি set-এ একটি critical website চালাচ্ছেন, যার Security Group কড়াভাবে (tightened) সেট করা এবং SSH access restricted। আপনি আপনার AWS Region-এ AWS Config enable করেছেন, এবং কেউ আপনার EC2 instance-গুলোর Security Group modify করলে email-এর মাধ্যমে notify হতে চান। AWS Config-এর কোন feature আপনাকে এটি করতে সাহায্য করে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Lokkho holo email e notify kora jokhon SG change hoy. Config Notifications configuration change ar compliance state change SNS e pathay (SNS email subscription), ba EventBridge diye non-compliant event e notify kora jay (PDF p. 619).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Remediation non-compliant resource ke fix kore (SSM document); email notification er feature na.
  • B. Rules shudhu compliance evaluate kore; nijera email pathay na, notify er jonno SNS/EventBridge lage.

Keyword: notified via email when SG modified → Config Notifications (SNS)

24·13U24-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q13

…………………………. is a CloudWatch feature that allows you to send CloudWatch metrics in near real-time to S3 bucket (through Kinesis Data Firehose) and 3rd party destinations (e.g., Splunk, Datadog, …).

…………………………. হলো CloudWatch-এর একটি feature, যা আপনাকে CloudWatch metrics near real-time-এ S3 bucket-এ (Kinesis Data Firehose-এর মাধ্যমে) এবং 3rd party destination-এ (যেমন, Splunk, Datadog, …) পাঠাতে দেয়।

Renamed: Kinesis Data Firehose -> Amazon Data Firehose (the answer is the same).

Correct: A. Why it is right

CloudWatch Metric Streams CloudWatch metrics near real-time, low latency e stream kore (PDF p. 580):

  • Kinesis Data Firehose (ekhon Amazon Data Firehose) e, tarpor S3, Redshift, OpenSearch.
  • Ba sorasori 3rd party: Datadog, Dynatrace, New Relic, Splunk, Sumo Logic.

Chaile shudhu kichu namespace filter kore stream kora jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Log Stream holo log group er bhitore ekta source er log sequence (ekta instance/container); metric pathanor feature na.
  • C. Metric Filter log theke pattern khuje metric banay; metric ke bahire stream kore na.
  • D. Log Group holo log er container (sadharonoto ekta application); metric deliver kore na.

Keyword: send metrics near real-time to Firehose / Datadog, Splunk → Metric Stream

24·14U24-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q14

A DevOps engineer is working for a company and managing its infrastructure and resources on AWS. There was a sudden spike in traffic for the main application for the company which was not normal in this period of the year. The application is hosted on a couple of EC2 instances in private subnets and is fronted by an Application Load Balancer in a public subnet. To detect if this is normal traffic or an attack, the DevOps engineer enabled the VPC Flow Logs for the subnets and stored those logs in CloudWatch Log Group. The DevOps wants to analyze those logs and find out the top IP addresses making requests against the website to check if there is an attack. Which of the following can help the DevOps engineer to analyze those logs?

একজন DevOps engineer একটি company-তে কাজ করছেন এবং AWS-এ এর infrastructure ও resource manage করছেন। Company-র main application-এ হঠাৎ traffic-এ একটি spike দেখা গেল, যা বছরের এই সময়ে স্বাভাবিক ছিল না। Application-টি private subnet-এ কয়েকটি EC2 instance-এ host করা, এবং এর সামনে public subnet-এ একটি Application Load Balancer আছে। এটি স্বাভাবিক traffic নাকি attack তা detect করতে DevOps engineer subnet-গুলোর জন্য VPC Flow Logs enable করেছেন এবং সেই log-গুলো CloudWatch Log Group-এ store করেছেন। DevOps সেই log-গুলো analyze করে website-এ সবচেয়ে বেশি request করা top IP address-গুলো বের করতে চান, যাতে attack হচ্ছে কিনা check করা যায়। নিচের কোনটি DevOps engineer-কে সেই log-গুলো analyze করতে সাহায্য করতে পারে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

CloudWatch Contributor Insights CloudWatch Logs er data analyze kore top-N contributors dekhay: top talker, heaviest network user, sobcheye beshi error dewa URL (PDF p. 605).

VPC Flow Logs already CloudWatch Logs e ache, tai Contributor Insights rule diye top 10 IP address ber kora jay: attack kina bojha shohoj.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Metric Stream metrics bahire (Firehose/3rd party) pathay; log analyze kore top IP ber kore na.
  • B. Alarm ekta metric threshold e notify kore; log er top contributor dekhay na.
  • D. Metric Filter ekta pattern count kore metric banay; kon IP sobcheye beshi request korche emon ranking (top-N) dey na.

Keyword: top IP addresses from VPC Flow Logs → Contributor Insights

24·15U24-Q15Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q15

A company is developing a Serverless application on AWS using Lambda, DynamoDB, and Cognito. A junior developer joined a few weeks ago and accidentally deleted one of the DynamoDB tables in the dev AWS account which contained important data. The CTO asks you to prevent this from happening again and there must be a notification system to monitor if there is an attempt to make such deletion actions for the DynamoDB tables. What would you do?

একটি company AWS-এ Lambda, DynamoDB ও Cognito ব্যবহার করে একটি Serverless application develop করছে। কয়েক সপ্তাহ আগে যোগ দেওয়া একজন junior developer ভুলবশত dev AWS account-এ গুরুত্বপূর্ণ data থাকা একটি DynamoDB table delete করে ফেলেছেন। CTO আপনাকে এটি আবার ঘটা আটকাতে বলেছেন, এবং DynamoDB table-গুলোর জন্য এমন delete action করার চেষ্টা হলে তা monitor করার একটি notification system থাকতে হবে। আপনি কী করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Duita requirement:

  • Prevent: developer der IAM group e dynamodb:DeleteTable deny kora policy.
  • Notify: DeleteTable API call CloudTrail e log hoy; EventBridge rule oi event dhore SNS e alert pathay (PDF p. 613: DynamoDB DeleteTable → CloudTrail → EventBridge → SNS).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Duita bhul: API call event S3 theke ase na, CloudTrail theke ase; ar KMS encryption key service, notification pathate pare na.
  • C. CloudTrail part thik, kintu KMS encryption er service; notification pathay SNS.

Keyword: prevent deletion + notification on DeleteTable → IAM deny + CloudTrail → EventBridge → SNS

24·16U24-Q16Udemy quiz · sec 24 · Q16

A company has a running Serverless application on AWS which uses EventBridge as an inter-communication channel between different services within the application. There is a requirement to use the events in the prod environment in the dev environment to make some tests. The tests will be done every 6 months, so the events need to be stored and used later on. What is the most efficient and cost-effective way to store EventBridge events and use them later?

একটি company-র AWS-এ একটি চলমান Serverless application আছে, যা application-এর ভেতরের বিভিন্ন service-এর মধ্যে inter-communication channel হিসেবে EventBridge ব্যবহার করে। কিছু test করার জন্য prod environment-এর event-গুলো dev environment-এ ব্যবহার করার একটি requirement আছে। Test-গুলো প্রতি 6 মাসে করা হবে, তাই event-গুলো store করে পরে ব্যবহার করতে হবে। EventBridge event store করে পরে ব্যবহার করার সবচেয়ে efficient ও cost-effective উপায় কোনটি?

Correct: A. Why it is right

EventBridge event bus e jawa event archive kora jay (sob ba filter kore, indefinitely ba nirdishto period), ar pore replay kora jay (PDF p. 600).

Built-in feature, kono custom code ba extra storage design lage na: tai most efficient ar cost-effective.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Lambda diye S3 e save korle custom code, maintenance, ar pore replay korar logic o nijeke likhte hobe. Built-in archive/replay thakte eta beshi kaaj.
  • C. EventBridge er DynamoDB te event store korar built-in option nai (direct target na); Lambda/custom code lagbe, ar replay o nije banate hobe.

Keyword: store events, use later + most efficient → EventBridge Archive and Replay

25·1U25-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 25 · Q1

You have strong regulatory requirements to only allow fully internally audited AWS services in production. You still want to allow your teams to experiment in a development environment while services are being audited. How can you best set this up?

আপনার কঠোর regulatory requirement আছে যে production-এ শুধু পুরোপুরি internally audited AWS service allow করা যাবে। Service-গুলো audit হওয়ার সময়েও আপনি আপনার team-গুলোকে একটি development environment-এ experiment করতে দিতে চান। আপনি এটি সবচেয়ে ভালোভাবে কীভাবে set up করতে পারেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Organizations e Prod OU ar Dev OU banao (course er 'Environmental Lifecycle' OU example, PDF p. 625). Prod OU te SCP attach koro je shudhu audited service allow kore (allowlist).

SCP OU er sob account er user/role (root o) ke restrict kore (PDF p. 626), ar Dev OU te restriction nai tai team experiment korte pare.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Dev ke alada account dile experiment hoy, kintu Prod e audited service enforce korar kono mechanism hoy na; central governance nai.
  • B. IAM policy user/group/role e attach hoy, 'global' account-wide guardrail na; root user ke restrict kore na, ar admin chaile shoray dite pare.
  • D. Config Rule shudhu non-compliant detect kore; action prevent kore na (PDF p. 616: no deny).

Keyword: only audited services in prod + dev e experiment → Prod/Dev OU + SCP on Prod

25·2U25-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 25 · Q2

You are managing the AWS account for your company, and you want to give one of the developers access to read files from an S3 bucket. You have updated the bucket policy to this, but he still can't access the files in the bucket. What is the problem?

{

    "Version": "2012-10-17",

    "Statement": [{

        "Sid": "AllowsRead",

        "Effect": "Allow",

        "Principal": {

            "AWS": "arn:aws:iam::123456789012:user/Dave"

        },

        "Action": "s3:GetObject",

        "Resource": "arn:aws:s3:::static-files-bucket-xxx"

     }]

}

আপনি আপনার company-র AWS account manage করছেন, এবং আপনি একজন developer-কে একটি S3 bucket থেকে file read করার access দিতে চান। আপনি bucket policy এটিতে update করেছেন, কিন্তু তিনি এখনও bucket-এর file access করতে পারছেন না। সমস্যা কী?

{

"Version": "2012-10-17",

"Statement": [{

"Sid": "AllowsRead",

"Effect": "Allow",

"Principal": {

"AWS": "arn:aws:iam::123456789012:user/Dave"

},

"Action": "s3:GetObject",

"Resource": "arn:aws:s3:::static-files-bucket-xxx"

}]

}

Correct: C. Why it is right

s3:GetObject holo object-level permission, tai Resource hobe arn:aws:s3:::static-files-bucket-xxx/* (PDF p. 632).

Bucket ARN (/* chara) shudhu bucket-level action er jonno, jemon s3:ListBucket. Ekhane resource match kore na, tai Dave read korte pare na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Logout/login e policy thik hoy na; resource ARN bhul thakle access asbe na.
  • B. Bucket khali thakle 'file nai' dekhabe, access denied na; asol problem policy er resource ARN.

Keyword: s3:GetObject + resource = bucket ARN → /* lagbe (object-level)

25·3U25-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 25 · Q3

You have 5 AWS Accounts that you manage using AWS Organizations. You want to restrict access to certain AWS services in each account. How should you do that?

আপনার 5টি AWS Accounts আছে, যেগুলো আপনি AWS Organizations ব্যবহার করে manage করেন। আপনি প্রতিটি account-এ নির্দিষ্ট কিছু AWS service-এর access restrict করতে চান। আপনার এটি কীভাবে করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Organizations er Service Control Policy (SCP) OU ba account e attach hoy, ar oi account er users/roles kon service/action use korte parbe tar maximum limit set kore (PDF p. 626). 5 account e ek jaygay theke service restrict korar thik tool.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. IAM Role permission dey (assume kora jay), account-level e service block korar central mechanism na.
  • C. Config resource configuration evaluate kore; kono action restrict/deny kore na.

Keyword: Organizations + restrict services in each account → SCP

25·4U25-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 25 · Q4

Which of the following IAM condition key you can use only to allow API calls to a specified AWS region?

শুধু একটি নির্দিষ্ট AWS region-এ API call allow করতে আপনি নিচের কোন IAM condition key ব্যবহার করতে পারেন?

Correct: D. Why it is right

aws:RequestedRegion API call kon region e jacche ta restrict kore (PDF p. 630). Shudhu nirdishto region e call allow korte eta-i condition key.

Arekta: aws:SourceIp client IP restrict kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. aws:RequiredRegion nam e kono IAM condition key nai.
  • B. aws:SourceRegion nam e global key nai; region er jonno key holo aws:RequestedRegion.
  • C. aws:InitialRegion nam e kono key nai.

Keyword: allow API calls to a specified region → aws:RequestedRegion

25·5U25-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 25 · Q5

When configuring permissions for EventBridge to configure a Lambda function as a target you should use ………………….. but when you want to configure a Kinesis Data Streams as a target you should use …………………..

EventBridge-এর জন্য permission configure করার সময় একটি Lambda function-কে target হিসেবে configure করতে আপনার ………………….. ব্যবহার করা উচিত, কিন্তু যখন একটি Kinesis Data Streams-কে target হিসেবে configure করতে চান, তখন আপনার ………………….. ব্যবহার করা উচিত

Correct: B. Why it is right

EventBridge rule challe target e permission lage (PDF p. 636):

  • Lambda, SNS, SQS, S3, API Gateway: target er resource-based policy e EventBridge ke allow koro.
  • Kinesis Data Streams, EC2 Auto Scaling, SSM Run Command, ECS task: EventBridge ekta IAM role (identity-based policy) use kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Ulta order: Lambda resource-based policy use kore, Kinesis Data Streams IAM role.
  • C. Lambda target e resource-based policy lage, IAM role na; tai duitai identity-based bhul.
  • D. Kinesis Data Streams e resource-based policy diye EventBridge ke allow kora hoy na; EventBridge IAM role diye write kore.

Keyword: EventBridge target: Lambda = resource-based, Kinesis = IAM role (identity-based)

Section 26: AWS Security & Encryption KMS, SSM Parameter Store, Shield, WAF (25)

Note: Section 26 note. Answer first, then press Check.

26·1U26-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q1

To enable In-flight Encryption (In-Transit Encryption), we need to have ........................

In-flight Encryption (In-Transit Encryption) enable করতে, আমাদের থাকতে হবে ........................

Correct: B. Why it is right

In-flight encryption mane data network e jawar shomoy encrypted thake, jate MITM (man in the middle) attack na hoy (PDF p. 652).

Eta hoy TLS/SSL diye, mane HTTPS. Ar HTTPS endpoint chalu korte ekta SSL/TLS certificate lagbei.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. HTTP plain text protocol; certificate thakleo HTTP endpoint data encrypt kore na. In-flight encryption er jonno HTTPS lagbe.
  • C. Shudhu TCP endpoint mane kono TLS nai, data plain text e jabe; encryption hobe na.

Keyword: In-flight encryption → HTTPS + SSL/TLS certificate

Instructor feedback (English)

B: In-flight Encryption = HTTPS, and HTTPS can not be enabled without an SSL certificate.

26·2U26-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q2

Server-Side Encryption means that the data is sent encrypted to the server.

Server-Side Encryption মানে হলো data encrypted অবস্থায় server-এ পাঠানো হয়।

Correct: B. Why it is right

Server-side encryption at rest e data server receive korar pore server nije encrypt kore, ar pathanor age decrypt kore (PDF p. 653).

Tumi data plain (HTTPS diye) pathao; agei encrypt kore pathano holo client-side encryption. Tai statement ta False.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul: data age theke encrypt kore pathano client-side encryption er kaaj. Server-side e server nije receive korar por encrypt kore.

Keyword: Server-Side Encryption → server receive korar por encrypt kore → False

Instructor feedback (English)

B: Server-Side Encryption means the server will encrypt the data for us. We don't need to encrypt it beforehand.

26·3U26-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q3

In Server-Side Encryption, where do the encryption and decryption happen?

Server-Side Encryption-এ encryption এবং decryption কোথায় ঘটে?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Server-side encryption e key server er kache (ba server er access ache, jemon S3 + data key). Tai encrypt o server kore, decrypt o server kore pathanor age (PDF p. 653).

Client er hate key thake na, tai client nije kichu encrypt/decrypt kore na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Dutoi client e hole seta client-side encryption, server-side na.
  • C. Client er kache server er key nai, tai client decrypt korte parbe na; server-i decrypt kore pathay.
  • D. Client encrypt korle server er kache oi key nai, server decrypt korte parbe na. Eta mixed model, server-side encryption na.

Keyword: Server-Side Encryption → encrypt + decrypt dutoi server e

Instructor feedback (English)

A: In Server-Side Encryption, we can't do encryption/decryption ourselves as we don't have access to the corresponding encryption key.

26·4U26-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q4

In Client-Side Encryption, the server must know our encryption scheme before we can upload the data.

Client-Side Encryption-এ, data upload করার আগে server-কে অবশ্যই আমাদের encryption scheme জানতে হবে।

Correct: A. Why it is right

Client-side encryption e client nije encrypt kore pathay, server shudhu encrypted object store kore, kokhono decrypt kore na (PDF p. 654).

Server ke tai encryption scheme ba key janar dorkar nai. Je kono storage (FTP, S3...) e rakha jay. Tai statement False.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Bhul: server ekhane kono encrypt/decrypt kore na, shudhu bytes store kore. Scheme jana lage na; server decrypt korte parbe na eta-i uddesho.

Keyword: Client-Side → server shudhu store kore, scheme jane na → False

Instructor feedback (English)

A: With Client-Side Encryption, the server doesn't need to know any information about the encryption scheme being used, as the server will not perform any encryption or decryption operations.

26·5U26-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q5

You need to create KMS Keys in AWS KMS before you are able to use the encryption features for EBS, S3, RDS ...

EBS, S3, RDS ...-এর encryption feature ব্যবহার করতে পারার আগে আপনাকে AWS KMS-এ KMS Keys তৈরি করতে হবে

Correct: B. Why it is right

EBS, S3, RDS er encryption er jonno nijer key banano mandatory na. AWS er AWS Managed Key (jemon aws/ebs, aws/rds) free te ready thake, encryption on korlei use hoy (PDF p. 657).

Chaile nijer customer managed key ($1/month) banate paro, kintu lage na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul: AWS managed key (aws/service-name) already ache, tai nijer KMS key na baniyeo EBS/S3/RDS encrypt kora jay.

Keyword: need to create KMS keys? → na, AWS Managed Keys ache → False

Instructor feedback (English)

A: You could also create your own keys for AWS to do the encryption, but it's not mandatory.

B: You can use the AWS Managed Service keys in KMS, therefore we don't need to create our own KMS keys.

26·6U26-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q6

AWS KMS supports both symmetric and asymmetric KMS keys.

AWS KMS symmetric এবং asymmetric দুই ধরনের KMS key-ই support করে।

Correct: A. Why it is right

KMS dui type key support kore (PDF p. 656):

  • Symmetric (AES-256): ek key diye encrypt + decrypt; AWS service gulo eta use kore.
  • Asymmetric (RSA, ECC pair): public key download kora jay, encrypt/decrypt ba sign/verify er jonno.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Bhul: KMS symmetric (AES-256) ar asymmetric (RSA/ECC) dutoi support kore.

Keyword: symmetric and asymmetric KMS keys → True

Instructor feedback (English)

A: KMS keys can be symmetric or asymmetric. A symmetric KMS key represents a 256-bit key used for encryption and decryption. An asymmetric KMS key represents an RSA key pair used for encryption and decryption or signing and verification, but not both. Or it represents an elliptic curve (ECC) key pair used for signing and verification.

26·7U26-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q7⚠ outdated

When you enable Automatic Rotation on your KMS Key, the backing key is rotated every .................

আপনার KMS Key-তে Automatic Rotation enable করলে, backing key প্রতি ................. rotate হয়

⚠ Outdated: Default automatic rotation ekhono 1 year (365 days), kintu April 2024 theke customer managed key er rotation period 90 theke 2560 days er moddhe set kora jay, ar on-demand rotation o ache. Course/exam er answer ekhono '1 year' (default). source

Correct: B. Why it is right

Course onujayi automatic key rotation on korle backing key protibochor (1 year) rotate hoy; AWS-managed key o automatic 1 year e rotate hoy (PDF p. 657).

Ajker update: default ekhono 1 year (365 days), kintu April 2024 theke customer managed key er rotation period 90-2560 days set kora jay, ar on-demand rotation o ache. Exam answer: 1 year.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. 90 days default na; eta ekhon customer managed key er custom period er minimum, default rotation 1 year.
  • C. 2 years default rotation period na; default 1 year.
  • D. 3 years default na; default 1 year (365 days).

Keyword: Automatic Rotation backing key → 1 year (default)

26·8U26-Q8Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q8

You have an AMI that has an encrypted EBS snapshot using KMS CMK. You want to share this AMI with another AWS account. You have shared the AMI with the desired AWS account, but the other AWS account still can't use it. How would you solve this problem?

আপনার একটি AMI আছে, যাতে KMS CMK ব্যবহার করে encrypt করা একটি EBS snapshot আছে। আপনি এই AMI-টি অন্য একটি AWS account-এর সাথে share করতে চান। আপনি কাঙ্ক্ষিত AWS account-এর সাথে AMI-টি share করেছেন, কিন্তু অন্য AWS account-টি এখনও এটি ব্যবহার করতে পারছে না। আপনি এই সমস্যার সমাধান কীভাবে করবেন?

Renamed: KMS Customer Master Key (CMK) -> AWS KMS key (customer managed key) (the answer is the same).

Correct: B. Why it is right

AMI er snapshot KMS key diye encrypted. Onno account AMI launch korte gele snapshot decrypt korte hobe, tai tader oi KMS key use korar permission lagbe (PDF p. 666).

  • AMI te launch permission add koro (already kora).
  • KMS key share koro (key policy) target account/role er sathe.
  • Target er DescribeKey, ReEncrypt*, CreateGrant, Decrypt permission lagbe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Logout/login e kono permission add hoy na; problem ta KMS key permission er, credential refresh er na.
  • C. Bhul: encrypted AMI share kora jay, shudhu KMS key o share korte hoy (PDF p. 666).

Keyword: encrypted AMI share korsi kintu use korte parche na → KMS key share koro

26·9U26-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q9

You have created a Customer-managed CMK in KMS that you use to encrypt both S3 buckets and EBS snapshots. Your company policy mandates that your encryption keys be rotated every 6 months. What should you do?

আপনি KMS-এ একটি Customer-managed CMK তৈরি করেছেন, যা আপনি S3 bucket এবং EBS snapshot দুটোই encrypt করতে ব্যবহার করেন। আপনার company-র policy অনুযায়ী আপনার encryption key প্রতি 6 মাসে rotate করতে হবে। আপনার কী করা উচিত?

Renamed: KMS Customer Master Key (CMK) -> AWS KMS key (customer managed key) (the answer is the same).

Correct: A. Why it is right

April 2024 theke customer managed key er automatic rotation period nijer moto set kora jay (90-2560 days), tai 180 days (6 mash) diye automatic rotation on korlei policy meet hoy. Option e 'Retention Period' likha, asole setting ta holo rotation period.

Purono course/original answer chilo C (manual rotation + alias), karon tokhon automatic rotation shudhu 1 year e fixed chilo. Ekhon automatic option ta simple ar kom kaaj.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Bhul: AWS managed key 3 mash e na, 1 year por por rotate hoy (PDF p. 657). Ar oder period change kora jay na.
  • C. Manual rotation (new key + alias, purono key rakha) kaaj korbe, eta purono answer chilo. Kintu ekhon automatic rotation e 180 days set kora jay, tai manual process extra operational overhead.

Keyword: Customer-managed key, every 6 months rotate → automatic rotation, period 180 days

Instructor feedback (English)

C: It is better to use Automatic Key Rotation and configure Retention Period

26·10U26-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q10

What should you use to control access to your KMS CMKs?

আপনার KMS CMK-গুলোতে access নিয়ন্ত্রণ করতে আপনার কী ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Renamed: KMS Customer Master Key (CMK) -> AWS KMS key (customer managed key) (the answer is the same).

Correct: A. Why it is right

KMS key er access control er main jinish KMS Key Policy, S3 bucket policy er moto resource-based policy (PDF p. 659).

Difference: key policy chara access control kora jay-i na. Custom key policy diye bolo ke key use korbe, ke administer korbe, ar cross-account access dao.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. 'KMS IAM Policy' namer alada kichu nai. IAM policy kaaj kore shudhu jodi key policy allow kore; main control key policy.
  • C. GuardDuty threat detection service, KMS key er access control kore na.
  • D. KMS e kono ACL nai; access control hoy key policy (ar grant) diye.

Keyword: control access to KMS keys → KMS Key Policies

26·11U26-Q11Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q11

You have a Lambda function used to process some data in the database. You would like to give your Lambda function access to the database password. Which of the following options is the most secure?

আপনার একটি Lambda function আছে, যা database-এর কিছু data process করতে ব্যবহৃত হয়। আপনি আপনার Lambda function-কে database password-এ access দিতে চান। নিচের কোন option সবচেয়ে secure (most secure)?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Course bole: secret kokhono plaintext e rakhba na, especially code e. KMS diye encrypt kora secret code/environment variable e rakha jay (PDF p. 655).

Tai Lambda te password ke encrypted environment variable e rakho ar runtime e KMS call kore decrypt koro. Ei 3 option er moddhe eta-i most secure.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Code e password embed kora sobcheye kharap: code dekhlei password dekha jay, git e chole jay.
  • B. Plaintext environment variable console e jei dekhe se password dekhte pay; encrypted na, tai secure na.

Keyword: most secure DB password Lambda te → encrypted env variable + runtime e decrypt

Instructor feedback (English)

A: This is not secure.

B: This is not secure.

C: This is the most secure solution amongst these options.

26·12U26-Q12Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q12

You have a secret value that you use for encryption purposes, and you want to store and track the values of this secret over time. Which AWS service should you use?

আপনার একটি secret value আছে, যা আপনি encryption-এর উদ্দেশ্যে ব্যবহার করেন, এবং আপনি সময়ের সাথে এই secret-এর value-গুলো store ও track করতে চান। আপনার কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

SSM Parameter Store configuration ar secret store kore, KMS diye optional encryption, ar version tracking built-in (PDF p. 667).

Prottek bar value edit korle notun version hoy, purono version gulo history te thake. Tai secret er value somoy er sathe track kora jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. KMS key rotate korte pare, kintu tomar secret value store ba tar version history rakhe na.
  • C. S3 te versioning + encryption ache, kintu secret store korar jonno eta best fit na; Parameter Store eta-i kore.

Keyword: secret store + track values over time → SSM Parameter Store

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS KMS can rotate stored KMS keys, but it can not help audit the values of an encrypted value over time.

B: SSM Parameters Store can be used to store secrets and has built-in version tracking capability. Each time you edit the value of a parameter, SSM Parameter Store creates a new version of the parameter and retains the previous versions. You can view the details, including the values, of all versions in a parameter's history.

C: Amazon S3 has both versioning and encryption features, but it is not the best fit for storing secrets.

26·13U26-Q13Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q13

Your user-facing website is a high-risk target for DDoS attacks and you would like to get 24/7 support in case they happen and AWS bill reimbursement for the incurred costs during the attack. What AWS service should you use?

আপনার user-facing website DDoS attack-এর একটি high-risk target, এবং attack হলে আপনি 24/7 support পেতে চান এবং attack চলাকালে হওয়া খরচের জন্য AWS bill reimbursement পেতে চান। আপনার কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করা উচিত?

Correct: B. Why it is right

  • 24/7 access to AWS DDoS Response Team (SRT).
  • DDoS er karone usage spike hole higher fees theke protection (cost protection / bill reimbursement).

Question e dutoi chawa hoyeche, tai Shield Advanced.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. WAF Layer 7 rule (SQLi, XSS, rate-based) diye filter kore; 24/7 DDoS team ba bill reimbursement dey na.
  • C. Shield (Standard) free, Layer 3/4 attack (SYN/UDP flood) theke basic protection dey; 24/7 SRT ar cost protection shudhu Advanced e.
  • D. 'AWS DDoS OpsTeam' namer kono service nai; distractor.

Keyword: 24/7 support + bill reimbursement during DDoS → Shield Advanced

26·14U26-Q14Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q14

You would like to externally maintain the configuration values of your main database, to be picked up at runtime by your application. What's the best place to store them to maintain control and version history?

আপনি আপনার main database-এর configuration value-গুলো বাইরে (externally) রাখতে চান, যাতে আপনার application runtime-এ সেগুলো নিতে পারে। নিয়ন্ত্রণ এবং version history বজায় রাখতে এগুলো store করার সবচেয়ে ভালো জায়গা কোনটি?

  • Security/control: IAM diye ke porte parbe.
  • Version tracking built-in.
  • Hierarchy (/my-app/dev/db-url) ar KMS encryption optional (PDF p. 668).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. S3 versioning ache, kintu config value runtime e porar jonno Parameter Store er moto purpose-built na (IAM per-parameter, hierarchy nai).
  • C. EBS ekta instance er disk; app er bairer central config store hote pare na, version history o nai.

Keyword: externally maintain configuration + version history → SSM Parameter Store

  • B. VPC Flow Logs GuardDuty scan kore (unusual internal traffic, IP).
  • C. DNS logs GuardDuty scan kore (compromised EC2 DNS diye data pathaley dhore).

Instructor feedback (English)
