It automatically provides an inventory of S3 buckets including a list of unencrypted buckets, publicly accessible buckets, and buckets shared with other AWS accounts. It allows you to identify and alert you to sensitive data, such as Personally Identifiable Information (PII).

26·21U26-Q21Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q21

An online-payment company is using AWS to host its infrastructure. The frontend is created using VueJS and is hosted on an S3 bucket and the backend is developed using PHP and is hosted on EC2 instances in an Auto Scaling Group. As their customers are worldwide, they use both CloudFront and Aurora Global database to implement multi-region deployments to provide the lowest latency and provide availability, and resiliency. A new feature required which gives customers the ability to store data encrypted on the database and this data must not be disclosed even by the company admins. The data should be encrypted on the client side and stored in an encrypted format. What do you recommend to implement this?

একটি online-payment company তাদের infrastructure host করতে AWS ব্যবহার করছে। frontend VueJS দিয়ে তৈরি এবং একটি S3 bucket-এ host করা, আর backend PHP দিয়ে develop করা এবং একটি Auto Scaling Group-এ EC2 instance-এ host করা। যেহেতু তাদের customer-রা সারা বিশ্বে, তাই সবচেয়ে কম latency দিতে এবং availability ও resiliency দিতে multi-region deployment implement করার জন্য তারা CloudFront এবং Aurora Global database দুটোই ব্যবহার করে। একটি নতুন feature প্রয়োজন, যা customer-দের database-এ encrypted data store করার সুবিধা দেয় এবং এই data company-র admin-দের কাছেও প্রকাশ করা যাবে না। data client side-এ encrypt করতে হবে এবং encrypted format-এ store করতে হবে। এটি implement করতে আপনি কী recommend করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Slide e exactly ei design (PDF p. 664): AWS Encryption SDK diye Aurora table er specific column client-side encrypt koro multi-Region KMS key diye.

  • Aurora Global DB encrypted data onno region e replicate kore.
  • Oi region e MRK replica thakay client local KMS call kore low latency te decrypt kore.
  • Client-side encryption tai database admin o data dekhte pare na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Data Aurora Global DB te, tai Aurora column er client-side encryption lagbe; 'Lambda client-side encryption' design ta course er pattern na, Lambda ekhane data store o na.
  • C. CloudHSM ek region er VPC te cluster; multi-region e same key low latency te use korar jonno MRK er moto suvidha nai, ar onek beshi manage korte hoy.
  • D. Lambda ekhane relevant na, ar CloudHSM multi-region key solution na; MRK lagbe.

Keyword: Aurora Global + client side + not disclosed even by admins → client-side encryption + KMS Multi-Region Keys

26·22U26-Q22Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q22

You have an S3 bucket that is encrypted with SSE-KMS. You have been tasked to replicate the objects to a target bucket in the same AWS region but with a different KMS Key. You have configured the S3 replication, the target bucket, and the target KMS key and it is still not working. What is missing to make the S3 replication work?

আপনার একটি S3 bucket আছে, যা SSE-KMS দিয়ে encrypted। আপনাকে object-গুলো একই AWS region-এর একটি target bucket-এ কিন্তু একটি ভিন্ন KMS Key দিয়ে replicate করার দায়িত্ব দেওয়া হয়েছে। আপনি S3 replication, target bucket এবং target KMS key configure করেছেন, তবুও এটি কাজ করছে না। S3 replication কাজ করাতে কী বাদ পড়েছে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

SSE-KMS object replicate korte option enable korte hoy ar (PDF p. 665):

  • Target bucket er jonno KMS key specify koro, target key policy adapt koro.
  • Replication er IAM Role e source key er kms:Decrypt ar target key er kms:Encrypt permission dao.

Permission na thakle replication fail kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul: SSE-KMS object replication supported, shudhu option ar permission lagbe.
  • B. Support ticket lagbe na; S3 replication nije chole, IAM role e KMS permission dilei hoy.
  • D. Same key lagbe na; target bucket e alada KMS key specify kora jay.

Keyword: SSE-KMS replication, different KMS key, still not working → role e kms:Decrypt (source) + kms:Encrypt (target)

26·23U26-Q23Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q23

You have generated a public certificate using LetsEncrypt and uploaded it to the ACM so you can use and attach to an Application Load Balancer that forwards traffic to EC2 instances. As this certificate is generated outside of AWS, it does not support the automatic renewal feature. How would you be notified 30 days before this certificate expires so you can manually generate a new one?

আপনি LetsEncrypt ব্যবহার করে একটি public certificate generate করেছেন এবং এটি ACM-এ upload করেছেন, যাতে আপনি এটি ব্যবহার করতে এবং EC2 instance-এ traffic forward করা একটি Application Load Balancer-এ attach করতে পারেন। যেহেতু এই certificate AWS-এর বাইরে generate করা, তাই এটি automatic renewal feature support করে না। এই certificate expire হওয়ার 30 দিন আগে আপনি কীভাবে notification পাবেন, যাতে আপনি manually একটি নতুন certificate generate করতে পারেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Import kora certificate e automatic renewal nai. ACM expiry er 45 din age theke protidin (daily) expiration event pathay, din sonkha configure kora jay, ar event gulo EventBridge e ashe (PDF p. 675).

Tai EventBridge rule banao ja SNS diye email pathabe. 30 din age notify paoyar jonno eta kaaj kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. ACM LetsEncrypt er sathe link kore notification dey na; ACM er nijer expiry event EventBridge e jay.
  • C. ACM monthly na, daily expiration event pathay.
  • D. Expiry notification ACM er event, eta EventBridge e jay, CloudWatch Alarm e na. Alarm metric er upor kaaj kore.

Keyword: imported cert, notify before expires → ACM daily expiry event → EventBridge → SNS

26·24U26-Q24Udemy quiz · sec 26 · Q24

You have created the main Edge-Optimized API Gateway in us-west-2 AWS region. This main Edge-Optimized API Gateway forwards traffic to the second level API Gateway in ap-southeast-1. You want to secure the main API Gateway by attaching an ACM certificate to it. Which AWS region are you going to create the ACM certificate in?

আপনি us-west-2 AWS region-এ main Edge-Optimized API Gateway তৈরি করেছেন। এই main Edge-Optimized API Gateway ap-southeast-1-এর second level API Gateway-তে traffic forward করে। আপনি main API Gateway-তে একটি ACM certificate attach করে এটিকে secure করতে চান। আপনি কোন AWS region-এ ACM certificate তৈরি করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Edge-Optimized API Gateway request CloudFront edge location diye route kore. Tai TLS certificate ta CloudFront er region, mane us-east-1 e thakte hobe (PDF p. 678).

API Gateway ta us-west-2 te holeo certificate us-east-1 e.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. us-west-2 te certificate lagto Regional endpoint hole; Edge-Optimized e CloudFront use hoy, tai us-east-1.
  • C. ap-southeast-1 e second level API ache; main Edge-Optimized API er cert ekhane na, us-east-1 e.
  • D. Shudhu us-east-1 kaaj kore; us-west-2 er cert Edge-Optimized endpoint e attach hobe na.

Keyword: Edge-Optimized API Gateway cert → us-east-1 (CloudFront)

Instructor feedback (English)

A: As the Edge-Optimized API Gateway is using a custom AWS managed CloudFront distribution behind the scene to route requests across the globe through CloudFront Edge locations, the ACM certificate must be created in us-east-1.

Section 28: Disaster Recovery & Migrations (10)

28·1U28-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q1

As part of your Disaster Recovery plan, you would like to have only the critical infrastructure up and running in AWS. You don't mind a longer Recovery Time Objective (RTO). Which DR strategy do you recommend?

আপনার Disaster Recovery plan-এর অংশ হিসেবে, আপনি AWS-এ শুধু critical infrastructure চালু রাখতে চান। একটি দীর্ঘ Recovery Time Objective (RTO)-তে আপনার আপত্তি নেই। আপনি কোন DR strategy recommend করবেন?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Pilot Light: app er ekta chhoto version, mane shudhu critical core (jemon RDS running) cloud e shob shomoy chole; EC2 gulo running thake na (PDF p. 783).

Disaster e baki infra chalu korte hoy, tai RTO Warm Standby er theke beshi, kintu Backup and Restore er theke kom. 'Longer RTO cholbe' + 'shudhu critical infra' = Pilot Light.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Backup and Restore e kichui running thake na, shudhu backup/snapshot; question e critical infra running chawa hoyeche.
  • C. Warm Standby e full system minimum size e running thake; shudhu critical na, ar khoroch beshi.
  • D. Multi-Site e full production scale active-active chole; sobcheye beshi khoroch, critical-only na.

Keyword: only the critical infrastructure running + longer RTO ok → Pilot Light

Instructor feedback (English)

B: If you're interested, read more about Disaster Recovery options in AWS here: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/whitepapers/latest/disaster-recovery-workloads-on-aws/disaster-recovery-options-in-the-cloud.html

28·2U28-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q2

You would like to get the Disaster Recovery strategy with the lowest Recovery Time Objective (RTO) and Recovery Point Objective (RPO), regardless of the cost. Which DR should you choose?

খরচ যাই হোক না কেন, আপনি সবচেয়ে কম Recovery Time Objective (RTO) এবং Recovery Point Objective (RPO) সহ Disaster Recovery strategy পেতে চান। আপনার কোন DR বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: D. Why it is right

DR strategy gulo RTO onujayi: Backup and Restore → Pilot Light → Warm Standby → Multi-Site (PDF p. 781).

Multi Site / Hot Site e full production scale on-premise ar AWS dui jaygay active chole, tai RTO minutes ba seconds; kintu very expensive (PDF p. 785). 'Regardless of cost' + lowest RTO/RPO = Multi-Site.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Backup and Restore er RPO ar RTO sobcheye beshi (sobcheye slow), lowest na.
  • B. Pilot Light e baki system chalu korte shomoy lage; RTO lowest na.
  • C. Warm Standby e scale up korte hoy, Multi-Site er cheye RTO beshi.

Keyword: lowest RTO and RPO, regardless of cost → Multi-Site

Instructor feedback (English)

D: If you're interested, read more about Disaster Recovery options in AWS here: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/whitepapers/latest/disaster-recovery-workloads-on-aws/disaster-recovery-options-in-the-cloud.html

28·3U28-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q3

Which of the following Disaster Recovery strategies has a potentially high Recovery Point Objective (RPO) and Recovery Time Objective (RTO)?

নিচের কোন Disaster Recovery strategy-র সম্ভাব্যভাবে high Recovery Point Objective (RPO) এবং Recovery Time Objective (RTO) আছে?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Backup and Restore slide er title-i 'High RPO' (PDF p. 782). Backup (snapshot, S3/Glacier) shudhu schedule e hoy, tai last backup er por er data harate pare (high RPO).

Disaster e sob kichu backup theke notun kore create korte hoy, tai RTO o beshi. Sobcheye shosta, kintu sobcheye slow.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Pilot Light e critical core (DB) running ar replicate hoy, tai RPO/RTO Backup and Restore er theke kom.
  • C. Warm Standby e full system minimum size e running, RTO/RPO onek kom.
  • D. Multi-Site e RTO minutes/seconds; sobcheye kom, high na.

Keyword: potentially high RPO and RTO → Backup and Restore

Instructor feedback (English)

A: If you're interested, read more about Disaster Recovery options in AWS here: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/whitepapers/latest/disaster-recovery-workloads-on-aws/disaster-recovery-options-in-the-cloud.html

28·4U28-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q4

You want to make a Disaster Recovery plan where you have a scaled-down version of your system up and running, and when a disaster happens, it scales up quickly. Which DR strategy should you choose?

আপনি এমন একটি Disaster Recovery plan তৈরি করতে চান, যেখানে আপনার system-এর একটি scaled-down version চালু থাকে, এবং disaster ঘটলে এটি দ্রুত scale up করে। আপনার কোন DR strategy বেছে নেওয়া উচিত?

Correct: C. Why it is right

Warm Standby: full system up and running, kintu minimum size e (jemon ASG minimum, RDS secondary running). Disaster hole production load e scale up kora hoy (PDF p. 784).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Backup and Restore e kono system running thake na, shudhu backup.
  • B. Pilot Light e shudhu critical core (DB) running, full system na; baki gulo pore chalu korte hoy.
  • D. Multi-Site e full production scale already running, scaled-down na.

Keyword: scaled-down version running, scales up on disaster → Warm Standby

Instructor feedback (English)

C: If you're interested, read more about Disaster Recovery options in AWS here: https://docs.aws.amazon.com/whitepapers/latest/disaster-recovery-workloads-on-aws/disaster-recovery-options-in-the-cloud.html

28·5U28-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q5

You have an on-premises Oracle database that you want to migrate to AWS, specifically to Amazon Aurora. How would you do the migration?

আপনার একটি on-premises Oracle database আছে, যা আপনি AWS-এ, বিশেষ করে Amazon Aurora-তে migrate করতে চান। আপনি কীভাবে migration করবেন?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Oracle → Aurora holo heterogeneous migration (engine alada). Tai dui step (PDF p. 791-792):

  • SCT (Schema Conversion Tool) database er schema ek engine theke onno engine e convert kore.
  • Tarpor DMS data migrate kore (full load + CDC).

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Role ulta: DMS schema convert kore na, data migrate kore; SCT schema convert kore, data na.

Keyword: Oracle → Aurora (different engine) → SCT schema, DMS data

28·6U28-Q6Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q6

You are running many resources in AWS such as EC2 instances, EBS volumes, DynamoDB tables... You want an easy way to manage backups across all these AWS services from a single place. Which AWS offering makes this process easy?

আপনি AWS-এ অনেক resource চালাচ্ছেন, যেমন EC2 instance, EBS volume, DynamoDB table... আপনি একটি single place থেকে এই সব AWS service জুড়ে backup manage করার একটি সহজ উপায় চান। কোন AWS offering এই process সহজ করে?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Backup fully managed service; ek jayga theke onek AWS service er backup centrally manage ar automate kore (PDF p. 797).

EC2/EBS, S3, RDS, Aurora, DynamoDB, EFS, FSx ityadi support kore; Backup Plan e frequency ar retention set kora jay, custom script lage na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 storage service; backup jaygay hote pare, kintu sob service er backup manage kore na.
  • B. Storage Gateway on-premise ke AWS storage er sathe connect kore; AWS service gulor backup centrally manage kore na.
  • D. EC2/EBS snapshot shudhu EBS volume er; DynamoDB ityadi cover kore na, central management o na.

Keyword: manage backups across EC2, EBS, DynamoDB from a single place → AWS Backup

Instructor feedback (English)

C: AWS Backup enables you to centralize and automate data protection across AWS services. It helps you support your regulatory compliance or business policies for data protection.

28·7U28-Q7Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q7

A company planning to migrate its existing websites, applications, servers, virtual machines, and data to AWS. They want to do a lift-and-shift migration with minimum downtime and reduced costs. Which AWS service can help in this scenario?

একটি company তার বিদ্যমান website, application, server, virtual machine এবং data AWS-এ migrate করার পরিকল্পনা করছে। তারা সর্বনিম্ন downtime এবং কম খরচে একটি lift-and-shift migration করতে চায়। এই scenario-তে কোন AWS service সাহায্য করতে পারে?

Correct: B. Why it is right

AWS Application Migration Service (MGN): lift-and-shift (rehost) solution; physical, virtual, cloud server ke AWS e natively run korar moto convert kore (PDF p. 802).

Continuous replication kore, cutover e minimal downtime, reduced costs. Question er words slide er sathe mile.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. DMS shudhu database migrate kore; puro server, VM, website lift-and-shift kore na.
  • C. AWS Backup backup manage kore; migration tool na.
  • D. SCT database schema convert kore; server migrate kore na.

Keyword: lift-and-shift servers/VMs, minimum downtime → Application Migration Service (MGN)

A company is using VMware on its on-premises data center to manage its infrastructure. There is a requirement to extend their data center and infrastructure to AWS but keep using the technology stack they are using which is VMware. Which AWS service can they use?

একটি company তার infrastructure manage করতে তার on-premises data center-এ VMware ব্যবহার করছে। তাদের data center এবং infrastructure AWS-এ সম্প্রসারণ করার requirement আছে, কিন্তু তারা যে technology stack ব্যবহার করছে, অর্থাৎ VMware, সেটিই ব্যবহার চালিয়ে যেতে চায়। তারা কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করতে পারে?

⚠ Outdated: 30 April 2024 theke AWS ar VMware Cloud on AWS resell kore na, Broadcom theke kinte hoy; AWS er nijer Amazon Elastic VMware Service (EVS) Aug 2025 e GA hoyeche. Course/exam er answer ekhono VMware Cloud on AWS. source

Correct: A. Why it is right

On-premise e VMware Cloud use kore, data center capacity AWS e extend korte chay kintu VMware software rakhte chay: eta-i VMware Cloud on AWS (PDF p. 803).

Ajker update: 30 April 2024 theke AWS ar VMware Cloud on AWS resell kore na (Broadcom theke kinte hoy), ar AWS er nijer Amazon Elastic VMware Service (EVS) Aug 2025 e GA hoyeche. Exam answer ekhono VMware Cloud on AWS.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. DataSync file/data copy kore (NFS, SMB → S3, EFS); VMware stack AWS e extend kore na.
  • C. MGN server ke AWS EC2 te rehost kore; VMware stack rakhe na, convert kore.
  • D. Application Discovery Service on-premise server er info collect kore migration plan er jonno; kono workload chalay na.

Keyword: extend data center to AWS, keep using VMware → VMware Cloud on AWS

28·9U28-Q9Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q9

A company is using RDS for MySQL as their main database but, lately they have been facing issues in managing the database, performance issues, and the scalability. And they have decided to use Aurora for MySQL instead for better performance, less complexity and less administrative tasks required. What is the best way and most cost-effective way to migrate from RDS for MySQL to Aurora for MySQL?

একটি company তাদের main database হিসেবে RDS for MySQL ব্যবহার করছে, কিন্তু ইদানীং তারা database manage করা, performance সমস্যা এবং scalability নিয়ে সমস্যার সম্মুখীন হচ্ছে। এবং তারা আরও ভালো performance, কম জটিলতা এবং কম administrative task-এর জন্য এর বদলে Aurora for MySQL ব্যবহার করার সিদ্ধান্ত নিয়েছে। RDS for MySQL থেকে Aurora for MySQL-এ migrate করার সবচেয়ে ভালো এবং সবচেয়ে cost-effective উপায় কী?

Correct: D. Why it is right

RDS MySQL → Aurora MySQL er Option 1: RDS MySQL er DB snapshot niye seta Aurora MySQL DB hisebe restore koro (PDF p. 794).

Option 2 (Aurora Read Replica, lag 0 hole promote) o ache, kintu slide e bola 'can take time and cost $'. Tai simple ar cost-effective holo snapshot restore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. Bhul: migration supported; support ticket lagbe na.
  • B. Dump (mysqldump) kaaj kore, kintu slow ar beshi kaaj; course e eta external MySQL er jonno, RDS theke snapshot restore sohoj.
  • C. Bhul: direct migration possible (snapshot restore ba Aurora read replica); custom app lagbe na.

Keyword: RDS MySQL → Aurora MySQL, cost-effective → snapshot restore to Aurora

28·10U28-Q10Udemy quiz · sec 28 · Q10

Which AWS service can you use to automate the backup across different AWS services such as RDS, DynamoDB, Aurora, and EFS file systems, and EBS volumes?

RDS, DynamoDB, Aurora, এবং EFS file system, এবং EBS volume-এর মতো বিভিন্ন AWS service জুড়ে backup automate করতে আপনি কোন AWS service ব্যবহার করতে পারেন?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Backup RDS (all engines), Aurora, DynamoDB, EFS, EBS sob service er backup ek jayga theke automate kore (PDF p. 797). Backup Plan e frequency, window, retention set koro (PDF p. 798).

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 Lifecycle Policy shudhu S3 object er storage class change/expire kore; RDS, DynamoDB backup kore na.
  • B. DataSync data copy/sync kore (on-premise ↔ AWS storage); service backup automate kore na.
  • D. Glacier archive storage class; nije onno service er backup automate kore na.

Keyword: automate backup across RDS, DynamoDB, Aurora, EFS, EBS → AWS Backup

29·1U29-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 29 · Q1

You are working on a Serverless application where you want to process objects uploaded to an S3 bucket. You have configured S3 Events on your S3 bucket to invoke a Lambda function every time an object has been uploaded. You want to ensure that events that can't be processed are sent to a Dead Letter Queue (DLQ) for further processing. Which AWS service should you use to set up the DLQ?

Correct: C. Why it is right

S3 Event Notification Lambda ke asynchronous bhabe invoke kore. Async invocation e Lambda nijei internally retry kore (3 bar), tarpor fail hole message discard hoy, na hole DLQ e jay (PDF p. 806).

Course er rule: SQS + Lambda hole DLQ thake SQS side e; kintu async (SNS/S3) + Lambda hole DLQ set korte hoy Lambda function side e. Tai DLQ (SQS queue ba SNS topic) Lambda function e configure korbe.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. S3 Events shudhu event pathay; S3 er kono DLQ setting nai. Fail handling Lambda er async invocation side e hoy.
  • B. SNS topic DLQ er target hote pare, kintu DLQ ta set korte hoy Lambda function e. Tachara ekhane SNS flow e nai, S3 direct Lambda ke call kore.

Keyword: S3 Events invoke Lambda (asynchronous) + DLQ → DLQ set koro Lambda function e

Instructor feedback (English)

C: The Lambda function's invocation is "asynchronous", so the DLQ has to be set on the Lambda function side.

29·2U29-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 29 · Q2

As a Solutions Architect, you have created an architecture for a company that includes the following AWS services: CloudFront, Web Application Firewall (AWS WAF), AWS Shield, Application Load Balancer, and EC2 instances managed by an Auto Scaling Group. Sometimes the company receives malicious requests and wants to block these IP addresses. According to your architecture, Where should you do it?

Correct: B. Why it is right

Architecture e shobar shamne CloudFront. Client er connection CloudFront e terminate hoy, tai ALB porjonto ja ashe ta CloudFront er public IP, client er IP na (PDF p. 817).

Tai client IP block korte hobe AWS WAF diye (CloudFront e attach kora), WAF er IP address filtering rule (IP set) diye. Course slide e NACL ke ei setup e "NOT helpful" bola ache.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CloudFront nije shudhu Geo Restriction (country level) dite pare, specific IP block er jonno WAF lagbe.
  • C. Shield holo DDoS protection; specific IP block korar rule Shield e likhte paro na.
  • D. ALB Security Group e shudhu CloudFront er IP dekha jay, client IP na. Tachara SG te deny rule nai, shudhu allow.
  • E. EC2 private subnet e, traffic ashe ALB theke; client IP EC2 porjonto pouchay na. SG te deny rule o nai.
  • F. NACL e deny rule thakleo, CloudFront thakay NACL client er IP dekhe na, CloudFront er IP dekhe. Tai ekhane NACL kaj kore na.

Keyword: CloudFront shamne + block IP addresses → AWS WAF (IP filtering)

29·3U29-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 29 · Q3

You have a set of Linux EC2 instances deployed in a Cluster Placement Group in order to perform High-Performance Computing (HPC). You would like to maximize network performance between your EC2 instances. What should you use?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Elastic Fabric Adapter (EFA) holo HPC er jonno improved ENA, shudhu Linux e kaj kore (PDF p. 821).

  • Inter-node communication ar tightly coupled workload er jonno best.
  • MPI standard use kore, Linux OS bypass kore low latency, reliable transport dey.

Cluster placement group + Linux + HPC + maximum network performance → EFA.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. ENI holo shudhu ekta virtual network card; network performance baray na.
  • C. ENA enhanced networking (up to 100 Gbps) dey, kintu EFA hocche ENA er improved version, OS bypass soho; HPC inter-node er jonno EFA beshi fast.
  • D. FSx for Lustre holo HPC er file system (storage), networking component na.

Keyword: Linux + HPC + maximize network performance between instances → EFA

30·1U30-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 30 · Q1

As part of your Disaster Recovery strategy, you would like to make sure your entire infrastructure is code (IaC) so that you can easily re-deploy it in any AWS region. Which AWS service do you recommend?

Correct: D. Why it is right

CloudFormation holo AWS er Infrastructure as Code (IaC) service: declarative template e bolo ki ki resource lagbe, CloudFormation sheta thik order e create kore (PDF p. 828-829).

Template ache mane jekono region e same infrastructure abar destroy ar re-create kora jay on the fly (PDF p. 830), DR er jonno perfect.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. CodePipeline holo CI/CD release pipeline (build, test, deploy automate kore); infrastructure ke code hishebe define kore na.
  • B. Beanstalk web application deploy korar developer-centric managed platform; puro infrastructure IaC hishebe model kore na.
  • C. CodeDeploy shudhu application code EC2/Lambda/on-prem e deploy kore; infrastructure create kore na.

Keyword: entire infrastructure is code (IaC) + re-deploy in any region → CloudFormation

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS CodePipeline is a fully managed continuous delivery (CD) service that helps you automate your release pipeline for fast and reliable application and infrastructure updates. It automates the build, test, and deploy phases of your release process every time there is a code change.

B: AWS Elastic Beanstalk is an easy-to-use service for deploying and scaling web applications and services developed with Java, .NET, PHP, Node.js, Python, Ruby, Go, and Docker. It automatically handles the deployment, from capacity provisioning, load balancing, auto-scaling to application health monitoring.

C: AWS CodeDeploy is a fully managed deployment service that automates software deployments to a variety of computing services such as EC2, Fargate, Lambda, and your on-premises servers.

D: AWS CloudFormation is the de-facto service in AWS for infrastructure as code (IaC). It enables you to create and provision AWS infrastructure deployments predictably and repeatedly.

30·2U30-Q2Udemy quiz · sec 30 · Q2⚠ outdated

Which AWS service allows you to send marketing SMS and push notifications to a large number of customers with personalized messages?

⚠ Outdated: Amazon Pinpoint 30 Oct 2026 e end of support (20 May 2025 theke new customer bondho). SMS/push/OTP API gulo ekhon AWS End User Messaging e, email er jonno Amazon SES. Course/exam er answer ekhono Pinpoint. source

Correct: B. Why it is right

Amazon Pinpoint holo scalable 2-way marketing communication service: email, SMS, push, voice, in-app (PDF p. 834).

  • Customer segment kore personalized message pathano jay.
  • Message template, delivery schedule, targeted segment, full campaign banano jay; SNS/SES e eshob nije manage korte hoy.

Note (today): Pinpoint 30 Oct 2026 e end of support; SMS/push er API ekhon AWS End User Messaging e, email SES e. Kintu course/exam answer ekhono Pinpoint.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. SNS notification pathay, kintu personalized marketing campaign, segment, template er feature nai; prottek message er audience/content nije manage korte hoy.
  • C. SES shudhu email service; SMS ba push notification pathay na.
  • D. Lambda compute service; nijei SMS/push campaign pathanor service na.

Keyword: marketing SMS + push + personalized messages → Amazon Pinpoint

30·3U30-Q3Udemy quiz · sec 30 · Q3

What is the most secure way to connect to an EC2 instance without exposing the SSH port 22?

Correct: A. Why it is right

SSM Session Manager EC2 ar on-prem server e secure shell dey SSM Agent ar IAM permission diye (PDF p. 835).

  • SSH access, bastion host, SSH key kichui lage na.
  • Port 22 open korte hoy na → better security.
  • Session log S3 ba CloudWatch Logs e pathano jay.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. Site-to-Site VPN on-prem network ke VPC er shathe jore; tarpor o instance e dhukte SSH (port 22) lagbe.
  • C. Client VPN user ke VPC te dhokay, kintu instance e login korte abar SSH port 22 open rakhte hobe.
  • D. Bastion host nijei SSH (port 22) diye kaj kore, ar ekta extra instance manage korte hoy; less secure.

Keyword: most secure + without exposing SSH port 22 → SSM Session Manager

30·4U30-Q4Udemy quiz · sec 30 · Q4

Which AWS service allows you to run and schedule hundreds of thousands of computing jobs on AWS such as big data and complex analytics jobs?

Correct: C. Why it is right

AWS Batch fully managed batch processing: 100,000s computing batch job efficiently chalay (PDF p. 848).

  • Batch job = start ar end ache emon job.
  • Batch nije EC2 ba Spot instance launch kore, right amount of compute/memory dey.
  • Tumi shudhu job submit ba schedule koro, baki Batch kore.

Why the other options are wrong

  • A. "AWS Simple Batch Service" namer kono AWS service nai; eta distractor.
  • B. EC2 shudhu virtual server; job scheduling, queue, scaling nije manage korte hobe, onek ops overhead.
  • D. Lambda er time limit (15 min), limited runtime ar disk ache; boro long-running batch/analytics job er jonno Batch better (PDF p. 850).

Keyword: run and schedule hundreds of thousands of computing jobs → AWS Batch

30·5U30-Q5Udemy quiz · sec 30 · Q5

The company you are working on is using Salesforce and Slack internally. For archival and some analytics requirements, you have been tasked to transfer the data in both Salesforce and Slack to AWS in an S3 bucket. Which AWS service is best suited for this scenario?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Amazon AppFlow fully managed integration service, SaaS application ar AWS er moddhe data securely transfer kore (PDF p. 851).

  • Source: Salesforce, SAP, Zendesk, Slack, ServiceNow.
  • Destination: S3, Redshift, Snowflake etc.
  • Schedule, event ba on-demand e chole; nije integration code likhte hoy na.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. DataSync on-prem/other cloud storage (NFS, SMB, HDFS, S3 API) theke AWS storage e data move kore; Salesforce/Slack er moto SaaS app theke na.
  • C. DMS database migrate kore (source DB → target DB); SaaS app er data S3 e ane na.
  • D. Application Migration Service (MGN) on-prem server lift-and-shift kore AWS e ane; SaaS data transfer kore na.

Keyword: Salesforce + Slack data → S3 → Amazon AppFlow

31·1U31-Q1Udemy quiz · sec 31 · Q1

Which AWS Service analyzes your AWS account and gives recommendations for cost optimization, performance, security, fault tolerance, and service limits?

Correct: A. Why it is right

Trusted Advisor kichu install na korei account er high level assessment kore, ar recommendation dey 6 category te: Cost optimization, Performance, Security, Fault tolerance, Service limits, Operational Excellence (PDF p. 860).

Question er list (cost, performance, security, fault tolerance, service limits) hubohu Trusted Advisor er category.

Why the other options are wrong

  • B. CloudTrail API call/account activity log kore (audit); recommendation dey na.
  • C. IAM user, group, role, permission manage kore; account analyze kore best practice recommendation dey na.
  • D. CloudFormation infrastructure as code diye resource create kore; account analyze kore na.

Keyword: analyzes your AWS account + cost, performance, security, fault tolerance, service limits → Trusted Advisor

Instructor feedback (English)

A: AWS Trusted Advisor provides recommendations that help you follow AWS best practices. It evaluates your account by using checks. These checks identify ways to optimize your AWS infrastructure, improve security and performance, reduce costs, and monitor service quotas.

B: AWS CloudTrail allows you to log, continuously monitor, and retain account activity related to actions across your AWS infrastructure. It provides the event history of your AWS account activity, audit API calls made through the AWS Management Console, AWS SDKs, AWS CLI. You can use CloudTrail to detect unusual activity in your AWS accounts.

C: AWS IAM enables you to manage access to AWS services and resources securely. You can create and manage IAM users, groups, and roles, and use permissions to allow and deny their access to AWS resources.

D: AWS CloudFormation is the de-facto service in AWS for infrastructure as code (IaC). It enables you to create and provision AWS infrastructure deployments predictably and repeatedly.
